### [1] [Ultrafast and high resolution spatial light modulation for cold atoms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18071)
*Alexander Dennisovich Deters,Yanfei Li,Alexander Douglas,Markus Greiner,Aaron W. Young*

Main category: physics.atom-ph

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种用于超冷原子阵列的光学控制系统，在空间分辨率、帧率和强度分辨率等关键指标上均达到或超越现有方案，并验证了其在全可编程Hubbard模型和二维光学镊子快速排列操作中的应用能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超冷原子阵列是量子计算与模拟的领先平台，但对量子态的所有局部控制都依赖于精密成形的光场，因此管理多体量子态本质上成为光学设计问题。此前光学系统在规模、分辨率、速度和灵活性等方面难以同时兼顾，亟需一种在多个维度上同步提升的解决方案。

Method: 研究人员构建了一套光学系统，在保持与先前方案相当规模的同时，在强度分辨率（$10^{-3}$）、帧率（大于84 MFPS）和空间分辨率（$83 \times 52$束腰）等所有其他维度上取得突破。基于这一架构，他们开发并数值验证了全可编程Hubbard模型方案，支持对局部化学势、隧穿振幅、在位相互作用及人工磁通模式的时间依赖控制；同一架构还支持二维光学镊子的快速任意排列。

Result: 该系统实现了$10^{-3}$的任意图案强度分辨率、每秒超过8400万帧的刷新率，以及$83 \times 52$束腰空间分辨率，其中借助单个40 GHz电光调制器可访问$11 \times 52$束腰。数值验证表明该架构可实现对Hubbard模型中全部关键参数和人工磁通的动态调控，并实现二维镊子的快速任意置换，从而将光学约束与高码率纠错码设计解耦。

Conclusion: 该光学系统在规模不变的前提下全面提升了其他性能指标，开辟了新一类实验的可能性，特别是为全可编程Hubbard模型模拟和快速任意排列操作提供了可行性验证，有望加速量子计算与量子模拟领域的研究进展。

Abstract: Programmable arrays of ultracold atoms are a leading platform for quantum computation and simulation, enabling state-of-the-art implementations of quantum error correction, and analog simulations of Hubbard models that address open problems in condensed matter physics. In these systems, all local control is mediated through precisely shaped optical fields, and so the challenge of managing many-body quantum states becomes an exercise in optical design. In particular, one wishes for fast, flexible control with low disorder and heating, and access to large arrays with many atoms. An ideal optical system therefore must generate arbitrary patterns with high spatial resolution and low disorder, and alter these patterns on a timescale that is faster than the relevant atomic dynamics. Here, we present an optical system that is comparable to previous approaches in scale, while advancing all other axes. We demonstrate arbitrary pattern generation with $10^{-3}$ intensity resolution, a frame rate of $>84$ MFPS (megaframes per second), and a spatial resolution of $83 \times 52$ beam waists (with $11 \times 52$ waists accessible via a single $40$ GHz electro-optic modulator). These capabilities unlock a new class of experiments. We develop and numerically validate a scheme for fully programmable Hubbard models, with time-dependent control over local chemical potentials, tunneling amplitudes, on-site interactions, and patterns of artificial magnetic flux. The same architecture performs fast, arbitrary permutations of tweezers in 2D, decoupling optical constraints from the design of high-rate error-correcting codes.

</details>

### [2] [Wavelength-Resolved Control of Photovoltaic Screening and Defect-Mediated Doping in Photo-Ferroelectric/Graphene Devices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17647)
*Krishna Prasad Maity,Mohd Uvais,Jean-Francois Dayen,Bernard Doudin,Roman Gumeniuk,Bohdan Kundys*

Main category: cond-mat.other

TL;DR: 本研究调查了铁电/石墨烯异质结器件在365 nm和530 nm光照下的光电响应，通过区分开关与非开关电荷动力学通道，揭示了光伏电荷屏蔽与缺陷辅助激发之间的竞争机制，并建立了通过光子能量与强度选择来调控剩余极化与沟道掺杂的实验指南。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 铁电体能够实现对二维覆盖层的大规模电荷掺杂，但开关与非开关电荷动力学的共存使电光分析变得复杂，因此需要系统解耦不同物理机制对器件电学行为的影响。

Method: 在暗态与光照条件下解析开关与非开关通道，利用石墨烯作为高增益传感层放大极化动力学信号，对比365 nm与530 nm两种波长下器件电阻与铁电滞回特性的变化。

Result: 高于带隙的365 nm光照产生自由载流子，引发漏电型铁电滞回，并将相反剩余极化态之间的石墨烯电阻比从290%抑制至15%；而530 nm光照主要通过缺陷态激发引起铁电体中的电荷重分布，对石墨烯电阻变化的抑制显著较弱。

Conclusion: 可通过选择光子能量与强度，既保留剩余极化同时调控沟道掺杂，或通过光学编程有意重构极化状态，为铁电器件的光电调控提供了实用设计准则。

Abstract: Ferroelectrics enable large charge doping of two-dimensional overlayers, but the coexistence of switching and nonswitching charge dynamics complicate electro-optical analysis. Here, we investigate the optoelectronic response of a ferroelectric/graphene device under 365 and 530 nm illumination, disentangling effects on ferroelectric dipole alignment from extrinsic current pathways. Graphene acts as a high-gain sensor, amplifying subtle polarization dynamics into a pronounced resistance difference. By resolving switching and nonswitching channels in dark and illuminated conditions, we reveal a competition between photovoltaic charge screening and defect-assisted excitation that governs device electrostatics. Above-band gap illumination generates free carriers that induce leaky ferroelectric hysteresis and suppress the graphene resistance ratio between opposite remanent polarization states from 290% to 15% due to dynamic photovoltaic charge screening. In contrast, 530 nm illumination primarily induces charge redistribution in ferroelectrics via defect-state excitation, leading to a significantly weaker suppression of the resistance variation of graphene. These results establish practical guidelines for selecting photon energy and intensity to either preserve remanent polarization while tuning channel doping or deliberately reconfigure polarization through optical programming.

</details>

### [3] [Unitary-orbit classification and a refinement theorem for context-independent projective probabilities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16936)
*Michael P. Rubin*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文在有限维复希尔伯特空间中对投影测量（projective measurement）进行了结构化分类，证明两个投影测量的G_P轨道相同当且仅当它们的互补结果秩多重集（multiset）一致，并揭示了上下文独立性（context independence）与细化一致性（refinement consistency）之间的等价关系，最终通过Gleason定理将最大上下文不变性和全上下文细化条件转化为Born形式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子力学中的上下文独立性是Born规则的核心特征之一，但对其与测量结构之间的精确关系缺乏系统性理解。作者旨在通过对称性分组（grouping by symmetry）对包含给定投影结果的测量空间进行轨道分类，从而为上下文独立性提供结构化的数学刻画。

Method: 设G_P为固定投影P上单位作用、P正交补空间上任意的酉群（unitary group），作者对G_P作用下的测量轨道进行分类：两测量同轨当且仅当其互补结果秩多重集相同。轨道作为紧致齐次空间（compact homogeneous space）具有显式实维数；通过引入均匀缺陷（uniform defect）、二进制细化（binary coarsening）和秩剖面细化图（rank-profile refinement graph），建立了细化一致性与上下文独立性的等价性，并证明了一个稳定性定理。

Result: 主要结果包括：轨道完全由互补结果的秩多重集决定；最大秩一测量上仅有一个轨道，从而G_P不变性等价于上下文独立性；任意投影测量的每个上下文都细化到唯一二进制上下文{P, I−P}，因此细化一致性等价于上下文独立性；轨道的秩剖面细化图直径为d−rankP−1；在维数至少三时，Gleason定理将最大上下文不变性和全上下文细化条件转化为Born形式Tr(ρP)。

Conclusion: 这些结果是结构性的刻画而非上下文独立性的独立物理推导，从数学上揭示了投影测量的对称性结构如何承载Born规则的上下文独立性内涵。

Abstract: Let $\mathcal H$ be a finite-dimensional complex Hilbert space, and let $w(P,\mathsf M)$ be a normalized probability weight assigned to an outcome projection $P$ as it occurs in a projective measurement $\mathsf M$. For fixed $P$, let $G_P\cong\mathcal U(P^\perp)$ be the group of unitaries acting identically on $\operatorname{ran}P$ and arbitrarily on $P^\perp$. We classify the $G_P$-orbits of projective measurements containing $P$: two measurements lie in the same orbit exactly when the multisets of ranks of their complementary outcomes agree. The orbit with profile $λ=(r_1,\ldots,r_k)$ is a compact homogeneous space of real dimension $(d-\operatorname{rank}P)^2-\sum_j r_j^2$. On maximal rank-one measurements there is one orbit, so context independence is equivalent to $G_P$-invariance, with equality of the corresponding uniform defects; invariance under two-level complementary unitaries already suffices. For arbitrary projective measurements, every context containing an outcome $P\neq I$ coarsens to the unique binary context $\{P,I-P\}$. Consequently, refinement consistency alone is equivalent to context independence. The finite orbit space carries a natural rank-profile refinement graph, whose diameter is $d-\operatorname{rank}P-1$ when $P\neq I$. We prove a stability theorem that compares the binary-coarsening path with a shortest path in this graph followed by one complementary unitary. In dimension at least three, Gleason's theorem converts the maximal-context invariance condition and the all-context refinement condition, on their respective domains, into the Born form $\operatorname{Tr}(ρP)$. The results are structural characterizations, not independent physical derivations of context independence.

</details>


### [4] [Non-CSS Quantum Code Embedding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16995)
*Andrew C. Yuan,Nouédyn Baspin*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文将[Phys. Rev. A 113, 022438 (2026)]中的统一框架推广到任意稳定子码（stabilizer codes），包括非CSS码（non-CSS codes），从而将逻辑测量、量子权重缩减（quantum weight reduction）和欧几里得嵌入（Euclidean embedding）等多项构造从CSS码扩展到更一般的场景，并以具体示例进行了详细推导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有统一框架仅适用于CSS码（纯X、Z型），而非CSS码（如Y型）的逻辑测量依赖局部Clifford变换等临时性方法，量子权重缩减和最优欧几里得嵌入也仅对CSS输入码成立，缺乏普适理论工具。

Method: 将此前基于（高度为1的）锥（cone）的CSS逻辑测量框架进行一般化推广，使其能够容纳任意稳定子码，并将该推广应用到逻辑测量、量子权重缩减和欧几里得嵌入等具体领域。

Result: 推广后的框架使得多项构造——包括qLDPC手术（qLDPC surgery）、Layer Codes和量子权重缩减——能够以相对直接的方式推广到非CSS码，论文对上述示例给出了详细推导。

Conclusion: 该推广填补了非CSS码在逻辑测量、量子权重缩减和欧几里得嵌入等方面的理论空白，为更广泛的稳定子码构造提供了统一框架。

Abstract: We generalize the unified framework in [Phys. Rev. A 113, 022438 (2026)] to accommodate arbitrary stabilizer codes (referred as non-CSS for emphasis). The generalization has immediate consequences in areas including logical measurement, quantum weight reduction and Euclidean embedding. For example, currently CSS (pure $X,Z$-type) logical measurements are well-understood based on the (height-1) cone, while non-CSS (e.g., $Y$-type) logical measurements are ad hoc (e.g., rely on local Clifford transform). Similarly, quantum weight reduction and optimal Euclidean embeddings only exist for CSS input codes. Here, we show that our generalized framework addresses this issue, and thus many constructions, including qLDPC surgery, Layer Codes and quantum weight reduction generalize to non-CSS codes in a relatively straightforward fashion. The previously mentioned examples are derived in detail for clarity.

</details>


### [5] [Lie-Algebraic Classical Simulation of Bosonic Systems Beyond Gaussian Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17094)
*Adelina Bärligea,Timothy Heightman,Jakob S. Kottmann,Antonio Acín*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文扩展了李代数模拟（Lie-algebraic simulation）框架，使其适用于玻色子系统（bosonic systems），证明了当算子模（operator modules）具有多项式维度时，期望值、固定阶关联函数（含多时间关联函数和乱时序关联函数）及梯度均可高效经典计算，统一了高斯量子光学并扩展至非高斯基态与相互作用非高斯动力学，为玻色量子动力学的经典多项式时间模拟提供了统一形式体系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典可模拟性最终由量子系统的动力学及其评估观测量共同决定，李代数模拟通过低维不变算子空间传播观测量实现精确多项式时间经典模拟。然而其传统表述基于多项式维动态李代数（dynamical Lie algebra），无法直接适用于代数既非紧致亦非半单的玻色系统，亟需克服这一局限。

Method: 作者将李代数模拟方案推广至玻色系统，证明当算子模块具有多项式维度时，期望值、固定阶关联函数（包括多时间关联函数和乱时序关联函数）及梯度均可高效计算；推导了超出精确扇区约束的受控微扰层级，用于挤压（squeezing）效应，并在至多400个模式的相互作用链上评估算子扩展以验证结果。

Result: 该框架恢复了高斯量子光学（Gaussian quantum optics）结果并将其扩展至非高斯基态，识别出包括有界光子Kerr和配对跳跃哈密顿量以及幂零多项式相位动力学在内的相互作用非高斯动力学的精确多项式正则区。与有限维自旋和费米子系统不同，有限维玻色生成元代数本身无法保证有限观测量动力学；微扰层级的预测误差阶数经数值验证，拓扑双子带（topological doublon band）与磁通反转边缘运动被确认关联。

Conclusion: 该研究为玻色量子动力学的多项式时间经典模拟提供了分类、发现和系统近似的统一形式体系，拓展了经典可模拟性的边界。

Abstract: Classical simulability is ultimately determined by both the dynamics of a quantum system and the observables being evaluated. Lie-algebraic simulation exploits the latter to make exact polynomial-time classical simulations by propagating observables through low-dimensional invariant operator spaces. However, its conventional formulation in terms of polynomial-dimensional dynamical Lie algebras does not directly accommodate bosonic systems as their algebras are neither compact nor semisimple. In this contribution, we overcome this limitation, making bosonic systems accessible to the Lie-algebraic programme of exact polynomial-time classical simulation. We prove that expectation values, fixed-order correlation functions, including multi-time correlators and out-of-time-ordered correlators, and gradients are efficiently computable whenever their operator modules have polynomial dimension. This recovers Gaussian quantum optics and extends it to non-Gaussian input states, while identifying exact polynomial regimes of interacting non-Gaussian dynamics including bounded-photon Kerr and pair-hopping Hamiltonians and nilpotent polynomial phase dynamics. We show that unlike in the finite-dimensional spin and fermionic setting treated previously, a finite-dimensional bosonic generator algebra alone does not guarantee finite observable dynamics. We further derive a controlled perturbative hierarchy for squeezing beyond exact sector confinement and confirm the predicted error orders numerically. We also evaluate operator spreading on interacting chains of up to $400$ modes and connect a topological doublon band with flux-reversed edge motion. These results provide a unified formalism for classifying, discovering, and systematically approximating tractable bosonic quantum dynamics with classical polynomial-time simulation.

</details>


### [6] [Multi-purpose quantum laboratories from superconducting circuits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17020)
*Arpit Arora,Emily M. Been,William Munizzi,Joel Wang,Taylor L. Patti,Aaron Chou,Prineha Narang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文是一篇综述（review），系统阐述了超导电路（superconducting circuits）作为通用量子实验室的作用，统合了其在凝聚态物理、高能物理和量子信息科学中的能力，并追溯了其技术演进历程，展示了这一体系如何成为解析宏观量子态复杂性的利器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超导电路是现代量子技术的基石，但其作为通用量子实验室的跨学科价值尚未被系统梳理；本文旨在综述并统一超导量子硬件在凝聚态、高能和量子信息领域的应用图景。

Method: 采用综述（review）方式，梳理并统一超导量子硬件在各学科领域的能力，并回溯其技术架构的演化历史，说明基础性开发如何汇聚为解析宏观量子态的完整工具集。

Result: 本文勾勒出关联物质-电路科学（correlated matter-circuit science）的新兴图景，论证了超导电路已从量子计算平台发展为高精度探索工具，并展示了其技术传统如何支撑宏观量子态研究。

Conclusion: 超导电路凭借其对宏观量子态的相干控制能力，成为跨越凝聚态、高能与量子信息科学的通用量子实验室，其基础性技术遗产为未来相关物质研究提供了重要支撑。

Abstract: Superconducting circuits (SCs) are the cornerstone of modern quantum technology, enabling scalable computing through coherent control of macroscopic quantum states. Through a legacy that predates modern quantum computing, SCs have emerged as high-precision instruments for discovery. In this review, we highlight the role of SCs as general-purpose quantum laboratories, outlining the emerging landscape of correlated matter-circuit science. We review and unify the capabilities of superconducting quantum hardware across condensed matter, high energy and quantum information sciences. We trace the technical evolution of these architectures, illustrating how their foundational development has culminated in a toolkit for resolving the complexities of macroscopic quantum states.

</details>


### [7] [Universal quantum theory from dynamical consistency](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17688)
*Chiara Marletto,Vlatko Vedral*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文通过动力学一致性论证（dynamical consistency argument），在特定哈密顿量（Hamiltonian）框架下论证量子理论的普适性，分析了简谐量子振子间的两类耦合，指出半经典模型（semiclassical models）的不足，并证明在联合幺正动力学（joint unitary dynamics）下保持正则代数（canonical algebra）会排除特定的混合经典-量子模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者试图从动力学一致性的角度论证量子理论的普适性，核心动机在于考察经典-量子混合模型的可行性，并检验线性引力场与量子电磁场及量子物质耦合时半经典描述的局限性。

Method: 论文在哈密顿量框架下分析了简谐量子振子之间的两类耦合，分别对应自由场与相互作用场的不同方面。通过要求联合幺正动力学保持正则代数不变，排除特定混合经典-量子模型，并将该推理应用于线性引力场分别与量子电磁场和量子物质的耦合情形。

Result: 研究建立了一个关键判据：正则代数在联合幺正动力学下的保持性排除了特定的混合经典-量子模型。此外，随机（stochastic）模型虽能保持海森堡不确定性原理（Heisenberg Uncertainty Principle），却与严格形式的守恒原理不一致，即便它们满足概率意义（on average）上的守恒。

Conclusion: 作者将结果与DeWitt对量子测量的分析进行了比较，指出若测量仪器为经典系统，则至少需要引入随机性才能保持海森堡不确定性原理；但随机模型无法同时满足严格守恒律。这一论证支持量子理论的普适性，并揭示了半经典与混合模型的根本局限。

Abstract: We argue for the universality of quantum theory using a dynamical consistency argument, within a specific Hamiltonian setting. We analyse two different types of coupling between simple quantum harmonic oscillators. We apply our reasoning to the gravitational field in the linear regime, coupled to the quantised electromagnetic field and, separately, to quantised matter. We conclude with a comparison to DeWitt's analysis of quantum measurement, in which the apparatus, if classical, must be at least stochastic to preserve the Heisenberg Uncertainty Principle. Under these conditions, the existing first-order error bound for uncompressed tree-tensor-network BUG also applies to the alternating-endpoint MPS schedule. We verify the uncompressed implementation against an independent six-site calculation. We then compare BUG with two-site TDVP for 16-site transverse-field Ising and Haldane-Shastry dynamics. At matched timestep and truncation settings, BUG performs fewer local exponential actions and has lower runtime. These settings do not produce equal accuracy. The runtime versus accuracy curves cross for the Ising model and are close for the Haldane-Shastry model. The comparison therefore identifies model-dependent trade-offs rather than a general advantage for either method.

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 变分量子传感（variational quantum sensing）的性能高度依赖于探测制备与测量所用的电路架构，而现有方法通常只在预定义拟设（ansätze）内优化连续参数，限制了设计空间的探索及其对传感任务和硬件约束的适应性。

Conclusion: 该工作确立了 AutoQSense 作为一种资源感知（resource-aware）的自适应、硬件兼容量子传感方法的价值。

Abstract: Variational quantum sensing offers a promising route to high-precision parameter estimation, but its performance depends strongly on the circuit architectures used for probe preparation and measurement. Existing approaches typically optimize continuous parameters within predefined ansätze, restricting the accessible design space and limiting adaptation to sensing tasks and hardware constraints. Here, we introduce \textsc{AutoQSense}, a reinforcement-learning framework that searches circuit architectures using Fisher-information-based objectives. For few-qubit systems, a single agent sequentially constructs preparation and measurement circuits. For larger systems, a distributed formulation assigns local circuit design to subsystem agents and inter-block entanglement to a budgeted agent. Numerical results show that the learned architectures recover known benchmark strategies, adapt to dephasing noise, and outperform fixed hardware-efficient ansätze while using fewer entangling gates. These results establish \textsc{AutoQSense} as a resource-aware approach to adaptive and hardware-compatible quantum sensing.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解磁振子与可调玻色浴之间的相互作用如何同时影响输运、量子纠缠和配对，是破解多体量子系统动力学的重要问题。

Method: 采用自旋-1/2 XX链与可调玻色-哈伯德浴耦合的模型，结合微扰理论（perturbation theory）和张量网络基于实时模拟（tensor network-based real-time simulations）进行分析。

Result: 单磁振子形成可移动极化子（mobile polaron），其延迟玻色云使其速度低于静态朗-富索（Lang-Firsov）预测并产生磁振子-玻色子纠缠；双磁振子云重叠时产生有限范围吸引，形成紧凑束缚态及瞬态散射后信号；在位玻色相互作用抑制局域数涨落，减少纠缠并削弱束缚态。

Abstract: We study one- and two-magnon dynamics in a spin-$1/2$ XX chain coupled to a tunable Bose-Hubbard bath. The XX Hamiltonian transports the magnons, while the bath dresses their motion and mediates an effective attraction. A single magnon forms a mobile polaron whose retarded bosonic cloud reduces its velocity below the static Lang-Firsov prediction and generates magnon-boson entanglement. In the weak-dressing, off-resonant mobile regime, the residual velocity deficit and entanglement are governed by the excitation weight of the cloud, as captured by perturbation theory and tensor network-based real-time simulations. Transport therefore provides a model-calibrated proxy for entanglement without joint-state reconstruction. When two clouds overlap, the bath mediates a finite-range attraction, yielding finite-size evidence for a compact two-magnon bound state and a transient post-scattering signature. On-site bath interactions suppress local boson number fluctuations and stiffen the bath response, reducing entanglement and weakening ground-state binding. Bath deformability thus emerges as a common control parameter for magnon transport, entanglement, and bath-mediated pairing.

*Matthew Rosenzweig*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文证明了Riesz Lieb–Oxford不等式中的最优固定粒子数常数Λ_N(s,d)可以达到，且这些常数随N严格递增。证明结合了大规范集中紧性（grand-canonical concentration–compactness）与严格单粒子延拓（strict one-particle extension），并通过归纳法从N=1开始闭合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 需要确定Riesz Lieb–Oxford不等式中的最优固定粒子数常数Λ_N(s,d)是否存在（即是否能够达到），以及这些常数随粒子数N的单调性，这是该不等式理论分析中的关键开放问题。

Method: 证明综合运用两类工具：一是大规范集中紧性方法处理最大化序列的极限行为，二是严格的单粒子延拓技术排除极限为规范或更低粒子数的情形。结合Di Marino和Lelotte的紧支撑定理（compact-support theorem）实现非乘积型单粒子延拓，最终通过数学归纳法闭合整个证明。

Result: 证明了对于所有1≤d、0<s<d及N≥1，最优固定粒子数常数Λ_N(s,d)可以达到，并且这些常数满足严格递增性质Λ_N<Λ_{N+1}。证明的关键在于排除最大化序列极限可能涉及的多种退化情形。

Conclusion: 本文通过归纳论证完成了对Λ_N(s,d)可达性和严格单调性的完整证明，为Riesz Lieb–Oxford不等式的最优常数理论提供了确定性结论。

Abstract: Let $\mathsf{d}\geq1$, $0<\mathsf{s}<\mathsf{d}$, and $N\geq1$. We prove that the optimal fixed-particle-number constant $Λ_N(\mathsf{s},\mathsf{d})$ in the Riesz Lieb--Oxford inequality is attained and that these constants are strictly increasing in $N$. The proof combines grand-canonical concentration--compactness with a strict one-particle extension. After recentering, a limiting plan arising from a maximizing sequence may assign positive probability to several particle numbers and hence be grand-canonical. A strict $N$-particle completion excludes this case, while the inequalities $Λ_N>Λ_k$ for $k<N$ exclude limits with a fixed lower particle number. Once attainment at particle number $N$ is known, the compact-support theorem of Di Marino and Lelotte arXiv:2607.11440, valid for all $0<\mathsf{s}<\mathsf{d}$, permits a non-product one-particle extension and yields $Λ_{N+1}(\mathsf{s},\mathsf{d})>Λ_N(\mathsf{s},\mathsf{d})$. Together, these implications close an induction beginning at $N=1$.

</details>

### [13] [PowderLine: a programmatic powder diffraction analysis application](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17009)
*Adam A. Corrao,Jennifer A. Perez,John D. Langhout,Megan M. Butal,Thomas A. Caswell,Daniel Olds*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文介绍了PowderLine，一个将粉末衍射全谱拟合（包括Rietveld精修和单峰分析）封装为单一声明式配方（recipe）的Python应用，通过版本化模式（schema）验证配方并执行精修软件，返回结构化机器可读结果，适合交互式、脚本化和自动化工作流。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Rietveld精修等全谱拟合方法虽然能从粉末衍射数据中提取详细的结构、化学和微观结构信息，但其可靠结果依赖于大量专业知识和软件特定操作，且规模化应用通常需要为每个应用编写自定义脚本。随着高通量实验和自主自驱动实验室（self-driving laboratories）的兴起，需要一种可程序化执行并返回结构化结果的粉末衍射分析方法。

Method: 作者开发了PowderLine，一个Python应用程序，其核心理念是将完整的精修过程封装为单一声明式描述（declarative recipe），该配方是机器可读且可写的，涵盖Rietveld精修或单峰分析的完整信息；配方通过版本化模式（versioned schema）进行验证，随后通过精修软件执行并返回结构化结果。配方可由用户、脚本或自动化代理以相同方式指定和运行，具有可组合性（composability）。

Result: PowderLine实现了对Rietveld精修和单峰分析的统一声明式描述与执行，能够从粉末衍射数据返回结构化、机器可读的结果，且因其可组合性，可自然适配交互式、脚本化及自主化（autonomous）多种工作流。

Conclusion: PowderLine通过声明式配方的方式降低了全谱拟合的复杂度门槛，使粉末衍射分析能够以标准化、可验证的方式集成到高通量和自驱动实验室的自动化流程中，同时保留了交互式和脚本化使用的灵活性。

Abstract: Whole-pattern fitting methods, such as Rietveld refinement, excel at extracting detailed structural, chemical, and microstructural information from powder diffraction data. Obtaining reliable results requires both considerable expertise and software-specific knowledge, and applying these methods at scale typically relies on custom scripts written for each application. High-throughput experiments and autonomous self-driving laboratories increasingly utilize powder diffraction analysis to proceed programmatically and to return structured, machine-readable results. Here, we introduce PowderLine, a Python application that encapsulates a complete refinement into a single declarative recipe, validates that recipe against a versioned schema, and executes it through refinement software to return structured results. The refinement recipe is an all-inclusive, machine-readable and -writable description of either Rietveld or single peak analysis that users, scripts, and automated agents can specify and run in the same way. As a result of PowderLine's composability, it naturally fits into interactive, scripted, and autonomous workflows alike.

</details>

The films are studied by X-ray and electron diffraction as well as soft X-ray angle-resolved photoemission spectroscopy. We demonstrate the ability to grow high-quality α-MnTe on various substrates, ranging from transparent band insulators, over topological insulators, metallic transition metal chalcogenides, to the van der Waals ferromagnet Fe$_3$GeTe$_2$. While insulating substrates are useful for transport experiments or optical spectroscopy, metallic topological surface states may trigger spintronic interface effects, such as spin-orbit torques. Metallic substrates, in general, are highly relevant to avoid charging at the insulating MnTe films in electron spectroscopy or microscopy methods. Lastly, ferromagnetic substrates will be of interest to control magnetization across the interface. This data-efficient framework paves the way for the autonomous discovery and thin-film synthesis of functional quantum materials.

TL;DR: 本文通过对称性分析、第一性原理计算（first-principles calculations）和哈密顿模型（Hamiltonian modeling），系统研究了交变磁体（altermagnet）中点缺陷如何通过选择性破坏晶体对称性，在原本纯净宿主中创造出缺失的新相，并在单层V2Se2O等体系中识别出三类不同的杂质效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 交变磁体中的点缺陷可通过选择性破坏晶体对称性，创造出纯净宿主中不存在的新物相，探索这一缺陷工程化途径对调控自旋织构（spin textures）和输运性质具有重要意义。

Method: 采用对称性分析、第一性原理计算与哈密顿模型建模相结合的方法，以d波交变磁单层V2Se2O为测试平台，系统考察点杂质对交变磁相的修饰作用，并在Mn4N2和2H-FeBr3等其他二维体系中验证结论的普适性。

Result: 识别出三类不同的杂质类型：保持自旋-动量锁定（spin-momentum locking）的杂质、诱导与埃德尔斯坦自旋转换（Edelstein spin conversion）相关的混合宇称态的杂质，以及产生伴随反常霍尔效应（anomalous Hall effect）的金属亚铁磁态的杂质；并讨论了二维交变磁体对点杂质的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 在多种二维体系和不同母体自旋谐波（spin harmonics）中均观察到相同的对称性控制机制，表明缺陷几何构型可作为调控自旋织构和输运性质的一般性工程路径。

Abstract: Point defects in altermagnets can create phases absent in the pristine host by selectively breaking crystal symmetries. Combining symmetry analysis, first-principles calculations, and Hamiltonian modeling, we identify how point impurities modify the altermagnetic phase. Using the pristine d- wave altermagnetic monolayer V2Se2O as a testbed, we identify three distinct classes of impurities: those that preserve spin-momentum locking, those that induce a hybrid-parity state associated with Edelstein spin conversion, and those that produce a metallic ferrimagnetic state with an anomalous Hall effect. We further discuss the robustness of two-dimensional altermagnets against point impurities. Results for other two-dimensional systems, such as Mn4N2 and 2H-FeBr3, reveal the same symmetry-based control across distinct lattices and parent spin harmonics, establishing defect geometry as a general route for engineering spin textures and transport properties.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非晶材料缺乏长程结构有序性，使其成为研究超越晶体图像（crystalline picture）电子输运的理想体系，但对这类材料中电子行为与输运机制的系统理解仍面临挑战。

Method: 首先回顾安德森定域化（Anderson localization）、迁移率边（mobility edge）、扩散输运（diffusive transport）和跳跃输运（hopping transport）等基础概念，随后介绍代表性非晶半导体（如非晶硅和非晶氧化物半导体），再讨论从传统玻尔兹曼（Boltzmann）和基于格林函数（Green's function）的输运理论到结合分子动力学（molecular dynamics）的实空间久保-格林伍德（Kubo-Greenwood）模拟等计算方法。

Result: 讨论聚焦于定域化、谱增宽（spectral broadening）、有限温度晶格涨落和电子-声子相互作用（electron-phonon interaction）等方面，并概述了非晶材料预测性输运建模的最新进展与遗留问题。

Conclusion: 综述指出非晶材料输运建模在预测能力上已取得进展，但仍存在若干未解决的问题，需要进一步研究。

Abstract: Amorphous materials are disordered solids without long-range structural order, making them useful systems for studying electron transport beyond the crystalline picture. This review discusses how structural disorder changes the spatial character of electron wavefunctions and how these changes govern carrier transport in amorphous materials, especially amorphous semiconductors. Basic concepts of Anderson localization, mobility edges, diffusive transport, and hopping transport are first reviewed, followed by representative amorphous semiconductors, including amorphous silicon and amorphous oxide semiconductors. Computational approaches are then discussed, from conventional Boltzmann and Green's-function-based transport theories to real-space Kubo-Greenwood simulations combined with molecular dynamics. The discussion focuses on localization, spectral broadening, finite-temperature lattice fluctuations, and electron-phonon interactions. Recent progress and remaining issues in predictive transport modeling of amorphous materials are then outlined.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Er³⁺掺杂CeO₂被视为一种有前景的自旋-光子界面（spin-photon interface），用于量子通信，但限制其光学相干性的机制此前尚不清楚，亟需识别具体的退相干来源以指导材料优化。

Conclusion: Ce³⁺极化子及其复合物是Er³⁺光学退相干的关键成因，该机制为Er³⁺掺杂CeO₂平台提供了明确的缺陷工程（defect engineering）靶点，并可能推广至其他Er³⁺掺杂的多价氧化物（multivalent-oxide）量子平台。

Abstract: Erbium ions (Er$^{3+}$) in cerium dioxide (CeO$_2$) represent a promising spin-photon interface for quantum communication, but the mechanisms limiting their optical coherence remain poorly understood. These defects exhibit finite photoionization cross-sections at 0.8 eV, coinciding with both the laser excitation energy used experimentally and the emission energy of Er$^{3+}$. This resonance enables photoionization of the polarons and photoluminescence quenching of Er$^{3+}$, leading to the broadening of optical linewidths, shortening of excited-state lifetimes, and introduction of charge noise. Our concentration-dependent photocurrent measurements in Er$^{3+}$-doped CeO$_2$ films under 0.8 eV illumination validate the predicted decoherence pathway. Our combined computational and experimental results identify a concrete defect-engineering target for improving the Er$^{3+}$-doped CeO$_2$ platform, and point to a decoherence mechanism likely relevant to other Er$^{3+}$-doped multivalent-oxide quantum platforms.

</details>

Result: 器件成功模拟了EPSC、约170%的PPF以及刺激依赖的STP与LTP；每个突触事件仅消耗皮焦（picojoule）量级能量，暗电流为纳安（nanoampere）量级，并实现了巴甫洛夫联想学习的低功耗神经形态模拟。ANN仿真在MNIST手写数字数据集上达到92.43%的识别准确率；还基于器件的波长选择性光响应特性实现了彩色图像滤波的先进神经形态视觉处理。

Conclusion: 该简单而多功能（multifunctional）的器件架构为节能、光谱选择性（spectral-selective）的神经形态视觉应用提供了一条有前景的路径。

Abstract: Two-dimensional transition-metal dichalcogenide alloys are potential candidates for advanced optoelectronic and neuromorphic applications due to their strong light-matter interactions and controllable defect properties. However, large-area growth of such alloys remains challenging, while the correlation between their physical and neuromorphic properties remains largely unclear. In this work, we present an innovative microcavity chemical vapor deposition (CVD) reactor pathway to grow uniform, and large-area MoWS$_2$ mono- and few-layer alloy films for demonstrating optoelectronic synaptic functionalities. Driven by growth-induced intrinsic sulfur vacancies, as confirmed by XPS, KPFM, and STEM measurements, our optoelectronic synaptic device (OSD) successfully emulates essential biological synaptic features, such as excitatory postsynaptic currents (EPSC), paired-pulse facilitation (PPF~170%), and stimulus-dependent short- and long-term plasticities (STP & LTP). With picojoule-order energy consumption per synaptic event and nanoampere-order dark current, the device enables low-power neuromorphic learning, including emulation of Pavlovian associative learning. Furthermore, the experimentally measured conductance weight-update characteristics enabled an artificial neural network (ANN) simulation to achieve 92.43% recognition accuracy on the MNIST handwritten digit dataset. Finally, we demonstrate advanced neuromorphic visual processing by executing color image filtering based on the device's wavelength-selective photoresponse characteristics. This simple, yet multifunctional device architecture provides a promising path toward energy-efficient, spectral-selective neuromorphic vision applications.

</details>

*Sangyong Lee,Anton V. Ievlev,Dongjae Shin,Jingxian Li,Yiyang Li*

Main category: physics.app-ph

TL;DR: 本研究提出了一种基于 Ta₂O₅/TaOₓ 的双端阻变存储单元（resistive memory cell），同时实现了非细丝式（nonfilamentary）开关与长数据保持能力，打破了传统 ReRAM 中保持性与均匀性之间的根本权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 阻变存储器（ReRAM）中，细丝型（filament-type）器件数据保持时间长但均匀性差、开关电流高；而非细丝型器件开关电流低、面积相关但保持性差，目前尚无双端器件能同时克服这一权衡。

Method: 制备了 Ta₂O₅/TaOₓ 双端阻变存储单元，通过电学测量与组分深度剖面（composition depth profile）分析开关机制，并在190°C高温下测试数据保持能力，提出长保持性源于非晶氧化钽中组成相分离（composition phase separation）的机制解释。

Result: 电学测量和组分深度剖面显示开关过程并非局限于单一细丝，而是在整个开关区域内均匀发生；该器件在190°C下可保持信息超过22小时，性能可与最优细丝型器件相媲美。

Conclusion: 该工作证明了信息保持性与开关均匀性之间的根本权衡可以被克服，为开发更均匀、更可靠的氧化物存储器件提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Resistive random-access memory (ReRAM) is a promising future nonvolatile memory technology. Most ReRAM exhibit a fundamental tradeoff: filament-type ReRAM provides long data retention but suffers from poor uniformity and high switching current, whereas nonfilamentary ReRAM shows lower-current, area-dependent switching but generally poor retention. No two-terminal device has been able to overcome this tradeoff. In this work, we present a two-terminal Ta2O5/ TaOX resistive memory cell that achieves both nonfilamentary switching and long retention. Electrical measurements and composition depth profile show that the switching is not confined to a single filament but is instead uniform across the entire switching region. Despite the nonfilamentary switching, this device can retain information for over 22 hours at 190 °C, which is comparable to the best filamentary devices. We propose that this long retention arises from composition phase separation in amorphous tantalum oxide. Our work shows that the fundamental tradeoff between information retention and switching uniformity can be overcome, and thereby provides a pathway toward more uniform and reliable oxide memory devices.

</details>

### [34] [Nonadaptive Learning in Robust Nonlinear Output Regulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17262)
*Shimin Wang,Martin Guay,Richard D. Braatz*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文针对任意高相对阶（high relative degree）输出反馈（output-feedback）设置下的一般非线性系统，提出了一种鲁棒非自适应调节（robust nonadaptive regulation）设计方法。该方法将输入驱动滤波器、通用内模（internal model）与递归反步（backstepping）控制律相结合，把调节问题转化为增广误差系统的鲁棒输入到状态稳定（input-to-state stabilization）问题，并在标准外系统假设下建立了全局渐近调节结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的自适应调节方案依赖于线性参数化回归元（linearly parameterized regressors），并需要构造仅具有非正导数的李雅普诺夫函数（Lyapunov function），这些条件在实际非线性系统中往往难以满足。因此，需要发展一种不依赖这些限制条件的非自适应鲁棒调节框架，以适用于系统动力学复杂或仅部分已知的情形。

Method: 提出一种非自适应设计，将输入驱动滤波器与通用内模结合，并通过递归反步控制律构建增广误差系统，将原始调节问题转化为该增广系统的鲁棒输入到状态稳定问题。设计依赖外系统特征值满足纯虚且简单（purely imaginary and simple eigenvalues）的标准假设，以及内动态满足最小相位输入到状态稳定性（minimum-phase input-to-state stability）条件。

Result: 建立了全局渐近调节（global asymptotic regulation）的理论结果，并推导出可验证的设计增益显式不等式。该方法无需线性参数化回归元，也无需构造仅具有非正导数的李雅普诺夫函数，同时保证估计误差和跟踪误差的收敛性。理论结果通过基准受控杜芬系统（Duffing system）仿真得到验证。

Conclusion: 所提出的非自适应鲁棒调节框架适用于系统动力学复杂或仅部分已知的一般非线性输出反馈系统，克服了自适应方案的典型限制，扩展了鲁棒输出调节（robust output regulation）的适用范围。

Abstract: This paper considers robust nonadaptive regulation for general nonlinear systems in an output-feedback setting with arbitrarily high relative degree. We develop a nonadaptive design that combines an input-driven filter and a generic internal model with a recursive backstepping law, thereby recasting the regulation problem as the robust input-to-state stabilization of an augmented error system. Unlike adaptive schemes, the proposed method does not rely on linearly parameterized regressors and does not require the construction of Lyapunov functions having merely nonpositive derivatives. Under standard assumptions on the exosystem, including purely imaginary and simple eigenvalues, together with a minimum-phase input-to-state stability condition on the internal dynamics, we establish global asymptotic regulation and derive explicit, verifiable inequalities for selecting the design gains. The resulting nonadaptive framework guarantees convergence of the estimation and tracking errors even when the controlled-system dynamics are complex or only partially known. The effectiveness of the theoretical results is demonstrated using a benchmark controlled Duffing system.

</details>

### [35] [Critical couplings of two dimensional Ising model on various lattices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16949)
*Sh. Khachatryan,A. Sedrakyan*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文利用Kac–Ward表示发展了一种统一的费米子场（fermionic field）表述，用于在多种平面格点上的二维伊辛模型（Ising model），通过行列式表达配分函数，并成功复现了包括方格子、蜂窝、三角、kagomé及对偶kagomé（dice/T₃）格点的精确临界耦合，特别给出了各向异性kagomé格点的完整临界曲面和低能谱方程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 二维伊辛模型在不同平面格点上的临界行为（critical behavior）和低能激发（low-energy excitations）通常需要针对不同格点几何分别处理，缺乏统一的表述框架。本文旨在发展一种基于Kac–Ward表示的费米子场公式，以统一描述具有不同局域几何和配位数（coordination number）的格点上的伊辛模型。

Method: 采用Kac–Ward表示，将Grassmann场（Grassmann field）关联到有向格点链（directed lattice links），费米子轨迹在格点顶点处的转向由相应的Kac–Ward相位因子编码。配分函数通过有限维动量空间矩阵的行列式表示，该矩阵的零点决定激发谱和临界耦合。

Result: 方法成功应用于正则方格子、蜂窝、三角、kagomé和对偶kagomé（dice/T₃）格点，在所有情况下均复现了已知的精确临界耦合。对kagomé格点给出了完整的临界曲面和低能谱方程；对各向异性dice模型推导了临界条件，在各项同性的dice模型中，低能动量下谱退化为相对论性有质量形式，质量在cosh(2J_c)=(1+√3)/2处消失。

Conclusion: 结果表明，同一套费米子构造能够为具有不同局域几何和配位数的格点上的伊辛模型提供临界性和低能激发的一致紧凑描述，展示了该方法的普适性。

Abstract: We develop a unified fermionic-field formulation of the two-dimensional Ising model on several planar lattices using the Kac--Ward representation. Grassmann fields are associated with directed lattice links, while the turning of a fermionic trajectory at a lattice vertex is encoded by the corresponding Kac--Ward phase factor. Within this approach the partition function is expressed through the determinant of a finite-dimensional momentum-space matrix, whose zeros determine the excitation spectrum and the critical coupling.
  We apply the method to the regular square, honeycomb, triangular, kagomé, and dual kagomé (dice or $T_3$) lattices. In all cases the known exact critical couplings are reproduced. Particular attention is given to the anisotropic kagomé lattice, for which the fermionic determinant yields the complete critical surface and the low-energy spectral equation. We also construct the fermionic action for the dual kagomé lattice and derive its anisotropic critical condition. In the isotropic dice model the spectrum reduces at low energy and momentum to a relativistic massive form, with the mass vanishing at $\cosh(2J_c)=(1+\sqrt3)/2$. The results demonstrate that the same fermionic construction provides a compact description of criticality and low-energy excitations for Ising models on lattices with different local geometries and coordination numbers.

</details>


### [36] [Incidence-based random walks on simplicial complexes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17229)
*C. T. Martínez-Martínez,Francisco J. Sevilla*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于边发生的随机游走（incidence-based random walk）模型，作用于具有完整1-骨架和独立保留三角面的随机二维单纯复形（random two-dimensional simplicial complex）上。模型通过混合参数q将顶点介导和三角面介导两种传输通道结合，揭示了中间面密度下高层级连通性（higher-order connectivity）异质性导致的传输瓶颈及非单调谱与输运行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统网络动力学多基于成对（pairwise）连接，难以刻画单纯复形中高层级连通性对谱与输运性质的影响。作者旨在构建一个简单框架，在不修改底层成对支撑结构的前提下，隔离并研究高层级连通性效应。

Abstract: We introduce an incidence-based random walk on the edges of a random two-dimensional simplicial complex with a complete $1$-skeleton and independently retained triangular faces. This construction isolates the effects of higher-order connectivity without modifying the underlying pairwise support of the walk. We characterize the model through structural observables, spectral relaxation, stationary localization, and first-passage transport. Our results show that partial face retention generates heterogeneous higher-order connectivity, giving rise to a pronounced transport bottleneck at intermediate face densities. In this regime, the second-largest eigenvalue modulus, the inverse participation ratio of the stationary distribution, and the mean first-passage time all exhibit non-monotonic behavior, reaching their largest values at intermediate face densities. The corresponding first-passage-time distributions reveal an enhanced probability of unusually long trajectories. Together, these results establish a simple framework for investigating how heterogeneous higher-order connectivity reshapes spectral and transport properties beyond pairwise network dynamics.

Abstract: We explore kinetic-constraint induced thermodynamic phase transition in the cubic lattice, employing the Fredrikson-Anderson spin model with hyperparameter $K=2$ as a representative kinetic system. We discover a continuous thermodynamic gas--crystal phase transition in the cubic system and determine the critical chemical potential $μ^* \approx -3.252$, at which the occupied sites of the equilibrium tree-packing configurations start to prefer one of the two nested cubic sublattices. This thermodynamic phase transition is absent in the two-dimensional square lattice.

For an SU(2) model on the complete simplicial $2$-complex with quenched random plaquette couplings, parallel-tempering simulations reveal a continuous disorder-driven loss of global compatibility. In the high-disorder phase, the uniform compatibility $M_P$ decreases with system size, the integrated adjacent correlation weight $\mathcal I_{\mathrm{adj}}$ remains finite value. Moreover, the connected replica-overlap width approaches the numerical noise floor and its distribution narrows, providing no evidence for thermodynamic replica-symmetry breaking. Dense frustration therefore produces a non-glassy correlated gauge liquid in which individual pair correlations are geometrically diluted while a finite integrated correlation weight survives.

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 论文为CPMC框架提供严格的数学和算法基础：形式上定义联合路径图概率权重（joint path-graph probability weights），推导保证细致平衡（detailed balance）的转移和均匀化求和规则；针对SIS模型系统构建并优化单节点和边图顶点集以防止锁雪崩（lock avalanches）并保持流行病主干的机构流动性；提出动态规划（dynamic programming）方案以精确实现复杂边界条件（包括初始感染者编号和宏观暴发规模约束），实现无拒绝的有效轨迹生成；评估算法计算复杂度并描述并行化策略。

Result: 该方法能够在小网络动力学上通过精确解验证CPMC的正确性，同时克服了传统方法在稀有事件模拟中的主要困难。通过优化图顶点集和动态规划边界条件实现，CPMC可产生无拒绝（rejection-free）的有效轨迹。

Conclusion: CPMC框架为随机网络动力学中的稀有宏观事件模拟提供了一种可行的替代方案，其严格数学基础和高效算法设计使其在小网络上与精确解一致。该工作为更广泛的应用（如疫情建模、通信网络故障分析和多体系统研究）提供了理论基础。

Abstract: The simulation of rare macroscopic events in stochastic network dynamics, such as widespread epidemic outbreaks, cascading failures in communication networks, or the escape from metastable states in many-body systems, is severely hindered by methodological challenges like catastrophic rejection rates, weight degeneracy, genealogical correlations, and critical slowing down inherent to standard forward-time algorithms, splitting methods, and transition-path sampling. Conditional-path Monte Carlo (CPMC) overcomes these limitations by employing non-local Swendsen-Wang-like cluster updates that operate directly on full-system trajectories. Serving as the technical companion to [Sun, Moody, and Barthel, arXiv:2608.16171], this paper provides the rigorous mathematical foundations and algorithmic details underlying the CPMC framework. We formally define the joint path-graph probability weights and derive the transition and uniformization sum rules that guarantee detailed balance. Applying the framework to susceptible-infectious-susceptible (SIS) models, we systematically construct and optimize single-node and edge graph vertex sets specifically designed to prevent lock avalanches and maintain the structural mobility of the epidemic trunk. Furthermore, we detail a dynamic programming scheme to exactly implement complex boundary conditions - including patient-zero and macroscopic outbreak-size constraints - enabling the rejection-free generation of valid trajectories. Finally, we assess the computational complexity of the algorithm, describe parallelization strategies, and validate CPMC against exact solutions for dynamics on small networks.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>

Result: 对于限制在反射边界间的单粒子，热容在持续性区域出现最大值，对应于形状转变（shape transition）。对于周期晶格上的排斥相互作用活性粒子，堵塞团簇的重组在热响应中产生相应的峰。此外，研究显示AC量热法（AC calorimetry）在实验中可观测到相同的热响应特征。

Conclusion: 非平衡热容可以作为非平衡相变的量热探针，为活性物质系统中持续性与相互作用诱导的相变提供了一种可实验测量的热力学诊断手段。

Abstract: Persistence influences the stationary states of active particles, producing boundary accumulation at the single-particle level, and clustering or jamming in interacting systems. These features disappear as the persistence decreases and the system approaches a more passive-like stationary state. We show that these transitions have a distinct calorimetric signature. Using a lattice run-and-tumble dynamics, consistent with local detailed balance, we compute the nonequilibrium heat capacity from the excess heat released following a small temperature perturbation. For a single particle confined between reflecting boundaries, the heat capacity develops a maximum in the persistence regime corresponding to shape transition. Adding an exclusion interaction to the active particles on a periodic lattice, the reorganization of jammed clusters produces a corresponding peak in the thermal response. We also discuss the impact of the time-symmetric part of the transition rates, and show the possibility of seeing the same signatures of heat response in experiments by AC calorimetry. Our results show that nonequilibrium heat capacities can serve as calorimetric probes of nonequilibrium phase transitions.

</details>

Abstract: The Light-Heavy (LH) model involves two species of particles (light and heavy) coupled with a fluctuating surface (described by tilts). The dynamics include the inherent diffusion of the particles (or tilts) as well as the drive provided by the tilts (or particles). When the two are of similar magnitude, the system lies in the unscaled (uLH) regime, while a significantly weaker drive leads to the scaled (sLH) regime. In the unscaled limit, the model exhibits an order-disorder transition characterized by the fluctuation-dominated phase ordering (FDPO). In this state, interestingly the dynamics is driven by multiple modes, giving rise to dynamic clusters. Away from the critical regime the disordered phase retains vestiges of FDPO behavior on length scales smaller than the correlation length. We examine this local FDPO-like behavior by using a scaling function that links the off-critical and critical regimes. We next turn to the scaled model and show that the multi-mode dynamics present in the unscaled regime is replaced by dynamics that is effectively controlled by a single dominant mode in the scaled regime. Concurrently, the two-point correlations change from the $\mathcal{O}(1)$ FDPO form to an anomalous long-range form that decays as $1/\sqrt{L}$. Drawing on the analogy with the sABC model, where similar anomalous correlations appear at criticality, we derive an analytical expression for the two-point correlation function using the same approach used for that model.

</details>

Method: 本文引入迭代张量网络变换（ITNTs），一种通用算法框架，用于对以张量列（TTs）编码的数据进行初等函数与非线性滤波函数的逐元素求值。该方法完全在压缩域内运行，从而能够在受控计算成本下对指数级规模的数据集进行高效计算。

Result: 方法在两大关键领域得到验证：其一，在三维反应流场上评估高度非线性的初等函数与滤波函数，实现高保真反应速率计算与区域滤波；其二，在复杂优化问题中寻找极值，例如求解空间规模高达2^70配置的Max-SAT实例。

Conclusion: ITNTs被确立为基础性工具（foundational tool），赋予张量网络方法执行通用数据科学与大规模优化的能力。

Abstract: Tensor networks are powerful formats for compressing large-scale data. However, their application to general data processing has been limited by the difficulty of performing nonlinear operations. Here, we introduce iterative tensor network transformations (ITNTs), a general algorithmic framework for the element-wise evaluation of elementary and nonlinear filtering functions on data encoded as tensor trains (TTs), a class of tensor networks. Our approach operates entirely in the compressed domain, enabling efficient computation on exponentially large datasets while maintaining a controlled computational cost. We demonstrate its power in two key areas: (I) evaluating highly nonlinear elementary and filtering functions on a 3D reactive flow field, enabling high-fidelity reaction rate computation and region filtering, and (II) finding extrema in complex optimization problems, such as solving Max-SAT instances on spaces up to $2^{70}$ configurations. These results establish ITNT as a foundational tool that provides tensor network methods with the capability for general-purpose data science and large-scale optimization.

</details>

Abstract: We introduce a unified framework for dark energy diagnostics based on the joint phase space of the equation of state $w$ and the sound speed $c_s^2$. The resulting $w-c_s^2$ plane provides a minimal extension beyond background cosmology, capturing both the expansion history and perturbative properties within a single representation. Building on this framework, we define the microphysical flow parameter $F=dc_s^2/dw$, which encodes the dynamical relation between background evolution and perturbative response. We derive a direct connection between the present-day value $F_0$, $H_0$, and $σ_8$, and show that the microphysical flow parameter enables a hierarchy of increasingly stringent consistency tests that substantially reduce the viable dark energy theory space. We further demonstrate how trajectories in the $w-c_s^2$ plane distinguish models that are nearly degenerate at the level of $w(a)$, including canonical quintessence, Chaplygin gas models, and noncanonical scalar field realizations. This framework provides a compact phenomenological bridge between dark energy microphysics and future perturbation-sensitive observations, establishing $F_0$ as a useful discriminator of the kinetic structure underlying cosmic acceleration.

Abstract: Persistence changes character as an autoregressive coefficient approaches one: for each fixed $0 < a < 1$, survival above zero decays exponentially, whereas at the unit root symmetric random-walk survival is of order $n^{-1/2}$. We study this transition for AR($1$) sequences driven by symmetric $α$-stable innovations and write $Λ(a,α)$ for their exponential persistence rate. The entire chain admits an exact representation through a single stable Lévy process observed on a geometrically expanding time grid. Comparison with continuous half-line survival gives $Λ(a,α) \leq \fracα{2}\log{(1/a)}$. For $0 < α< 2$, this bound disproves the stable specialization of a conjecture of Hinrichs, Kolb and Wachtel for regularly varying innovation tails. To obtain a lower bound of the same near-unit order, we combine stable closure under subsampling with a monotonicity coupling. This proves $Λ(a,α) \asymp \log{(1/a)}$ as $a \uparrow 1$ and shows that the ratio $Λ(a,α)/\log{(1/a)}$ converges to a limit in $(0,α/2]$, equal to its supremum over $0 < a < 1$. Finally, a Lamperti transformation reduces identification of this constant to a dense-sampling persistence problem for a stationary stable Ornstein--Uhlenbeck process. Existing Gaussian theory determines the sharp value at $α=2$. For $0 < α< 2$, identifying the value requires controlling paths that cross below zero and return above zero between consecutive observations.

</details>

Abstract: A body floating atop a supersaturated fluid may accumulate bubbles along its underbelly, which can render the body rotationally unstable. But rotation can strip the surface of these bubbles when they make contact with the air above. To explore this coupling we perform experiments using cylinders floating on carbonated water. The cylinders exhibit an array of distinct dynamics, including constant rolling, periodic and aperiodic oscillation, chaos, and intermittent capsizing, which can be tuned by body size, mass distribution, and gas concentration. A continuum model for the bubble density dynamics, and Galerkin projection, tie the experiment to the generalized Lorenz system and its famed chaotic attractor. Even an extremely small center-of-mass offset can have a qualitative impact on the dynamics, and an offset of as little as a few percent can fully stabilize the system. The experiment represents a highly accessible physical realization of the Lorenz system, which, owing to continuous gas loss from the fluid to the air above, sweeps slowly without intervention across a classical bifurcation diagram in time.

Abstract: Poroelastic materials, such as polymer tissue scaffolds, porous rocks, and hydrogels, can weaken due to interactions between the solid skeleton and chemical species in the interstitial fluid. Our model couples large-deformation poroelasticity with an advection-diffusion equation for the solute. Furthermore, we introduce a decay equation for the material stiffness, whose rate of decay depends on the solute concentration. In this way, we describe a three-way coupling between poroelastic deformation, weakening of the skeleton and transport of solute through the material. We exploit numerical and analytical techniques to reveal the flow-driven uniaxial compression of a weakening poroelastic material and determine parameter regimes for which weakening the material facilitates pore closure at the downstream boundary. We identify parameter regimes in which (1) a steady state is attained without pore closure, (2) pore closure occurs at a finite time or (3) the pores close instantaneously; we uncover case (2) through the introduction of weakening into the system. We provide insights into the relationship between the differing behaviours and the separation between the timescales of the system. For systems with slow weakening, we derive a leading-order approximation for the time of pore closure, treating the ratio of the timescales of poroelastic relaxation and weakening as a small parameter, and investigate the accuracy of this approximation and the new behaviours that arise when these timescales become comparable.

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Abstract: We classify left-invariant Rarita-Schwinger fields on 2- and 3-dimensional Lie groups with left-invariant Riemannian metrics. The left-invariant Rarita-Schwinger equation is reduced to an algebraic system on the associated metric Lie algebra. We show that, in dimension 2, non-trivial examples occur only in the flat abelian case. In dimension 3, apart from the flat abelian case, the only non-trivial examples occur on SU(2) equipped with a special left-invariant metric, for which the space of left-invariant Rarita-Schwinger fields has complex dimension 2. This metric is a Berger metric on $S^3$.

</details>

TL;DR: 本研究将Pais-Uhlenbeck振荡器——一种具有单一内部频率参数ω的高阶导数模型——用于建模虚拟现实头部指向运动学。62名健康年轻被试完成的水平与垂直头部运动数据支持了模型预测的运动时长(T)与平滑度(LDLJ)之间的抛物线关系（R²=0.851），且方向仅影响抛物线截距与线性系数，不影响二次系数；该模型为颈部运动规划提供了非侵入性量化框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 头部运动需要整合视觉、本体感觉、前庭觉和颈部运动信号，但其时间组织的动力学原理尚不清楚。已有模型难以从原理层面解释头部运动的时间特征，因此需要引入新的理论框架来描述其运动学规律。

Method: 采用Pais-Uhlenbeck振荡器（一种高阶导数力学模型，含单一内部频率参数ω）预测头部运动时长与平滑度之间的抛物线关系；62名健康年轻成人在虚拟现实环境下执行振幅30°的水平与垂直头部指向任务，记录运动时长、端点精度、峰值角速度、过冲量及对数无量纲急动度（log-dimensionless jerk, LDLJ），并通过混合效应抛物线回归进行验证，同时以参数ω进行内部一致性检验。

Result: 混合效应抛物线回归确认了LDLJ与运动时长T之间的抛物线关系（R²=0.851）；二次系数不受方向或被试显著影响，但截距和线性系数在水平与垂直运动间存在差异；由回归估计的ω参数推导出的运动时长与实测值高度吻合，通过内部一致性检验。

Conclusion: 头部指向运动存在非线性的时间组织，并受方向特异性生物力学约束调节。高阶导数力学（higher-derivative mechanics）可能为量化颈部运动规划提供了一种有原理依据的非侵入性框架，需在临床人群中进一步验证。

Abstract: Head movements require integration of visual, proprioceptive, vestibular, and cervical motor signals. The dynamical principles governing their temporal organization remain unclear. We tested whether a Pais-Uhlenbeck oscillator -- a higher-derivative model with one internal frequency parameter $ω$ -- can model head kinematics during virtual-reality-based head pointing. Our model predicts a parabolic relationship between movement duration ($T$) and smoothness (log-dimensionless jerk, $LDLJ$) that we have experimentally checked. Sixty-two healthy young adults performed horizontal and vertical head movements with an amplitude of 30°. Movement duration, endpoint accuracy, peak angular velocity, overshoot, and $LDLJ$ were extracted from headset kinematics. A mixed-effects parabolic regression confirmed the predicted $LDLJ$ vs $T$ parabolic relation ($R^2 = 0.851$). The quadratic coefficient was not significantly modified by direction or participant, but the intercept and linear coefficient differed between horizontal and vertical movements. As an internal consistency check of the model, we find that the parameter $ω$, as estimated from the regressions, gives time durations that closely match the measured ones. These findings outline a nonlinear temporal organization of head pointing modulated by direction-specific biomechanical constraints, and suggest that higher-derivative mechanics may provide a principled, non-invasive framework for quantifying cervical motor planning, warranting further validation in clinical populations.

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生物网络虽具有反复出现的模体（motif），但其不均等的丰度长期未被充分理解。前馈环作为大肠杆菌和酿酒酵母转录网络中的重要模体，其八种类型出现频率极不均匀，传统框架难以解释这种模式，本研究旨在建立一种信息理论框架来揭示丰度模式的拓扑来源。

Method: 本文构建了一个信息论框架，将输入-输出互信息（mutual information）分解为通路成分（pathway component）和干扰成分（interference component）。其中干扰互信息（interference mutual information, IMI）量化直接与间接调控通路对信息传输的联合影响，并进一步将其层次追溯到通路-干扰相互作用的强度及局部通路敏感性。

Result: 在两种生物中，IMI均紧密跟随观察到的FFL丰度模式，而输入-输出互信息和通路成分则不能解释丰度差异。更强者IMI还与参数变化的稳健性（robustness）降低相关，提示基因调控回路中存在信息处理与弹性之间的权衡。

Conclusion: 研究识别出一种与丰度模式相关的拓扑依赖信息论特征（IMI），为理解基因调控网络中模体不均等丰度的成因提供了新的理论视角，并揭示了信息处理能力与系统稳健性之间的潜在权衡关系。

Abstract: Biological networks feature recurring motifs, but their uneven abundance remains poorly understood. Feed-forward loops (FFLs) are important motifs in the transcriptional networks of \textit{Escherichia coli} and \textit{Saccharomyces cerevisiae}, yet their eight types appear in highly unequal frequencies. This study presents an information-theoretic framework that decomposes input-output mutual information into pathway and interference components. The interference mutual information (IMI) quantifies the joint influence of direct and indirect regulatory pathways on information transmission. Our results show that IMI closely follows the observed abundance patterns in both organisms, whereas the input-output mutual information and the pathway component do not. We also trace the IMI hierarchy to the strength of pathway-interference interactions and local pathway sensitivities. Notably, a stronger IMI is also associated with reduced robustness to parameter variation, suggesting a trade-off between information processing and resilience in gene regulatory circuits. Our results, thus, identify a topology-dependent information-theoretic feature that relates to the abundance patterns.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 需要检验并回顾外流法在不同数据集中对Sgr A*自旋参数的稳健性，以确认该方法对弱致密射电源（weak compact radio source）测量的可靠性。

Method: 采用外流法对六组独立的同时或同期获得的X射线与射电数据进行处理，提取Sgr A*的无量纲自旋角动量、无量纲自旋函数及黑洞质量分量等参数，并对不同数据集的结果进行交叉验证。

Result: 六组数据集获得一致结果；首选数据集（Set I）给出a_* = 0.90 ± 0.06和F = 0.62 ± 0.10，与Daly（2019）在独立数据集上获得的a_* = 0.93 ± 0.15在误差范围内一致。

Conclusion: 将外流法应用于当前状态下弱致密射电源Sgr A*时，使用同时或同期的射电与X射线测量可获得最精确的自旋参数结果。

Abstract: The dimensionless spin angular momentum, dimensionless spin function, and blackhole mass components of Sagittarius A* (Sgr A*) were obtained by Daly et al. (2024) by applying the outflow method to six independent sets of simultaneously or contemporaneously obtained X-ray and radio data. Here, results obtained for Sgr A* with the outflow method are reviewed. Consistent results were obtained with each data set. Set I (the preferred data set) indicates that Sgr A* has a dimensionless spin angular momentum $a_* = (0.90 ~\pm~ 0.06)$ and a dimensionless spin function $F = (0.62 ~\pm~ 0.10)$. The results are consistent with the value of $a_* = (0.93~\pm~ 0.15)$ obtained with the outflow method applied to an independent data set (Daly 2019). The application of the outflow method to a weak compact radio source such as Sgr A* in its current state is most accurately determined with simultaneous or contemporaneous radio and X-ray measurements.

</details>

### [55] [On the Cauchy-Hamel Continuity of Real Functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16942)
*Gabriel Istrate*

Main category: math.LO

TL;DR: 本文比较了多种使所有加性函数（additive function）连续的"病态"连续性概念，目标是确定其中哪一种性质最佳，值得被称为柯西-哈梅尔连续性（Cauchy-Hamel continuity）。结论表明，先前提出的Q连续性（Q-continuity）概念的双边版本似乎是最有前景的候选者。该工作与力学的公理化基础相关，特别是力的平行四边形法则的公理化刻画问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 达布（Darboux）曾指出，平行四边形法则的特征刻画需要连续性假设；若无该公理，则可能存在基于替代合成法则的"奇异"物理模型。一个引人深思的问题是，这些奇异物理模型是否仍具备某种弱的、残余的连续性概念。本文所研究的概念为该问题提供了初步回答的线索。

Method: 本文对多种使所有加性函数连续的病态连续性概念进行系统比较，包括先前提出的Q连续性的双边版本。通过对这些概念的相对行为进行评估，判断哪一种在数学上表现最佳，从而作为柯西-哈梅尔连续性的最佳候选。

Result: 研究表明，先前引入的Q连续性概念的双边版本似乎是最有前景的候选者，值得被称为柯西-哈梅尔连续性。本文所考察的概念对"奇异物理模型是否仍具有弱连续性"这一问题提供了初步线索。

Conclusion: 双边形式的Q连续性最有资格被命名为柯西-哈梅尔连续性。该结果对力学公理化基础，尤其是力的平行四边形法则的特征刻画问题具有相关性，并为探索奇异物理模型中的残余连续性提供了理论起点。

Abstract: We compare several "pathological" notions of continuity that make every additive function continuous. Our goal is to determine which of these notions is best behaved and deserves to be called Cauchy-Hamel continuity. The conclusion is that a bilateral version of a previously introduced notion of Q-continuity seems the most promising candidate.
  Our work is relevant to the axiomatic foundations of mechanics, specifically to the problem of axiomatically characterizing the parallelogram rule for the composition of forces. It was noted by Darboux that a continuity assumption is needed for such a characterization of the parallelogram rule and that, absent such a continuity axiom, "exotic" physical models based on alternative composition rules may exist. An intriguing question is whether such exotic physical models still possess some weak, residual notion of continuity. The concepts studied in this paper offer a first hint of a response to this question.

</details>

### [56] [Equilibrium and nonequlibrium scaling behaviors of localization transition in a non-Hermitian Aubry-André model with onsite gain and loss](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17527)
*Wen-Jing Yu,Yue-Mei Sun,Xin-Yu Wang,Liang-Jun Zhai*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 本文通过有限尺寸标度分析和线性驱动的非平衡动力学研究，系统刻画了含原位增益与损耗（on-site gain and loss）的非厄米Aubry-André模型的局域化跃迁临界行为，提取出临界指数ν=1.00(2)、s=0.7965(2)、z=1.999(2)，证明该机制属于一个新的普适类，并验证了有限时间标度（FTS）框架在该非厄米系统无带隙初始态下的适用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非厄米性与局域化的相互作用已引起广泛关注，但在含原位增益与损耗的非厄米系统中，局域化跃迁的驱动动力学仍缺乏系统性研究。本文旨在填补这一空白，探究此类系统的临界标度行为及跨临界点的非平衡动力学。

Method: 作者对含原位增益与损耗的非厄米Aubry-André (AA)模型进行有限尺寸标度分析，基于局域化长度、反参与率（IPR）和能隙提取临界指数。在驱动动力学部分，将系统初始制备于无带隙扩展态，以线性驱动方式跨过临界点，并采用有限时间标度（FTS）框架进行标度验证，在满足判据z'<r的条件下检验预测的标度形式。

Result: 提取的临界指数为ν=1.00(2)、s=0.7965(2)、z=1.999(2)，其中IPR指数s与厄米AA模型及非互易跳跃AA模型均不同，表明增益-损耗机制属于不同的普适类。数值结果在广泛的系统尺寸和驱动速率范围内验证了FTS预测的IPR标度形式。

Conclusion: 本文确立了含增益-损耗的AA模型作为局域化跃迁的一个新普适类，同时将有限时间标度框架的适用范围推广至具有无带隙初始态的非厄米系统，为统一标度描述在非厄米物理中的应用提供了新案例。

Abstract: The interplay between non-Hermiticity and localization has attracted considerable interest, yet the driven dynamics of localization transitions in non-Hermitian systems with on-site gain and loss remains largely unexplored. Here we investigate the critical scaling behavior and driven dynamics of the non-Hermitian Aubry-André (AA) model with on-site gain and loss. Through finite-size scaling analyses of the localization length, the inverse participation ratio (IPR), and the energy gap, we extract the critical exponents $ν= 1.00(2)$, $s = 0.7965(2)$, and $z = 1.999(2)$. These exponents are different from those of both the Hermitian AA model and the nonreciprocal hopping AA model, particularly the IPR exponent $s$, demonstrating that the gain-loss mechanism belongs to a distinct universality class. For the driven dynamics, we focus on the case where the system is initially prepared in a gapless extended state and linearly driven across the critical point. We verify that the finite-time scaling (FTS) framework remains applicable provided that the criterion $z' < r$ is satisfied, where $z' = 1.999(2)$ characterizes the gap closure in the extended phase and $r = z + 1/ν\approx 2.999$. The predicted FTS scaling forms for the IPR are numerically validated across a wide range of system sizes and driving rates, demonstrating that the unified scaling description can be successfully generalized to the gain-loss type non-Hermitian AA model. Our work not only establishes the gain-loss AA model as a new universality class of localization transitions but also extends the applicability of the FTS framework to non-Hermitian systems with gapless initial states.

</details>


### [57] [Long-range Nonlinear Sigma Model for a Singular Quantum Kicked Rotor](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17649)
*Weitao Chen,Yunxiang Liao*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 本文从具有幂律或对数奇异性的量子踢转子（quantum kicked rotor）出发，直接推导出一个非局域超对称非线性sigma模型，并通过两阶重整化群分析证明，在匹配对称性类别和耦合约定后，转子系统再现了对应幂律随机带矩阵（power-law random banded matrices，PRBM）的远程安德森相变，包括局域化、临界和扩展三种红外行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 奇异踢转子与幂律随机带矩阵（PRBM）长期被相互对比，因为二者动量空间的Floquet矩阵元均呈代数衰减。然而，转子中的确定性关联在长距离下是否变得无关、以及两者在何种条件下共享相同的红外理论，此前尚不明确，这一问题正是本研究试图解答的核心动机。

Method: 本文直接从具有幂律或对数奇异性的量子踢转子出发，推导出一个非局域超对称非线性sigma模型（supersymmetric nonlinear sigma model），并对其进行至两阶（two-loop order）的重整化群（renormalization group，RG）分析，同时匹配相应的对称性类别（symmetry class）与耦合约定以确保理论可比较性。

Result: 在匹配对称性类别和耦合约定后，转子模型再现了对应PRBM的远程安德森相变（long-range Anderson transition），涵盖局域化（localized）、临界（critical）和扩展（extended）三类红外区域。

Conclusion: 本研究首次从确定性量子踢转子出发系统证明其与PRBM在红外极限下具有相同的有效理论，尽管转子内部存在确定性关联，它们在长距离尺度上不影响临界行为；但该等效性依赖于对称性类别与耦合约定的正确匹配。

Abstract: Singular kicked rotors have long been compared with power-law random banded matrices (PRBM) because their momentum-space Floquet matrix elements decay algebraically. However, it has remained unclear whether the deterministic correlations of the rotor become irrelevant at long distances and, consequently, under what conditions the two systems share the same infrared theory. To address this question, we derive a nonlocal supersymmetric nonlinear sigma model directly from a quantum kicked rotor with a power-law or logarithmic singularity. By carrying out the renormalization-group analysis up to two-loop order, we show that, after matching the symmetry class and coupling convention, the rotor reproduces the long-range Anderson transition of the corresponding PRBM, including its localized, critical, and extended infrared regimes.

</details>


### [58] [Hidden Unbounded Potential and Re-Entrant Multifractalization in a Generalized Su-Schrieffer-Heeger Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17899)
*Yun-Yan Chen,Jia-Ming Zhang,Zhi Li*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 该研究在广义Su-Schrieffer-Heeger模型中揭示了多分形临界性（multifractal criticality），发现系统中虽无显式无界势（unbounded potential），但通过将跳跃项映射为有效势可证明存在隐藏无界势，该机制驱动了多分形临界相的出现以及重入多分形化（re-entrance multifractalization, REM）现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者旨在理解无界势如何诱导多分形临界相（multifractal critical phases），并探索在广义SSH模型中实现去多分形化（demultifractalization）与重入多分形化的可能机制。

Method: 将跳跃项映射为有效势，在理论上证明模型存在隐藏无界势；通过构造显式势与隐式无界势竞争平衡的条件实现相变调控；并采用波包动力学（wave packet dynamics）对理论预测进行数值验证。

Result: 系统不仅支持临界相，还表现出重入多分形化；当显式势与隐藏无界势竞争恰好平衡时，多分形临界相消失，系统回归扩展相（extended phase）；数值结果与理论分析一致。

Conclusion: 该工作拓展了对无界势诱导多分形临界相机制的理解，为设计具有多分形临界相的新型系统提供了理论基础。

Abstract: We study the multifractal criticality in a generalized Su-Schrieffer-Heeger model. The results show that the system supports not only critical phases but also re-entrance multifractalization (REM). By mapping the hopping term to an effective potential, we analytically prove that although the model has no explicit unbounded potential, a hidden unbounded potential is actually present-this is the key mechanism driving the emergence of multifractal critical phases. Moreover, one can get a condition where the competition between the explicit and hidden unbounded potentials is exactly balanced. Under this condition, the multifractal critical phase vanish, and the system returns to the extended phase. Based on this mechanism, we achieve both demultifractalization and re-entrant multifractalization. Finally, we double check the theoretical predictions through wave packet dynamics, and the numerical results are consistent with our theoretical analysis. This work broadens our understanding of how unbounded potentials induce multifractal critical phases, providing a theoretical basis for designing new systems with multifractal critical phases.

</details>


### [59] [Dephasing-induced distinct mobility edges in a dimerized off-diagonal quasicrystal](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17937)
*Ming-Jie Tao,Yi-Ting Wang,Jing Li,Hongsheng Hou,Xiang-Ping Jiang,Lei Pan*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 本文研究了外加纯退相干（pure dephasing）作用下二聚化非对角 Aubry-André-Harper（AAH）准晶中的局域化行为，证明纯退相干可以在原本所有本征态均离域或多分形的闭合系统中诱导出流动性边缘（mobility edge, ME），包括区分扩展态与局域态的常规 ME 以及区分多分形临界态与局域态的异常 ME。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统理论认为退相干和退相位会破坏局域化并促进输运，但本文质疑这一直觉，旨在探索耗散系统（dissipative system）中纯退相干能否反向诱导局域化转变并产生流动性边缘。

Method: 在强退相干极限下，作者在 Lindblad 主方程框架中应用绝热消除（adiabatic elimination），推导出控制耗散弛豫动力学的有效经典马尔可夫转移矩阵；随后利用分形维数有限尺寸标度、波包扩展动力学和能量谱统计等多种数值方法验证计算结果。

Result: 数值结果证实耗散系统的弛豫谱中同时存在完全扩展态、多分形临界态和局域态三个区域，并构建了全局耗散相图（dissipative phase diagram）。

Conclusion: 退相干可以被视为控制局域化转变的有力机制，这一发现深化了对耗散准晶系统的理解。

Abstract: Anderson localization and the mobility edge (ME) have been extensively studied in isolated aperiodic systems. Conventional theory suggests that dephasing and decoherence should disrupt localization and facilitate transport. In this work, we investigate localization behaviors in a dimerized off-diagonal Aubry-Andre-Harper (AAH) quasicrystal subject to on-site pure dephasing. In the strong-dephasing limit, we apply adiabatic elimination within the Lindblad master equation framework to derive an effective classical Markov transition matrix that governs the dissipative relaxation dynamics. Counterintuitively, we demonstrate that pure dephasing can induce distinct MEs, including both conventional MEs separating extended and localized states and anomalous MEs separating multifractal critical states from localized states, even when all eigenstates of the original closed coherent system are delocalized or multifractal. Using fractal dimension finite-size scaling, wave-packet spreading dynamics, and energy spectrum statistics, we numerically verify the coexistence of fully extended, multifractal critical, and localized regions within the relaxation spectrum of the dissipative system, and construct the global dissipative phase diagram. These findings reveal that dephasing can see as a powerful mechanism for controlling localization transitions, thus enhancing our understanding of dissipative quasicrystal systems.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>

Conclusion: 本文系统地建立了交替伽马函数的乘积理论，涵盖单重与多重情形下的恒等式结构、特殊值求值及算术性质，并展示了交替版本与经典理论之间的深度对应关系，为其进一步应用奠定基础。

Abstract: Let $\tildeΓ(x)$ denote the modified gamma function recently introduced by the authors as an alternating analogue of the classical Euler gamma function. In this paper, we establish a complete alternating generalization of Mizuno's celebrated product formula: \begin{equation*} \prod_{m=0}^{\infty}\left(\prod_{j=1}^{n}(m+x_{j})^{(-1)^{m}}\right) =\frac{\left(\sqrt{\fracπ{2}}\right)^n}{\prod_{j=1}^{n}\tildeΓ(x_{j})} =\prod_{j=1}^{n}\left(\prod_{m=0}^{\infty}(m+x_{j})^{(-1)^{m}}\right). \end{equation*} This identity, which we refer to as the alternating Mizuno formula, replaces the classical gamma function $Γ$ and the constant $\sqrt{2π}$ by their natural alternating counterparts $\tildeΓ$ and $\sqrt{π/2}$. As immediate consequences, we recover the alternating Lerch formula and, by specializing to $x=1$, a remarkably concise derivation of Wallis' famous product \begin{equation*} \frac{2\cdot2}{1\cdot3}\cdot\frac{4\cdot4}{3\cdot5}\cdot\frac{6\cdot6}{5\cdot7}\cdot\cdots=\fracπ{2}. \end{equation*} More generally, by exploiting polynomial factorizations, we derive Kurokawa--Wakayama type formulas for alternating products over cyclotomic fields. Beyond these product identities, we investigate the multiple alternating gamma functions $\barΓ_N(x)$, obtaining explicit factorizations in terms of Barnes' multiple gamma functions, closed-form evaluations involving the Glaisher--Kinkelin constant, and a Gauss--Legendre type multiplication formula. Finally, we introduce an alternating analogue of Shintani's double sine function and establish a clean arithmetic dichotomy: for algebraic $τ$, its special values at integer points are algebraic precisely when $τ$ is rational, and transcendental otherwise.

</details>

Conclusion: 该研究提供了一条通用且实验上可实现的路径，用于直接测量此前无法触及的手性轨道磁矩，适用于当前多种前沿自旋量子平台，并为表征具有非平凡手性的量子态开辟了新方向。

Abstract: Quantum states with finite scalar spin chirality carry an orbital magnetic moment that has so far eluded direct measurement. We show that, whereas uniform electron spin resonance preserves chirality, a local drive breaks the cyclic symmetry and activates chirality-changing transitions, providing direct access to the chiral orbital magnetic moment. Lindblad simulations demonstrate experimentally accessible signatures in platforms with local spin addressability, including electron spin resonance with a scanning tunneling microscope, donor spins in silicon, and quantum-dot spin qubits.

</details>
