*Amaury Baret,Ngoc Duy Nguyen*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文提出金属纳米线网络（metallic nanowire networks）的热红外发射率（thermal infrared emissivity）可由单一局部几何长度——平均线间间隙（inter-wire gap）g——通过几何电感（geometric inductance）统一解释，该无参数（parameter-free）电抗项压过欧姆损耗（ohmic loss），并在渗流阈值（percolation threshold）处保持有限而非发散。仅用单一拟合参数即可复现56个银纳米线样品（四种直径）的发射率数据（平均绝对误差0.04），并预测五个独立实验组的发射率-透射率数据及光谱发射率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 金属纳米线网络的发射率呈现出三条长期缺乏物理解释的规律：强线径依赖且无线长依赖、与方块电阻（sheet resistance）的关联在约15 ohm/sq以上失效、随网络致密化由介电型向金属型转变的角向特征。作者旨在寻找一个统一机制来解释这些看似矛盾的实验规律。

Method: 作者证明上述所有规律均源自一个局部几何长度——平均线间间隙g——通过远小于热波长（thermal wavelength）的网格的几何电感起作用，其表达式为 X_L = Z_0 (g/λ) ln(2g/πD)。该电抗不含材料常数，且与直流方块电阻不同，在渗流阈值处保持有限而不是发散；由于间隙仅由面积密度（areal density）和线径决定，光学性质与线长无关并脱离渗流输运（percolative transport）。

Result: 单一拟合参数下，理论复现56个银纳米线样品（四种直径）的平均绝对误差为0.04，并将所有数据塌缩到一条普适曲线上；该理论无需调整即可预测五个独立实验组的发射率-透射率数据及实测光谱发射率（包括二氧化硅声子带（silica phonon band））。网络致密化时观察到的从介电型到金属型的角向转变实为平滑的阻抗交叉（impedance crossing），而非第二次光学渗流。

Conclusion: 由于该电抗不包含任何材料常数，线间间隙成为金属纳米线透明导体辐射性质的主设计变量（master design variable）。该理论为发射率与传输之间长期脱节的观察提供了统一物理解释，并明确了可工程调控的核心几何参数。

Abstract: The thermal infrared emissivity of metallic nanowire networks - the property that makes them candidate low-emissivity transparent electrodes - follows regularities that have resisted physical explanation: a strong wire-diameter dependence with no wire-length dependence, a correlation with sheet resistance that breaks down above ~15 ohm/sq, and an angular signature that turns from dielectric-like to metallic as the network densifies. We show that all of them follow from one local geometric length, the average inter-wire gap g, acting through the geometric inductance of a mesh finer than the thermal wavelength: $X_L = Z_0 (g/λ) \ln(2g/πD)$, a parameter-free reactance that exceeds the ohmic loss and, unlike the DC sheet resistance, stays finite instead of diverging at the percolation threshold. Since the gap is fixed by areal density and wire diameter alone, the optics is length-independent and decoupled from percolative transport; what has been read as a second, optical percolation is instead a smooth impedance crossing. With a single fitted parameter the theory reproduces 56 silver-nanowire samples across four diameters to a mean absolute error of 0.04, collapses them onto one universal curve, and - unchanged - predicts the emissivity-transmittance data of five independent groups and the measured spectral emissivity, including the silica phonon band. Because this reactance contains no material constant, the inter-wire gap emerges as the master design variable for the radiative properties of metallic nanowire transparent conductors.

</details>


### [64] [Flat band and Bulk-Boundary correspondence in a non-Hermitian trimerized lattice model with generic boundary conditions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17428)
*Supriyo Ghosh,Pijush K. Ghosh,Shreekantha Sil*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文研究了一类具有次近邻相互作用（next-nearest-neighbor interaction）和平衡损耗-增益（balanced loss-gain）的Su-Schrieffer-Heeger（SSH）型三聚体模型，系统考察了晶格对称性、拓扑、非厄米性（non-hermiticity）及一般边界条件（general boundary conditions）对平带存在性和体-边界对应关系（bulk-boundary correspondence）的联合影响，并解析地建立了各类边界条件下的拓扑相变与边缘态性质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索非厄米拓扑系统中的平带物理与体-边界对应关系，是当前凝聚态物理与光子学领域的重要前沿问题。该工作旨在综合理解晶格对称性、拓扑结构、非厄米效应与一般边界条件之间的耦合，填补在多体相互作用及非厄米框架下平带与拓扑边界态系统性研究的空白。

Method: 在周期边界条件（periodic boundary condition）下解析推导了实谱存在与否的充要条件，以及平带对应的紧致局域态（compact localized states）和能量本征值的精确表达式；利用Zak相位和子格Zak相位分别刻画PT对称与伪手征对称（pseudo-chiral symmetry）诱导的拓扑相变；在开边界条件（open boundary condition）下通过数值计算研究边缘态；推广已有形式化方法处理一般边界条件，并在允许平带的参数区间解析求解能量与本征态；基于谱拓扑解析计算绕数（winding number）。

Result: 在周期边界条件下，模型在特定参数范围内存在完全实能谱及精确可解的平带紧致局域态。通过Zak相位和子格Zak相位分别建立了PT对称和伪手征对称下的拓扑相变。开边界条件下在拓扑非平庸相中观察到边缘态，确认了非厄米体-边界对应的存在。研究发现，仅具有伪手征对称的系统在体相和边界均存在紧致局域态，而额外的PT对称会破坏边界处的紧致局域态。在一般边界条件下，解析获得了拓扑非平庸相中的边缘态，建立了体-边界对应；同时，在具有互易体相互作用和强非互易边界项的模型中观察到了非厄米趋肤效应（non-Hermitian skin effect），并解析计算了基于谱拓扑的绕数。

Conclusion: 该模型系统地展示了在非厄米与一般边界条件下，平带紧致局域态的存活与否取决于对称性类型（伪手征对称单独存在或与PT对称共存），且在拓扑非平庸相中边缘态的解析存在性证实了广义体-边界对应。非互易边界项可诱导非厄米趋肤效应，拓展了对非厄米拓扑系统的理解。

Abstract: We consider a Su-Schrieffer-Heeger(SSH)-type trimer model with next-nearest-neighbor(NNN) interaction and balanced loss-gain(BLG) to study the combined effect of lattice symmetries, topology, non-hermiticity and general boundary conditions(GBC)on the existence of flat band and the nature of Bulk-Boundary correspondence(BBC). We derive the necessary and sufficient conditions for the existence of an entirely real spectrum under the periodic boundary condition(PBC). The exact expressions for the compact localized states(CLS) and energy eigenvalues corresponding to flat bands are derived analytically under the PBC. We establish topological phase transitions(TPT) for PT-symmetry and pseudo-chiral symmetry through the computation of the Zak phase and sub-lattice Zak phase, respectively. The Hamiltonian under the open boundary condition(OBC) is studied numerically, and edge states are observed in the topologically non-trivial phase, thereby establishing the non-hermitian BBC. The CLS exists in both bulk and the boundary for systems having only pseudo-chiral symmetry, and an additional PT-symmetry destroys the CLS at the boundary. We generalize a known formalism to study the same Hamiltonian under GBC, and derive analytic expressions for the energy and eigenstates for a class of boundary conditions in parametric ranges which admit flat band under the PBC. The edge states for these boundary conditions, including the OBC, are obtained analytically in the topologically non-trivial phase, thereby establishing BBC. The non-hermitian skin effect(NHSE) is seen in the model with reciprocal bulk interaction and strongly non-reciprocal boundary terms. The winding number based on spectral topology is computed analytically.

</details>


### [65] [Zero Point Density Fluctuations and Electron Brownian Motion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17230)
*L. H. Ford,G. O. Heymans,N. F. Svaiter*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文研究了声子真空态（phonon vacuum state）涨落导致的材料介电性质局域零点涨落，进而对材料外部测试电荷（如电子）产生涨落力并引发其布朗运动。作者计算了电子在法向和切向的平均速度平方，发现两方向均非零且法向更大，某些情况下该效应可超过热运动和量子动量不确定度的影响，可能成为纳米器件中的量子噪声来源，并可用于远程探测材料中的零点密度涨落。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索声子真空涨落如何通过材料的零点密度涨落产生局域介电性质涨落，进而对材料外部电子产生可观测的力学效应。这一机制可能构成纳米尺度器件中未被充分认识的量子噪声来源，并可能为远程探测材料零点涨落提供新的物理途径。

Method: 提出物理论证：材料中的零点密度涨落引发局域偶极矩涨落，从而对材料外部短距离处的测试电荷产生涨落力。基于此物理图像，计算电子在垂直于材料边界（法向）和平行于边界（横向）两个方向上的均方速度。

Result: 计算结果在两个方向均非零，但法向分量大于横向分量。数量级估算表明，在某些情况下该量子布朗运动（quantum Brownian motion）效应可超过热运动和量子动量不确定度的影响。

Conclusion: 该效应可能可被观测，并可能构成纳米器件中的量子噪声来源；同时，它也可能提供一种远程传感材料中零点密度涨落的潜在手段。

Abstract: The fluctuations in the phonon vacuum state can lead to zero point density fluctuations in a material, which in turn lead to local zero point fluctuations of the dielectric properties of the material. We argue that the density fluctuations lead to a fluctuating force on a test charge, such as an electron, located a short distance outside of the material. This force is due to fluctuating dipole moments inside the material, and produces Brownian motion of the electron. We calculate the mean squared velocity of the electron in both the normal and transverse direction relative to the boundary of the material. The result is nonzero in both directions, but larger in the normal case. We estimate the magnitude of this quantum Brownian motion and find that, in some cases, it can exceed the effects of both thermal motion and quantum momentum uncertainty. This suggests that the effect may be observable, and could constitute a source of quantum noise in nanoscale devices. It also potentially offers a means to remotely sense zero point density fluctuations in a material.

</details>


### [66] [Flux-tunable global and local superconductivity in a topological insulator nano-SQUID](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17404)
*Ella Nikodem,Jakob Schluck,Michał Papaj,Max Geier,Mahasweta Bagchi,Liang Fu,Henry F. Legg,Yoichi Ando*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文使用柱状纳米超导量子干涉仪（nano-SQUID）架构重新研究体绝缘三维拓扑绝缘体（3D TIs），同时探测全局超导性质（通过临界电流）和局部末端态（通过隧道结）。观测到与进入拓扑区域一致的全局超导特征（周期性临界电流振荡、超导二极管效应符号反转）及局域/非局域电导谱跳跃；但零偏压峰（ZBP）在拓扑预测区和平庸区均存在，最终结论是不能仅凭ZBP确认马约拉纳束缚态（Majorana bound states），需结合全局拓扑性质和多手段综合探测。

  <summary>Details</summary>

Abstract: We study the dissipative dynamics of a Majorana tetron qubit in the presence of extrinsic quasiparticle poisoning due to the coupling to external leads. The analysis shows that the exponential suppression of the dephasing rate is gradually removed by the energy splitting of the qubit states. These results can be useful to understand time-domain experimental data in Majorana qubit prototypes.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 相互作用纳米结构耦合费米子库的实时模拟需要越来越多的环境自由度来保留长时关联，这成为实际模拟的瓶颈。传统方法（如指数分解的HEOM）在代数衰减关联的平带库情形下无法收敛，亟需一种能高效处理长时关联的新模拟策略。

Abstract: Real-time simulations of interacting nanostructures coupled to fermionic reservoirs can require a growing number of environmental degrees of freedom to retain long-lived correlations. For each outgoing-mode truncation, we derive a nonperturbative upper bound on the infidelity between the exact and truncated full device--reservoir states over any prescribed finite interval. The bound depends on the mode's remaining coupling weight and finite-interval response factors. Numerically, the active-mode count saturates in time at fixed relative threshold and grows logarithmically as the threshold is reduced. We benchmark the method on a two-site quantum point contact at zero temperature and maximal bias. For Lorentzian reservoirs, the dynamics agrees with converged HEOM calculations and the steady-state current with the Landauer--Büttiker result. For flat-band reservoirs with algebraically decaying correlations, it agrees with direct Schrödinger evolution before finite-size recurrences and reproduces the Landauer--Büttiker stationary current, while finite exponential HEOM decompositions remain unconverged. For interacting contacts, the method yields Coulomb-blockade peak splitting. In the noninteracting driven limit, it agrees with an exact Floquet Green-function calculation and reproduces coherent current suppression under periodic driving, which persists at finite Coulomb repulsion. Together, these benchmarks show that tape-recorder coarse graining enables practical long-time simulations of the full device--reservoir state in interacting fermionic transport.

  <summary>Details</summary>

Abstract: Large twist angles in twisted bilayer graphene are widely expected to be electronically trivial, with negligible interlayer coupling and no electronic reconstruction, in contrast to the rich moiré-driven band reconstruction and correlated physics that emerge at small twist angles. Here, we show that this paradigm breaks down near a twist angle of 29°, where the system crosses over between quasicrystalline and commensurate order. Atomic-resolution transmission electron microscopy directly reveals the coexistence of near-dodecagonal quasicrystalline symmetry and emerging moiré periodicity, indicating an intermediate, nonperiodic structural regime. Magnetotransport measurements uncover strong interlayer hybridization mediated by Umklapp scattering, manifested by magneto-intersubband oscillations and a highly unconventional Landau-level spectrum. Remarkably, the Landau-level degeneracy evolves from 4- to 12-fold with increasing temperature, a behavior incompatible with two decoupled graphene monolayers. These findings establish large-angle twisted bilayer graphene as a platform where quasiperiodic symmetry fundamentally reshapes low-energy electronic states beyond the conventional moiré framework.

Zhao,Y. Zhao,A. Zhelezov,S. Z. Zheng,X. Z. Zheng,Y. Zheng,T. Zhou,X. Zhou,V. Zhovkovska,L. Z. Zhu,X. Zhu,X. Zhu,Y. Zhu,V. Zhukov,J. Zhuo,D. Zuliani,G. Zunica*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 利用LHCb实验记录的5.4 fb⁻¹质子-质子（pp）碰撞数据，搜索B介子衰变至含四个或六个μ子末态的过程。未发现任何信号模式，在95%置信水平下设定分支比上限为0.6×10⁻⁹至5.4×10⁻⁷，并给出中间粒子寿命范围内的质量积分上限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: B介子衰变至多μ子末态可经由中间轻子或长寿命粒子产生，此类过程对超出标准模型（beyond Standard Model）的新物理信号敏感，是对稀有衰变分支比进行严格约束的重要途径。

Method: 基于LHCb实验记录的pp碰撞数据（积分亮度5.4 fb⁻¹），对四种目标衰变模式进行搜索，包括中间粒子同时为瞬发（prompt）和长寿命（long-lived）两种情况。

Result: 未发现任何信号模式的证据，在95%置信水平下设定分支比上限，范围从0.6×10⁻⁹到5.4×10⁻⁷，具体界限取决于中间粒子的质量与寿命。此外还确定了覆盖所考虑中间粒子寿命范围的质量积分上限。

Conclusion: 研究结果对B介子多μ子稀有衰变通道的分支比施加了严格实验约束，为相关新物理场景提供了更强的排他性限制。

Abstract: A search for decays of $B$ mesons to final states with four or six muons using $pp$ collision data recorded by the LHCb experiment corresponding to an integrated luminosity of $5.4~\text{fb}^{-1}$ is presented. The decay modes of interest are $B_{(s)}^0 \rightarrow μ^+μ^-μ^+μ^-$, $B^+ \rightarrow K^+μ^+μ^-μ^+μ^-$, $B_{(s)}^0 \rightarrow μ^+μ^-μ^+μ^-μ^+μ^-$ and $B^+ \rightarrow K^+μ^+μ^-μ^+μ^-μ^+μ^-$, proceeding via both prompt and long-lived intermediate particles. No evidence for any of the signal modes is found, and upper limits spanning the range of $0.6\times10^{-9}$ to $5.4\times10^{-7}$ at the $95\%$ confidence level are set on their branching fractions, depending on the intermediate-particle masses and lifetimes. In addition, mass-integrated limits across the intermediate-particle lifetime ranges considered in this analysis are determined.

</details>


<div id='math-ph'></div>

# math-ph [[Back]](#toc)

### [74] [A Projected Semiexplicit Integrator for Dissipative Systems with Configuration-Dependent Kinetic Energy: Contact-Herglotz Formulation and Benchmarks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17198)
*Lorena Loera-Galeana,Santiago Mejía,Espartaco Alvarado,Héctor Medel-Cobaxin*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文针对具有动量交叉项的不可分离接触哈密顿系统，提出一种投影Pihajoki-接触积分器（projected Pihajoki-contact integrator），通过相空间复制、对称投影和恒定摩擦阻尼半步结合，在稠密度量的双摆等系统中实现二阶精度，并精确复现接触衰变律，优于Tao基线和未投影平均方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 接触哈密顿动力学（contact Hamiltonian dynamics）为耗散力学提供了固有的作用量变量，但显式接触分裂仅适用于动能项可精确积分的冻结坐标对角度量（如球摆、环面粒子），而带有动量交叉项的稠密度量（如双摆）无法用现有分裂方法处理。

Method: 提出投影Pihajoki-接触积分器，结合相空间复制（phase-space duplication）、对称投影到物理对角线、恒定摩擦阻尼半步，以及由精确Herglotz更新承载作用量因子。构造无需绑定参数，每步将复制体投影回对角线，非线性求解仅限于2n个投影变量。恒定摩擦下，当投影精确求解时步长重缩放因子为精确的e^{-γτ}（经典共形辛恒等式）。

Result: 方法在阻尼双摆、球摆和环面粒子上具有二阶精度，复现接触衰变律，并在粗糙或刚硬区域中控制长时间能量和接触漂移，而Tao基线和未投影平均方法在此类区域失效。与精确接触同胚分裂的对比表明：当存在冻结坐标分裂时后者精确保持接触形式且以匹配成本胜出；对于稠密双摆度量，可实现备选方案仅为一阶且常数过大，投影方法占优。

Conclusion: 接触形式误差估计是局部的、单步的且针对恒定摩擦。时间对称性、一致性和光滑性产生O(τ³)的单步接触形式残差，该界不特定于接触形式。该方法填补了不可分离非接触哈密顿系统高精度积分器的空白。

Abstract: Contact Hamiltonian dynamics gives dissipative mechanics an intrinsic action variable, but explicit contact splittings reach only kinetic energies whose terms are exactly integrable: frozen-coordinate diagonal metrics (the spherical pendulum, a torus particle) are included, while dense metrics with momentum cross terms, with the double pendulum as flagship, are not. We introduce a projected Pihajoki-contact integrator for this non-separable setting, combining phase-space duplication, symmetric projection onto the physical diagonal, and constant-friction damping half-steps, with the action factor carried by an exact Herglotz update. As in the projected extended-phase-space framework it builds on, the construction needs no binding parameter, returns the copies to the diagonal at every step, and confines the nonlinear solve to the $2n$ projection variables. For constant friction the step rescales $ω=dη$ by the exact factor $e^{-γτ}$ when the projection is solved exactly (a classical conformally symplectic identity, realized here for this class), while time-symmetry, consistency, and smoothness yield an $O(τ^3)$ one-step contact-form residual, a bound not specific to the contact form. On the damped double pendulum, spherical pendulum, and torus particle the method is second-order accurate, reproduces the contact decay law, and controls long-time energy and contact drift in coarse or stiff regimes where the Tao baseline and the unprojected average lose the solution. A head-to-head with exact-contactomorphism splittings delimits the niche: where a frozen-coordinate splitting exists it preserves the contact form exactly and wins at matched cost; for the dense double-pendulum metric the realizable alternative is first-order with a prohibitive constant and the projected method prevails. The contact-form estimate is local, one-step, and constant-friction.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解和刻画Romanovski多项式中度数依赖参数（degree dependent parameters）情形下的代数结构，为这类在量子力学等应用中出现的特殊函数族提供显式的升降算子构造，并阐明其在$(n,\ell)$格点上的ladder关系与李代数表示。

Method: 作者对参数$\beta_K=-K$、$\alpha_K=c/(K+1)$的首一Romanovski多项式，在固定$(\alpha,\beta)$下显式给出参数保持的（parameter preserving）一阶降和升关系，比例常数为实数；通过组合这些关系恢复超几何型族中标准的三项递推，重复迭代则从单位常数多项式出发有序地构造$R_n^{\alpha,\beta}$。进一步解决了与$\alpha=0$族的有限三角连接问题，并将该族识别为Gegenbauer多项式，随后将ladder关系转移至$(n,\ell)$格点上，并作代换$x=\cot\chi$分析算子结构。

Result: 得到了显式的一阶降升关系及由单位多项式迭代构造$R_n^{\alpha,\beta}$的有序方案；连接问题以有限三角形式求解，$\alpha=0$族被识别为Gegenbauer多项式。在代换$x=\cot\chi$后，层内（in-level）算子仅依赖$\ell$而不依赖$K$；得到的dressed函数沿固定$\ell$的列支持内在最低权$\mathrm{su}(1,1)$模，而圆周行（$n=0$）需要显式边界处方（boundary prescription）与重标度（rescaling）处理。

Conclusion: 研究将Romanovski多项式的升降算子构造与表示论联系起来，揭示了其沿$(n,\ell)$格点列结构上的$\mathrm{su}(1,1)$最低权模解释，并指出圆周行的边界特殊性需要特别处理，这为理解度数依赖参数多项式族的代数和表示性质提供了系统框架。

Abstract: We study the monic Romanovski (pseudo-Jacobi) polynomials corresponding to the degree dependent parameters $β_K=-K$, $α_K=c/(K+1)$, with $K=n+\ell$. We write down, in explicit form, the parameter preserving first order lowering and raising relations at fixed $(α,β)$, with real proportionality constants. Combining them one recovers the standard three-term recurrence existing in any hypergeometric-type family, and iterating them one gets an ordered first order construction of $R_n^{α,β}$ starting from the unit constant polynomial. We also solve the connection problem with the $α=0$ family in a finite triangular form, identifying this last family with the Gegenbauer polynomials, and transferring the ladder relations to the $(n,\ell)$ lattice. After the substitution $x=\cotχ$, the in-level operators depend on $\ell$, but not on $K$. The resulting dressed functions support intrinsic lowest weight $\mathrm{su}(1,1)$ modules along the columns with $\ell$ fixed, while the circular row ($n=0$) requires an explicit boundary prescription and a rescaling.

Conclusion: 该工作将Dyckerhoff--Kapranov关于五边形方程的经典结论推广到所有多边形方程情形，为多边形方程的研究提供了同调代数和范畴论框架。

Abstract: Polygon equations form a family of equations generalizing the pentagon equation. In this paper, we construct semi-simplicial sets of permitted colorings associated with set-theoretic solutions of polygon equations and use them to define the corresponding (co)homology groups. We investigate several properties of these groups and establish an equivalence of categories between set-theoretic solutions of polygon equations and higher Segal semi-simplicial sets satisfying certain conditions. As a special case, our result recovers the correspondence between bijective set-theoretic solutions of the pentagon equation and $2$-Segal semi-simplicial sets proved by Dyckerhoff--Kapranov.

### [77] [Tracking nonlinear solar-wind dynamics over three solar cycles using Wind observations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17037)
*Dario Javier Zamora,Facundo Maximo Abaca*

Main category: astro-ph.SR

TL;DR: 本研究利用Wind飞船1995–2025年的逐时质子密度数据，在一年滑动窗口内估计非广延q-三重态的三个分量，证实了太阳风湍流具有重尾统计、慢弛豫和弱混沌多重分形动力学的持久特征，并揭示了这些参数与太阳活动周期间的时变关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 太阳风是典型的湍流弱碰撞等离子体，表现出非高斯涨落、长程关联和多重分形动力学，但其长期演化特征尚未被充分检验。此前结论主要来自多飞船OMNI数据库，需用单飞船独立数据集进行验证。

Method: 使用Wind飞船1995–2025年的逐时质子密度数据，在月度滑移的一年窗口内估计非广延三重态的三个分量：qstat由密度增量分布得到，qrel由自相关函数衰减得到，qsens由多重分形谱得到。进一步对参数时间序列进行傅里叶分析、与太阳活动代理量的相关性与互信息分析。

Result: 三个分量的均值为qstat=1.71±0.07, qrel=4.62±0.52, qsens=−0.45±0.27，确认了重尾统计、慢弛豫与弱混沌多重分形动力学的持续存在。qstat的傅里叶谱呈现约10.1年的主导周期，与太阳黑子数呈正相关（r=0.611）；qstat主要与太阳代理量关联，qsens与地磁指数关联更强，qrel依赖较弱。2004年前后qstat的异常增强与强行星际扰动序列重合。

Conclusion: 这些发现证明了非广延描述在描述太阳风质子密度统计与动力学性质方面的稳健性，并表明太阳风的统计和动力学特性受到太阳活动的显著调制。

Abstract: The solar wind is a turbulent, weakly collisional plasma characterized by non-Gaussian fluctuations, long-range correlations, and multifractal dynamics. We investigate the long-term evolution of these properties using hourly proton density measurements obtained directly by the Wind spacecraft over 1995--2025. The use of a single-spacecraft dataset provides an independent test of previous results derived from the multi-spacecraft OMNI database. The three components of the nonextensive $q$-triplet are estimated within one-year sliding windows shifted monthly: $q_{stat}$ from the distribution of density increments, $q_{rel}$ from the decay of the autocorrelation function, and $q_{sens}$ from the multifractal spectrum. Their mean values, $q_{stat}=1.71\pm0.07$, $q_{rel}=4.62\pm0.52$, and $q_{sens}=-0.45\pm0.27$, confirm the persistent presence of heavy-tailed statistics, slow relaxation, and weakly chaotic multifractal dynamics. The parameters nevertheless exhibit distinct temporal variability and relationships with solar activity. The Fourier spectrum of $q_{stat}$ contains a dominant period of approximately $10.1$ years, while its correlation with the sunspot number is positive and moderate ($r=0.611$). Correlation and mutual-information analyses show that $q_{stat}$ is primarily associated with solar proxies, whereas $q_{sens}$ displays stronger relationships with geomagnetic indices and $q_{rel}$ exhibits weaker dependencies. An anomalous enhancement of $q_{stat}$ around 2004 coincides with a sequence of intense interplanetary disturbances, although possible effects related to Wind's orbital transition must also be considered. These findings demonstrate the robustness of the nonextensive description and show that the statistical and dynamical properties of solar wind proton density show evidence of modulation by solar activity.

Result: 在95%置信水平下，对自耦合修正参数获得的联合约束区间为 $-10.3<c_3<11.2$ 和 $-222<d_4<234$，其中 $c_3=\kappa_3-1$、$d_4=\kappa_4-1$。同时发现四希格斯玻色子产生过程可以进入要求至少六个b-标记喷注（b-tagged jets）的三希格斯玻色子信号区域。对单实单态和双实单态扩展模型，分别获得了以新标量质量为函数的四希格斯玻色子产生截面预期上限（expected upper limits）。

Conclusion: 在高亮度LHC上，通过八-b-喷注末态的多变量分析，四希格斯玻色子产生过程可以在95%置信水平同时约束三重与四重希格斯自耦合修正。标准模型的标量扇区扩展（单实单态与双实单态模型）为四希格斯产生提供了新的共振产生渠道，并可以给出有意义的截面上限约束。

Abstract: We investigate the production of four Higgs bosons via gluon fusion at the high-luminosity LHC. We construct a phenomenological analysis of the eight-$b$-jet final state and use a multivariate analysis to derive expected simultaneous constraints on the triple and quartic Higgs boson self-coupling modifications. At 95% confidence level, the resulting region projects onto $-10.3<c_3<11.2$ and $-222<d_4<234$, where $c_3=κ_3-1$ and $d_4=κ_4-1$. We also examine how quadruple-Higgs boson production can enter a triple-Higgs boson signal region requiring at least six $b$-tagged jets.
  In addition, we consider two extensions of the Standard Model scalar sector: a one-real-singlet extension yielding the direct resonant process $pp\rightarrow S\rightarrow hhhh$, and a two-real-singlet extension yielding the cascade $pp\rightarrow h_3\rightarrow h_2h_2\rightarrow (hh)(hh)$, where $S$, $h_2$ and $h_3$ are new scalar resonances. For these processes, we derive expected upper limits on the quadruple-Higgs boson production cross sections as functions of the new scalar masses.

TL;DR: 本文研究了非极化质子-质子（pp）碰撞中背对背胶子对的极化关联，计算了其密度矩阵（density matrix），并从能量关联子的各向异性中提取线性极化关联。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 胶子对（gluon pair）的量子关联结构是高能强相互作用中尚未充分探索的物理量，其是否处于量子纠缠态（entangled state）对于理解QCD中量子信息特性具有重要意义。

Method: 在非极化pp碰撞中，分别对q̄q湮灭产生胶子对和gg→gg散射两个通道计算胶子对的密度矩阵，并通过能量关联子（energy correlators）的各向异性提取cos2ΔΦ调制。

Result: 在q̄q湮灭通道中，当末态胶子垂直于束流方向发射时，线性极化关联可达1；结合螺旋度（helicity）在散射角变化下保持最大关联，胶子对处于最大纠缠Bell态（maximally entangled Bell state）。而gg→gg通道的螺旋度和线性极化关联均被显著抑制，产生可分离态（separable state），但在中心散射处仍保有约11%的线性极化关联。

Conclusion: 胶子对的线性极化关联可通过对能量关联子各向异性的测量，从cos2ΔΦ调制中被提取出来，为实验检验胶子对的量子关联结构提供了可观测途径。

Abstract: We investigate the polarization correlation of back-to-back gluon pairs in unpolarized $pp$ collisions and compute their density matrix. For gluon pairs produced through $q\bar{q}$ annihilation, the linear polarization correlation is sizable, even reaching unity when the final-state gluons are emitted perpendicular to the beam direction in the $q\bar{q}$ rest frame. Combining with the fact that their helicity remains maximally correlated across different scattering angle, the gluon pair system thus resides in a maximally entangled Bell state in this configuration. In contrast, for the $gg\to gg$ channel, both the helicity and linear polarization correlations are considerably suppressed, producing a separable state. Nonetheless, it still retains an appreciable linear polarization correlation of about $11\%$ at central scattering. Employing the anisotropy of energy correlators, we demonstrate that the linear polarization correlation of gluon pairs can be extracted from the $\cos 2ΔΦ$ modulation.

</details>


### [80] [VERaiPHY -- Validation & Evaluation for Robust AI in PHYsics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17724)
*Gaia Grosso,Ramon Winterhalder,Lydia Brenner,Louis Lyons,Tilman Plehn*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文是VERaiPHY系列论文的开篇之作，旨在为机器学习（machine learning）技术在基础物理学中的应用建立统计标准，系统梳理了后续文章所依赖的概率论、统计学和机器学习基础及统一符号约定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代机器学习在基础物理学中显著提升了精度、灵活性和计算效率，但统计验证（statistical validation）、不确定性量化（uncertainty quantification）和鲁棒性评估（robustness assessment）缺乏系统性处理，需要建立统一的统计标准。

Method: 本文作为VERaiPHY系列的开篇文章，从统计学视角出发，为概率、统计和机器学习的基础概念建立统一的数学框架，并定义贯穿整个系列的符号体系，为后续各专题文章提供共同的理论前提。

Result: 本文确立了VERaiPHY系列后续论文所假设的概率论、统计学和机器学习基础，并统一了全系列的术语与记号规范。

Conclusion: 该系列文章旨在为ML技术在物理领域的开发、评估和部署建立统计标准，本文作为起点为后续各方法论域文章提供一致的概念基础。

Abstract: Modern machine learning is leading to substantial gains in precision, flexibility, and computational efficiency in fundamental physics. Statistical validation, uncertainty quantification, and robustness assessment are less systematically addressed. The VERaiPHY initiative (Validation & Evaluation for Robust AI in PHYsics) is a series of articles developed within the PHYSTAT programme, aimed at establishing statistical standards for the development, evaluation, and deployment of ML techniques. Each article focuses on a specific methodological domain from a statistics perspective and clarifies statistical questions, tests, and the interpretation of results. This opening article establishes the probabilistic, statistical, and machine learning foundations that the later contributions assume, together with the notation used throughout.

</details>

Although the absolute bound state energies depend appreciably on the cutoff parameter, the energy differences relative to the $1s$ level are considerably less sensitive to it and exhibit a regular pattern that decreases approximately as $A^{-2/3}$ with increasing nuclear mass number. A cosh-type potential with a common nuclear geometry provides a compact description of these spectra. The predicted bound-state structures and level-spacing systematics could be investigated in future high-statistics near-threshold photoproduction experiments at the upgraded JLab facility.

Abstract: The nature of the $X(3872)$ exotic hadron remains one of the most debated questions in hadron spectroscopy. We calculate its magnetic moment within a non-relativistic quark model that includes the three-body force arising from the cubic Casimir operator of $SU(3)_C$ with consistent dimensional analysis. Unlike previous works that used an uncontrolled large coupling, we fix the three-body strength using the realistic value of $\sim 10$--$20$ MeV extracted from the recent analysis of Noh et al.~(2024) based on lattice QCD and baryon spectroscopy.
  We find that the magnetic moment predictions fall into two distinct regions: the pure color-singlet configuration yields $μ_X = -0.99 \pm 0.02\,μ_N$, while the compact configurations yield $μ_X = -1.17$ to $-1.23\,μ_N$. The difference between the two compact scenarios ($0.06\,μ_N$) is comparable to the systematic uncertainty of the model ($\sim 0.15\,μ_N$) and should be interpreted with caution. However, the distinction between the pure color-singlet and the compact scenarios ($\sim 0.18$--$0.24\,μ_N$) is larger than the estimated model systematic uncertainty and may provide a qualitative structural indicator.
  We emphasize that the pure color-singlet configuration is a simplified proxy for a molecule and does not represent a physical $D^0\bar{D}^{*0}$ molecule with large spatial extent. A full molecular treatment would require coupled-channel dynamics and long-range pion-exchange potentials, which are beyond the scope of this work.
  We also resolve a long-standing dimensional inconsistency in the cubic Casimir three-body force formulation and demonstrate through a detailed sensitivity and uncertainty analysis that our conclusions are robust for the physically relevant range of the three-body coupling.

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Result: 首次从万能Lindblad框架直接推导出玻尔兹曼输运方程，其有效性超越小偶极近似；与Yao等人的方程相比，单态方程几乎完全一致，但八态方程包含描述八态散射态连续区间内跃迁的额外碰撞项；此外还获得了单态玻尔兹曼方程的领先量子修正。

Conclusion: 该工作为QGP中夸克偶素的半经典输运描述建立了更一般、更系统的理论基础，克服了此前RWA近似的局限性，能够描述重夸克对从紧致到宽分离构型的演化。

Abstract: Recently, a set of coupled singlet-octet universal Lindblad equations (ULEs) was derived within the framework of non-relativistic QCD (NRQCD) to describe quarkonium dynamics in the quark-gluon plasma (QGP). These equations provide a unified quantum description spanning the quantum Brownian and quantum optical regimes. In this work, we further develop this framework and establish its connection with semiclassical transport. We first derive the universal Lindblad equations within the potential non-relativistic QCD (pNRQCD) effective field theory and show that they coincide with the small-dipole limit of the NRQCD ULEs. We then derive the semiclassical limit of the NRQCD ULEs, obtaining a set of coupled singlet-octet Boltzmann equations. To our knowledge, this is the first derivation of Boltzmann transport equations directly from the universal Lindblad framework. The resulting equations are valid beyond the small-dipole approximation, allowing the evolution of heavy-quark pairs from compact to widely separated configurations. Taking their small-dipole limit allows a direct comparison with the Boltzmann equations of Yao et al. [Phys. Rev. D 99, 096028 (2019)], which were derived within pNRQCD from the Davies secular equation, relying on the rotating-wave approximation (RWA). While the singlet equations are found to be in near-complete agreement, the octet equation contains an additional collision term describing transitions within the continuum of octet scattering states, which is absent from the RWA-based derivation. Finally, we derive the leading quantum correction to the singlet Boltzmann equation. Our results establish a more general and systematic theoretical foundation for the semiclassical transport description of quarkonium in the QGP.

### [85] [Nonlocal Transition Kernel for Efficient Learning of Restricted Boltzmann Machines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17450)
*Kaiji Sekimoto,Muneki Yasuda*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于深度退火（deep tempering, DT）RBM序列的往返迁移核（round-trip transition kernel），能在单次迁移中实现非局部移动（nonlocal move），相比块状吉布斯采样（blocked Gibbs sampling, BGS）和深度退火，具有更高的采样质量、更稳定的训练表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受限玻尔兹曼机（restricted Boltzmann machines, RBMs）的学习依赖于对期望值的采样近似，通常使用块状吉布斯采样（blocked Gibbs sampling, BGS）。然而BGS是局部马尔可夫链蒙特卡洛迁移核，当RBM存在高能量壁垒时采样质量差、损害学习性能。深度退火（deep tempering, DT）虽能缓解局部性问题，但算法上需要多步穿越RBM序列才能实现非局部迁移，效率受限。

Method: 提出一个定义在DT所用RBM序列之上的新迁移核，该迁移核具有往返（round-trip）结构，能在单次迁移中实现非局部移动，同时保持RBM序列的不变性。

Result: 数值实验表明，所提迁移核比BGS和DT能更频繁地执行非局部迁移，并以更少的迁移步数获得更高的采样质量。基于该迁移核的学习过程更加稳定，缓解了BGS和DT学习中观察到的训练失败问题。

Conclusion: 往返结构迁移核有效克服了局部采样的局限，在采样效率和学习稳定性上均优于BGS与DT，为RBM的高效学习提供了新的采样方案。

Abstract: Learning restricted Boltzmann machines (RBMs) is computationally challenging because it requires expectations whose exact evaluation is generally intractable. The expectations are typically evaluated using a sampling approximation based on blocked Gibbs sampling (BGS), which is a local Markov chain Monte Carlo transition kernel. However, the locality of BGS can lead to poor sampling quality when the RBM has high energy barriers, thereby degrading learning performance. Deep tempering (DT), which performs parallel tempering over a sequence of learnable RBMs including the training RBM, alleviates this locality issue. However, DT algorithmically requires multiple steps to move through the RBM sequence to achieve a nonlocal transition. In this paper, we propose a transition kernel defined over the RBM sequence used in DT. The proposed kernel has a round-trip structure over the sequence, enabling nonlocal moves within a single transition while leaving the RBM sequence invariant. Numerical experiments show that the proposed kernel performs nonlocal transitions more frequently and achieves higher sampling quality with fewer transitions than BGS and DT. We further verify that learning based on the proposed kernel is more stable and mitigates the training failures observed with BGS- and DT-based learning.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 表面相变通常被认为具有二维特征，但在CoSi(001)表面观测到的电荷密度波转变却表现出三维XY普适类的临界行为，这与其名义上的二维表面身份存在矛盾，需要通过实验精确测量临界指数来理解这一反常现象的物理起源。

Method: 采用温度依赖的旋转各向异性二次谐波产生（RA-SHG）技术，监测SHG响应随温度的演化以追踪序参量（order parameter）振幅。通过本底扣除后的强度幂律拟合提取临界指数，并对比表面与体响应以确认相变的表面localization。

Result: 在CoSi(001)表面观测到起始温度为90.0±0.8 K的电荷密度波转变，SHG响应由两个非线性张量元主导，其本底扣除后强度随温度呈幂律演化，提取的临界指数β=0.30±0.03与三维XY普适类一致；体响应中未观察到对应的异常，确认该相变为纯表面驱动的相变。

Conclusion: 该表面电荷密度波转变的三维临界行为源于表面费米弧态（Fermi arc states）与所关联的体拓扑之间的耦合，先前观测到的表面子层间胞内相位关系赋予序参量内在的三维结构，从而打破了名义上二维表面层的预期普适类。

Abstract: Using temperature-dependent rotational anisotropy second harmonic generation (RA-SHG), we identify a charge density wave (CDW) instability on the CoSi (001) face with an onset temperature at $90.0 \pm 0.8$ K. The SHG response tracks the order parameter amplitude, dominated by two nonlinear tensor elements, whose background-subtracted intensity evolves with temperature as a power law. The extracted critical exponent $β= 0.30 \pm 0.03$ is consistent with the 3D XY universality class, an unexpected result for a nominally two-dimensional surface layer. No corresponding anomaly is observed in the bulk response, establishing the transition as a purely surface-driven phase transition. We attribute this unexpected scaling to the coupling between surface Fermi-arc states and the bulk topology they are tied to, grounded in a previously observed intra-unit-cell phase relationship between surface sublayers that gives the order parameter intrinsic three-dimensional structure.

</details>

*Shunsuke Sengoku,Haruki Watanabe*

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前的研究尚未覆盖一类特定自旋-1链模型，其中部分四极耦合（quadrupolar couplings）为零。理解这类系统中局域守恒量的存在性是判定连续对称性自发破缺机制的关键问题。

Method: 应用Shiraishi技术（Shiraishi's technique）到这些系统，对周期链上的任意N个格点，证明对任何整数k（满足3≤k≤N/2）不存在k-局域守恒量（k-local conserved quantity）。

Result: 证明了一维空间中零温自发U(1)对称性破缺的无阻挫（frustration-free）自旋-1链，所有支撑范围不超过系统一半的局域守恒量仅为单位算符、总磁化强度Sz和哈密顿量本身的线性组合。此外，还证明了周期Motzkin链中也不存在3≤k≤N/2的k-局域守恒量。

Conclusion: 与海森堡铁磁体（Heisenberg ferromagnet）不同，该模型不存在与哈密顿量对易的局域序参量，其连续对称性破缺是由无阻挫结构所驱动而非守恒序参量所决定。

Abstract: We prove the absence of nontrivial local conserved quantities in a class of $U(1)$-symmetric spin-$1$ chains with nearest-neighbor interactions in which some of the quadrupolar couplings vanish, a class that is not covered by previous studies. Applying the technique of Shiraishi to these systems, we show that, for every model in this class on a periodic chain of $N$ sites, there is no $k$-local conserved quantity for any $3\le k\le N/2$. In particular, for a frustration-free spin-$1$ chain that exhibits spontaneous $U(1)$ symmetry breaking at zero temperature in one spatial dimension, we prove that every local conserved quantity with support up to half of the system size is a linear combination of the identity, the total magnetization $S^z$, and the Hamiltonian itself. This rigorously establishes that, unlike the Heisenberg ferromagnet, the model admits no local order parameter commuting with the Hamiltonian, so that its continuous symmetry breaking is enabled by the frustration-free structure rather than by a conserved order parameter. We also prove the absence of $k$-local conserved quantities for $3\le k\le N/2$ in the periodic Motzkin chain, a frustration-free spin-$1$ chain closely related to the original Motzkin chain, for which spontaneous $U(1)$ symmetry breaking at zero temperature has also been reported.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 需要高效且数值稳定的方法计算多任意子非阿贝尔编织矩阵，并探究不同编织方案在有限尺寸效应下的可靠性，以及实验相关因素（如相互作用和囚禁势）对非阿贝尔物理的影响。

Method: 采用混合蒙特卡洛方法计算Moore-Read准空穴在不同编织方案下的多任意子非阿贝尔编织矩阵；通过将全局旋转编织方案的误差与简单双任意子交换的误差进行基准比较，验证其对有限尺寸效应的鲁棒性；并研究电子-电子相互作用及局域静电捕获势如何影响不同融合通道的能量偏好。

Result: 基于全局旋转的任意子编织方案在编织矩阵误差上优于简单的双任意子交换方案，表现出对有限尺寸效应的显著鲁棒性；同时发现电子-电子相互作用和局域静电势阱会改变不同融合通道的能量偏好，并进而影响非阿贝尔编织矩阵，这一现象体现了非阿贝尔态的长程纠缠特性。

Conclusion: 研究结果对分数量子霍尔（fractional quantum Hall）及类似系统（包括摩尔（moiré）材料中快速发展的分数量子反常霍尔态）中非阿贝尔物理的实验实现具有重要参考价值。

Abstract: We employ the hybrid Monte Carlo method to efficiently compute the many-anyon non-Abelian braiding matrices associated with different braiding schemes of the Moore-Read quasiholes. A novel proposal in this work is that anyon braiding schemes based on a global rotation are robust against finite-size effects, as demonstrated by benchmarking their errors in the braiding matrix against those of a simple two-anyon exchange. Moreover, we investigate how electron-electron interactions and local electrostatic trapping potentials influence the energetic preference of different fusion channels. Their effect on the non-Abelian braiding matrices has been verified, a surprising phenomenon that demonstrates long-range entanglement of non-Abelian states. Our results are relevant to the experimental realization of non-Abelian physics in fractional quantum Hall and other analogous systems, including the fast-growing field of fractional quantum anomalous Hall states in moiré materials.

</details>


### [89] [Inducing metal-insulator transition via disorder in correlated kagome systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17558)
*Qingzhuo Duan,Hongdao Zhuge,Zixuan JIa,Tianxing Ma*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本研究利用行列式量子蒙特卡罗（determinant quantum Monte Carlo）方法，系统研究了具有跳跃无序（hopping disorder）的关联kagome系统中的电子输运与磁性性质，揭示了跳跃无序与电子关联（electron correlations）之间的协同作用如何促进电子局域化，并划分出反铁磁金属、关联安德森绝缘体（correlated Anderson insulator）和无序莫特绝缘体（disordered Mott insulator）三种相图区域。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: kagome材料多为金属性，阻碍了其在逻辑和光电器件中的实际应用，因此需要实现绝缘态以充分挖掘其潜力。学术界尚缺乏对跳跃无序与电子关联协同作用如何驱动kagome系统金属-绝缘体相变（metal-insulator transition）的系统认识。

Method: 采用行列式量子蒙特卡罗方法模拟相关kagome系统中的电子输运与磁性行为，并通过分析动能（kinetic energy）、直流电导率（dc conductivity）和费米能级态密度（density of states at the Fermi level）等关键物理量来评估金属-绝缘体转变。

Result: 研究发现跳跃无序与电子关联的协同相互作用促进了电子局域化；在绝缘相内，无序程度升高会降低实现莫特转变（Mott transition）所需的库仑相互作用强度。无序虽然部分抑制了反铁磁有序（antiferromagnetic ordering），但不足以引发完全的磁性转变。最终总结出区分反铁磁金属、关联安德森绝缘体和无序莫特绝缘体的两幅示意性相图区域。

Conclusion: 该研究增进了对无序驱动kagome系统金属-绝缘体相变的理解，并为实验中调控这类相变提供了可操作的理论指导。

Abstract: The metal-insulator transition is often accompanied by fascinating quantum phenomena, including superconducting domes, antiferromagnetic phase transitions, and quantum spin liquids. Concurrently, kagome materials are predominantly metallic, necessitating the realization of insulating states to fully exploit their significant potential in logic and optoelectronic device applications. To address this, we investigate the electronic transport and magnetic properties in correlated kagome systems with hopping disorder using the determinant quantum Monte Carlo method. Through comprehensive analysis of the kinetic energy, dc conductivity, and density of states at the Fermi level, we demonstrate that the cooperative interplay between hopping disorder and electron correlations promotes electron localization. Within the insulator, an increase in the disorder level reduces the Coulomb interaction required for the Mott transition. Additionally, while disorder partially suppresses antiferromagnetic ordering, it remains insufficient to induce a complete magnetic transition. Finally, we summarize two schematic regions distinguishing between antiferromagnetic metal, correlated Anderson insulator, and disordered Mott insulator. Our study advances the understanding of metal-insulator transition in kagome systems by disorder and provides actionable insights for experimental control of these transitions.

</details>


### [90] [Gauge-constrained Spinon Complexes Near Deconfined Quantum Criticality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17631)
*Zhi-Yao Ning,Xue-Feng Zhang,Naoki Kawashima,Jun Takahashi*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本研究利用量子蒙特卡洛模拟与钉扎自旋缺陷技术，在柱状价键固体（columnar valence-bond-solid, VBS）相中系统研究了受限自旋子（confined spinon）复合体及其畴壁弦的断裂机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子磁体为研究涌现规范场（emergent gauge field）所约束的禁闭（confinement）和规范约束结构提供了微观平台，但其多自旋子结构的组装与解析尚缺乏可控手段。

Method: 通过在近去禁闭量子临界点（deconfined quantum criticality）的柱状VBS相中引入钉扎自旋缺陷作为静态自旋子源，并借助量子蒙特卡洛（quantum Monte Carlo）模拟，测量缺陷能量并对VBS畴壁弦进行实空间可视化。

Result: 自旋子-反自旋子源匹配时，激发能在特征间距处饱和，表现为一个延伸偶极子重组为两个较短的中性偶极子，提供了弦断裂（string breaking）的能量与实空间证据；畴壁连通性受晶格尺度VBS相位偏移调控，多钉扎图案可形成全局连通的多自旋子复合体与延伸畴壁网络。

Conclusion: 钉扎自旋缺陷被确立为在VBS相中组装和解析多自旋子结构及其禁闭弦的可控工具。

Abstract: Quantum magnets provide a microscopic platform for studying confinement and gauge-constrained structures mediated by emergent gauge fields. We investigate confined spinon complexes in the columnar valence-bond-solid (VBS) phase near deconfined quantum criticality using pinned-spin defects and quantum Monte Carlo simulations. The pinned spins act as static spinon sources with controlled positions, spin projections, and VBS vorticities, enabling measurement of defect energies and direct real-space visualization of the associated VBS domain-wall strings. For matched spinon-antispinon sources, the excitation energy saturates beyond a characteristic separation as one extended dipole reorganizes into two shorter neutral dipoles, providing energetic and real-space evidence of string breaking. We further show that domain-wall connectivity is governed by lattice-scale VBS phase offsets in addition to vorticity and spin-projection neutrality. Compatible multi-pin patterns generate connected four-spinon complexes and extended domain-wall networks that retain their global connectivity under local distortions. These results establish pinned-spin defects as a controlled tool for assembling and resolving multi-spinon structures and their confining strings in a VBS phase.

</details>

### [91] [Controlling Structure and Properties of Vapor-Deposited Glasses of Organic Semiconductors: Recent Advances and Challenges](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17080)
*Kushal Bagchi,MD Ediger*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 本文综述了近十年在调控气相沉积玻璃（vapor-deposited glasses）结构方面的进展，聚焦于通过改变沉积过程中衬底温度来调节有机半导体玻璃的密度和分子取向，进而显著提升OLED（有机发光二极管）的外量子效率和寿命；同时强调了分子模拟在复现实验结构方面的能力，为未来十年基于模拟设计与优化气相沉积玻璃铺平道路。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 有机半导体气相沉积玻璃的结构（密度与分子取向）对其器件性能至关重要，但此前缺乏系统性的调控手段和认知。通过综述近十年研究，作者旨在总结结构调控策略及其对OLED性能的改善效果，并展望计算机模拟在设计此类玻璃中的潜力。

Method: 本文为综述型论文（review article），系统梳理了气相沉积有机半导体玻璃的结构调控研究，重点对比了不同衬底温度下制备的玻璃在密度与分子取向上的差异，并评述了分子模拟在再现实验观测结构方面的作用。

Result: 研究发现，通过调节沉积时的衬底温度，可以从同一分子制备出密度和分子取向范围广泛的玻璃材料；这些结构变化被证明能显著提升OLED的外量子效率（external quantum efficiency, EQE）和器件寿命。此外，分子模拟已能够成功复现实验观测到的玻璃结构。

Conclusion: 气相沉积玻璃结构的可控调控是提升有机半导体器件性能的有效途径；分子模拟再现实验结构的能力为未来十年实现气相沉积玻璃的计算机内设计（in-silico design）奠定了基础。文章同时指出了通过结构控制进一步改善有机半导体性能的研究机遇。

Abstract: The last decade has seen great progress in manipulating the structure of vapor-deposited glasses of organic semiconductors. By varying the substrate temperature during deposition, glasses with a wide range of density and molecular orientation can be prepared from a given molecule. We review recent studies that show the structure of vapor-deposited glasses can be tuned to significantly improve the external quantum efficiency and lifetime of OLEDs (organic light emitting diodes). We highlight the ability of molecular simulations to reproduce experimentally observed structures, setting the stage for in-silico design of vapor-deposited glasses in the coming decade. Finally, we identify research opportunities for improving the properties of organic semiconductors by controlling the structure of vapor-deposited glasses.

</details>


### [92] [Rejuvenation versus overaging: The effect of cyclic loading/unloading on the segmental dynamics of PMMA glasses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17086)
*Trevor Bennin,Enran Xing,Josh Ricci,M. D. Ediger*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 本研究通过对PMMA玻璃在Tg-10 K至Tg-25 K温度范围内进行5000次拉伸加载/卸载循环实验，利用探针再取向技术和时间-老化时间叠加方法分别测量链段动力学和力学响应，发现循环加载后链段动力学保持不变或加快，且不产生过老化（overaging）效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 形变加速聚合物玻璃的结构弛豫或物理老化现象（即过老化）已被实验和模拟广泛报道，准确理解过老化对于预测聚合物玻璃在工程应用中的长期行为至关重要。本文旨在探讨循环加载/卸载对PMMA玻璃链段动力学和力学性能的影响，以澄清过老化机制。

Method: 采用探针再取向（probe reorientation）技术测量链段动力学，利用时间-老化时间叠加（time-aging time superposition）方法分析力学响应。在Tg-10 K至Tg-25 K温度范围内、循环拉伸应变0.003至0.007的条件下，对PMMA玻璃进行了5000次加载/卸载循环。

Result: 循环加载后，探针再取向技术测得的链段动力学相对于未形变样品保持不变或变快；循环玻璃的弛豫时间以与其老化时间同一量级的公共时间尺度恢复，表明样品保留了原始老化记忆而非完全清除热力学和力学历史。令人意外的是，循环引起的探针再取向测量变化比力学性能变化更显著，说明探针再取向技术可灵敏检测形变的非线性效应。光学和力学测量均未观察到过老化证据。

Conclusion: 循环加载/卸载并未在本研究条件下诱导PMMA玻璃发生过老化，且玻璃对自身老化历史保留记忆。探针再取向技术相比传统力学测量对形变的非线性效应具有更高的灵敏度，可作为探测该类效应的重要工具。

Abstract: The acceleration of structural relaxation or physical aging by deformation, known as overaging, has been reported in experiments and simulations of polymer and colloid glasses, and correctly accounting for overaging is important for the prediction of the long-term behavior of polymer glasses in engineering applications. Here the effects of cyclic loading/unloading on the segmental dynamics and mechanical properties of PMMA glasses are investigated using a probe reorientation technique and time-aging time superposition of the mechanical response, respectively. Sets of 5000 tensile loading/unloading cycles were performed at temperatures between Tg - 10 K and Tg - 25 K with cycle extension strains ranging from 0.003 to 0.007. After cycling, the segmental dynamics measured with the probe reorientation technique either remained unchanged or were faster relative to an undeformed sample. The relaxation times of cycled glasses recovered with a common time scale on the order of their aging time, indicating that they retain a memory of their original age, as opposed to a full erasure of their thermal and mechanical history. Surprisingly, changes as a result of cycling were more obvious in probe reorientation measurements than in the mechanical properties, suggesting that the probe reorientation technique can sensitively detect nonlinear effects of deformation. No evidence of overaging was observed in the optical or mechanical measurements as a result of these cyclic loading/unloading experiments.

</details>


### [93] [Resonant Untrapping of Active Polymers in Breathing Lattices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17359)
*Yihang Sun,Yixiang Li,Tsvi Tlusty,Guolong Zhu*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 本研究通过布朗动力学模拟揭示了波动性受限环境可对活性聚合物的自阻陷构象产生共振脱困效应，当环境波动速率与螺旋本征弛豫速率相匹配时，长时扩散可提升两个数量级，且无需相干周期驱动。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 活性聚合物（active polymers）在拥挤环境中会卷曲成亚稳构象并自陷其中，阻碍其长时输运。作者旨在探究环境受限涨落是否能够加速聚合物从自生成陷阱中的逃逸，从而调控其输运行为。

Method: 采用布朗动力学（Brownian dynamics）模拟，研究对象为在呼吸式障碍晶格（breathing obstacle lattice）中运动的半柔性链（semiflexible chain）。通过改变环境波动速率与链的内在弛豫时间之比，系统考察扩散系数等输运指标的响应，并比较周期性涨落与随机涨落下的行为差异。

Result: 模拟观察到系统在紧凑旋转螺旋态与伸展平动态之间发生间歇性切换。当环境波动速率与螺旋本征弛豫速率接近时，长时扩散系数提升可达两个数量级；该增强在随机涨落条件下依然存在，表明相干周期性驱动并非必需。此外，活性强度存在双优化效应：适中活性促进有利构象形成后的逃逸，而过强驱动则稳定螺旋并抑制构象形成。

Conclusion: 共振脱困（resonant untrapping）为波动环境调控由内部构象动力学生成势垒的输运提供了一种普适机制，对理解活性聚合物在复杂环境中的扩散与迁移行为具有理论意义。

Abstract: In crowded environments, active polymers can trap themselves by winding into long-lived conformations. We show that fluctuations of the surrounding confinement can resonantly accelerate escape from these self-generated traps. Brownian dynamics simulations of a driven semiflexible chain in a breathing obstacle lattice reveal intermittent switching between a compact rotating spiral and an extended translating state. Long-time diffusion increases by up to two orders of magnitude when the environmental fluctuation rate becomes comparable to the spiral's intrinsic relaxation rate. The enhancement persists under stochastic fluctuations, showing that coherent periodic forcing is not required. Activity creates a second optimum: it promotes escape once favorable conformations form, yet at strong drive stabilizes the spiral and suppresses their formation. Resonant untrapping thus provides a general mechanism by which fluctuating environments regulate transport through barriers generated by internal conformational dynamics.

</details>


### [94] [Effect of Convection Rolls in Motility-Induced Phase Separation of Active Janus Particles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17692)
*Poulami Bag*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 本文通过数值模拟研究了二维对流卷（convection rolls）中活性粒子（active particles）的运动诱导相分离，发现弱流抑制聚集并使自推进临界速度偏移、旋节线区域收缩，同时揭示了极高自推进阻碍相分离的重入相行为以及密度分布对卷周期性的强依赖。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解活性物质（active matter）中非平衡集体行为（non-equilibrium collective behaviour）如何受外部流场调控，特别是对流卷对运动诱导相分离（motility-induced phase separation）的影响，此前尚未被系统研究。

Method: 采用数值模拟方法，分析局域堆积分数分布、密度涨落、相图以及扩散率曲线，系统考察自推进、全局堆积分数与平流强度（advection strength）之间的相互作用。

Result: 弱流区呈现双峰密度分布、缓慢衰减的密度涨落以及扩散率急剧下降的相分离特征；增强平流通过提升粒子输运和减少捕获来抑制聚集，同时导致运动诱导相分离的临界自推进速度偏移和旋节线区域收缩；此外发现极高自推进通过促进粒子逃逸密集区域而阻碍相分离的重入相行为，且密度分布强烈依赖卷周期性。

Conclusion: 对流卷提供了一种有效调控活性物质中非平衡集体行为的手段，其影响不仅体现为对聚集的直观抑制，还涉及重入相行为等非平凡现象。

Abstract: We numerically study motility-induced phase separation of active particles in two-dimensional convection rolls. We analyse local packing-fraction distributions, density fluctuations, the corresponding phase diagrams, and diffusivity curves to characterise the interplay between self-propulsion, global packing fraction, and advection strength. In the weak-flow regime, the system exhibits phase separation characterised by bimodal density distributions, slowly decaying density fluctuations, and a sharp reduction in diffusivity. Increasing advection suppresses clustering by enhancing particle transport and reducing trapping, leading to a shift in the critical self-propulsion velocity for motility-induced phase separation and a shrinkage of the spinodal region. Beyond the intuitive suppression of clustering by weak-flow advection, our results reveal several non-trivial phenomena including a reentrant phase behaviour where extremely high self-propulsion hinders motility-induced phase separation by facilitating particle escape from dense regions. We also observe that the density distributions strongly depend on roll periodicity. These findings demonstrate that convection rolls provide an effective means to control non-equilibrium collective behaviour in active matter.

</details>


### [95] [Hydrodynamic Mode Coupling: Effects of density variations in nanoscale channel flows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17705)
*L. Heitmeier,J. S. Hansen*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个模态框架（modal framework），用于研究纳米尺度下密度变化对重力驱动型流动的影响。通过将密度场和加速度场进行本征函数分解，作者将每个剪切—压力模态划分为齐次贡献和非齐次贡献，从而揭示密度变化与外场驱动之间的耦合机制及其对流动行为的主导作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 纳米尺度下密度变化对重力驱动型流动的影响机制尚不明确，特别是外部加速度与密度场之间如何耦合并决定流动行为需要系统的理论工具来阐明。

Method: 采用模态框架方法，对密度场和加速度场进行本征函数分解（eigenfunction decomposition），将每个剪切—压力模式（shear-pressure mode）分解为齐次贡献与由密度和加速度的傅里叶振幅决定的非齐次贡献，从而建立密度变化与外加驱动之间的模式耦合分析工具。

Result: 对于Poiseuille流动，当通道高度大于特征分子间距离时，齐次贡献主导长波（小波矢）响应，与已有模拟结果一致；而对于正弦驱动的流动，对加速度模式的选择性激励可以产生相反行为，即非齐次贡献主导长波响应。密度变化的影响依赖具体流动类型，关键在于加速度场与密度场之间的详细模式耦合方式。

Conclusion: 该模态框架为理解并预测不同外加加速度条件下纳米尺度流动行为提供了一种直接且系统的分析途径。

Abstract: We apply a modal framework for investigating the effect of density variations on gravity-type driven flows at the nanoscale. Using eigenfunction decomposition of the density and acceleration fields, each shear-pressure mode is separated into a homogeneous contribution and an inhomogeneous contribution determined by the Fourier amplitudes of the density and applied acceleration. This decomposition provides a direct means of identifying how density variations and external forcing couple and govern the flow behavior. We first revisit the Poiseuille flow and show that for channel heights larger than the characteristic intermolecular distance the homogeneous contribution dominates the long wave length (small wave vector) response, consistent with previous simulation results. In contrast, for sinusoidally driven flow, selective excitation of acceleration modes can produce the opposite behavior, with the inhomogeneous contribution dominating the long wave length response. The results show that the effect of the density variations depends on the specific flow; specifically the detailed mode coupling between the acceleration and density fields. The framework presented here provides a direct systematic approach for understanding and predicting the flow depending on the applied acceleration.

</details>


### [96] [Rheology and Dynamic Arrest in Colloidal Depletion Gels Mediated by Surface Brush Density](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17764)
*Ziye Zhuang,Robert A. Campbell,Safa Jamali,Ali Mohraz*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 本研究以表面接枝聚合物刷（surface-grafted polymer brush）密度作为几何保持的控制参数，调控胶体耗尽力凝胶（colloidal depletion gel）的流变学性质。降低刷密度加速凝胶化，并产生具有更高平台储能模量和屈服应力的凝胶，且这种力学增强并非以局部致密化为代价，而是通过降低形成弹性节点的配位数阈值实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 胶体耗尽力凝胶的流变学调控通常依赖改变颗粒浓度或有效吸引力强度，但缺乏一种既能保持体系几何形态又能有效调节力学响应的实验控制手段。作者希望探索表面刷密度是否能作为这样一种几何保持（geometry-preserving）的控制参数，同时解耦有效吸引力、网络构型和接触运动学对凝胶力学性质的影响。

Method: 通过调控表面接枝聚合物刷的密度，系统改变胶体颗粒间的有效排斥层厚度，从而调节耗尽力相互作用强度。随后对体系进行流变学测量，记录凝胶化动力学、平台储能模量（plateau storage modulus）、屈服应力（yield stress）等力学响应；并结合微观结构分析，考察平均接触数（average contact number）与空间异质性（spatial heterogeneity）的变化；最后以Soft Glassy Rheology框架分析有效噪声温度与蠕变行为。

Result: 降低刷密度加速凝胶化过程，并获得更高的平台储能模量和屈服应力。力学增强并未伴随局部致密化：低刷网络的平均接触数更低、空间异质性更小，弹性响应却更强，表明低刷凝胶网络中形成弹性节点所需的配位数阈值更低。此外，低刷凝胶松弛更慢、蠕变变形累积更少，且在Soft Glassy Rheology框架下表现出更低的有效噪声温度。

Conclusion: 表面刷密度被确认为一种实验上易于获取的胶体耗尽力凝胶流变学控制参数，它能够同时耦合地改变有效吸引力、网络构型与接触运动学，从而实现对凝胶力学性能的定向调控。

Abstract: We use the density of surface-grafted polymers as a geometry-preserving control parameter for tuning the rheology of colloidal depletion gels. Reducing brush density accelerates gelation and produces gels with higher plateau storage modulus and yield stress. This mechanical enhancement is not accompanied by increased local densification; low-brush networks exhibit lower average contact number and reduced spatial heterogeneity while displaying stronger elastic responses than their high-brush counterparts. Our findings demonstrate a reduced coordination threshold to form elastic nodes in the low-brush gel network. In addition, low-brush gels relax more slowly, accumulate less creep deformation, and exhibit lower effective noise temperatures within the Soft Glassy Rheology framework. These results establish surface-brush density as an experimentally accessible control parameter for colloidal depletion gel rheology with coupled changes in effective attraction, network architecture, and contact kinematics.

</details>


### [97] [A solid-state theory for dense cylindrical packings of balls](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17850)
*Luke K. Davis,Alexander R. Klotz*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 本文针对圆柱体（cylinder）中硬球（hard sphere）的密集堆积问题，提出了一种解析理论，通过对三维密排固体进行有限圆柱遮蔽处理，覆盖了全部圆柱长径比（aspect ratio）范围，并推导出适用于任意规则晶格（regular lattice）的精确堆积分数方程，同时提出优化圆柱取向的方法以收紧堆积分数的下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前的研究多聚焦于特定宽度范围内的细长圆柱体，缺乏覆盖全尺寸范围的通用解析理论，导致对堆积分数振荡与标度行为的理解不够系统。

Method: 该理论将三维密排固体进行有限圆柱遮蔽（finite cylindrical masking）处理，推导出适用于任意规则晶格的精确堆积分数方程；在此基础上，通过数学优化程序调整圆柱取向（orientation of the cylinder）以收紧解析下界（rigorous lower bound），并提出简单技术改进预测堆积结构。

Result: 该方法推导出的解析关系为圆柱体堆积提供了严格下界，并通过取向优化进一步收紧了该下界；该理论为以往工作中出现的体积分数振荡与标度现象提供了理论基础，且有效覆盖全部圆柱尺寸（container size）。

Conclusion: 该研究为圆柱体中球的堆积问题提供了统一的一般性理论框架，适用于任意容器尺寸，超越了此前仅针对高圆柱体的有限宽度范围的研究。

Abstract: We develop an analytical theory for the dense packing of hard spheres in cylinders. Physically, our theory consists of a finite cylindrical masking of a close-packed three-dimensional solid and covers the entire range of cylinder aspect ratios, thus going beyond efforts that are focused on very tall cylinders in a narrow range of widths. We explicitly derive an exact equation for resulting packing fractions, valid for any regular lattice, and it provides a basis to understand the oscillations and scaling of volume fractions that have appeared in previous works. Our analytical relation serves as a rigorous lower bound and to tighten it we derive, and implement, an efficient mathematical procedure to optimize the orientation of the cylinder. Furthermore, we suggest simple techniques to improve on the predicted packings. Overall, we provide a general theoretical foundation for the packing of balls in cylinders, valid for all container sizes.

</details>


### [98] [Topology of Nonequilibrium Currents Controls Active Transport](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17954)
*A. Escobar,G. Geva,A. Alexander-Katz,L. R. Arriaga,J. V. Alvarez,J. L. Aragones*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 本文揭示结构化环境中活性粒子输运路径的大尺度组织受拓扑约束支配：流体动力学散射产生的非平衡流场（nonequilibrium current fields）由其整数值缺陷结构（defect structure）刻画，这些拓扑缺陷约束输运路径并使其对平滑扰动具有鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 结构化环境反复重定向活性粒子，产生无法直接从单粒子轨迹推断的输运路径，理解这些路径的大尺度组织规律是核心科学问题。

Method: 通过旋转胶体在障碍物阵列中的实验演示该原理，并将分析扩展到斯托克斯流（stokeslet）和力偶极（force-dipole）流动，从而将微观输运与流体动力学产生的非平衡电流的拓扑结构联系起来。

Result: 证明流体动力学散射生成的非平衡电流场具有以整数指标表征的缺陷结构，这些缺陷约束输运路径，并使其在平滑扰动下保持鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 微观尺度输运可由流体动力学生成的非平衡电流的拓扑性质所支配，该原理在旋转胶体系统及其他流体动力学流动模型中得到统一验证。

Abstract: Structured environments repeatedly redirect active particles, producing transport pathways that cannot be readily inferred from individual trajectories. Here, we show that the large-scale organization of these transport pathways is governed by topological constraints. Hydrodynamic scattering generates nonequilibrium current fields whose defect structure, characterized by integer indices, constrain transport pathways and renders them robust to smooth perturbations. This principle is demonstrated with rotating colloids in obstacle arrays and extended to stokeslet and force-dipole flows, thereby linking microscale transport to the topology of hydrodynamically generated nonequilibrium currents.

</details>

### [99] [Simplicial closure fragments the explosive cooperation transitions in higher-order public goods games](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17968)
*Wenjia Rao,Jianneng Huang*

Main category: physics.soc-ph

TL;DR: 本研究通过受控高阶公共品博弈实验，发现单纯复合形闭包（simplicial closure）对超图（hypergraph）上的合作演化转变具有选择性影响：当超图上的合作转变是连续型时，单纯闭包仅拓宽转变而不改变其性质；当转变是爆炸式一阶型时，单纯闭包会将紧凑的双稳态碎裂为广泛的亚稳态终态集合。这一效应源于单纯闭包在合作核（cooperative nucleus）动力学中的双重角色。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超图和单纯复合形（simplicial complex）是两种常见的高阶网络表示方法，通常被认为在编码可比较的群组相互作用时主要在定量上存在差异。作者旨在检验这一预期在演化合作动力学（evolutionary cooperation dynamics）中是否成立，从而揭示两者可能存在的质性差异。

Method: 使用带有最少额外参数（minimal ad hoc parameters）的受控高阶公共品博弈（public goods game, PGG），在同一三角形骨干（triangular backbone）基础上构造随机化的超图和单纯复合形，系统比较两者上的合作转变（cooperation transition）行为，并通过详细分析和进一步的实验验证效应的普遍性。

Result: 研究发现单纯复合形闭包的影响具有选择性：在超图上合作转变为连续型时，施加单纯闭包主要拓宽转变而不改变其质性特征；在超图上转变为爆炸式一阶型时，单纯闭包将紧凑的低/高双稳态（bistability）碎裂为广泛的亚稳态（metastable）终态集合。进一步实验表明该碎裂效应不依赖于具体收益函数形式，是爆炸式合作转变在高阶公共品博弈中的普遍特征。

Conclusion: 超图与单纯复合形在高阶合作动力学中的差异并非仅是定量的。单纯闭包在合作核动力学中扮演双重角色：一方面促进局部合作核的存续，另一方面抑制其向系统级联（system-wide cascade）的转化，从而选择性地影响不同类型的合作转变。这一发现表明超图与单纯复合形在编码高阶相互作用时可能产生质性的动力学差异。

Abstract: Hypergraph (HG) and simplicial complex (SC) are two common representations of higher-order networks, and are often expected to differ mainly quantitatively when they encode comparable group interactions. Here we show that this expectation fails in evolutionary cooperation dynamics. Using a controlled higher-order public goods game (PGG) with minimal ad hoc parameters, we compare cooperation transitions on randomized HG and SC constructed from the same triangular backbone. We find that the impact of simplicial closure is selective: when the cooperation transition is continuous-like on HG, imposing simplicial closure mainly broadens the transition without changing its qualitative nature; however, when the transition is explosive and first-order-like on HG, simplicial closure will fragment the compact low/high bistability into a broad ensemble of metastable final states. Detailed analysis reveals that this selective effect arises from the dual role of simplicial closure in cooperative-nucleus dynamics: it promotes the survival of local cooperative nuclei while suppressing their conversion into system-wide cascades. Further experiments confirm that this fragmentation is not tied to a specific payoff form, but is a generic feature of explosive cooperation transitions in higher-order PGGs.

</details>

### [100] [Entropy and Frenesy: the arrow and the bustle of time](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17586)
*Christian Maes*

Main category: physics.pop-ph

TL;DR: 本文以非正式、半科普风格撰写的论文，旨在探讨非平衡统计力学（nonequilibrium statistical mechanics）的三大基础问题，并提出熵产生（entropy production）与“ frenesy”（指与熵产生互补的动力学涨落量）共同构成理解介观至宏观尺度物理时间的更完整起点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非平衡统计力学涉及众多现象，但其背后存在三个根本性未解问题：熵产生的起源与时间箭头的定义、远离平衡态驱动如何产生并选择稳定且功能丰富的结构、以及如何对多样化的非平衡系统建立系统而统一的第一性原理描述。本文旨在以通俗方式厘清这些核心议题。

Method: 本文采用非正式的、半科普（semi-popular-science）的论述文体，围绕三大基础问题展开，重点阐释熵产生与较新提出的 frenesy 概念在稳态驱动系统的动力学涨落中所扮演的互补角色。

Result: 作者论证了熵产生与 frenesy 二者在描述稳态驱动系统动力学涨落方面具有互补性，共同为介观（mesoscopic）到宏观（macroscopic）尺度的物理时间理解提供了更完整的出发点。

Conclusion: 从原子论机械世界观出发，熵产生赋予宏观过程方向并定义时间箭头，而 frenesy 作为补充概念有助于更全面理解物理时间；两者结合为构建非平衡统计力学的统一框架提供了更强的基础。

Abstract: Nonequilibrium statistical mechanics is the science of time as it manifests itself in the transient or steady entropy producing dynamical condition of many-body systems. Beyond the vivid multiformity of the phenomena themselves, three foundational issues lie at its heart. The first question concerns the very origin of entropy production, which gives macroscopic processes their direction and thereby defines the arrow of time: how does an atomistic mechanical world give rise to dissipated heat, thermalization, and time-reversal symmetry breaking? The second asks why, far beyond the familiar behavior of equilibrium systems, driving or agitation may produce and select stable and functionally rich structures: what is it about far-from-equilibrium, low-entropy driving that unleashes the wealth and intricacy of the natural world? The third issue is partly methodological: to determine how, and for which subclass of such wildly diverse nonequilibrium systems, a systematic, unified description and modeling from first principles remains possible. This essay addresses these issues in an informal, semi-popular-science style, offering a personally engaging yet wide-ranging introduction to the complementary roles of entropy production and the less familiar, more recently coined notion of frenesy in the dynamical fluctuations of steadily driven systems. Together, we argue, they provide a more complete starting point for understanding physical time on mesoscopic to macroscopic scales.

</details>
