### [101] [Detecting Lorentz-violation induced by a tensor field with S-star's motion around Sgr A*](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16920)
*Qi Qi,Yu Sang,Xiao-Mei Kuang*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文基于Kalb-Ramond引力框架下的静态球对称黑洞模型，利用Sgr A*周围S2恒星的轨道数据，通过14维马尔可夫链蒙特卡洛（Markov Chain Monte Carlo）分析，对单一洛伦兹破坏参数ℓ施加了约10^-5量级的约束，比事件视界望远镜（Event Horizon Telescope）成像给出的约束紧约三个数量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强引力场中测试洛伦兹对称性（Lorentz symmetry）为标准模型扩展提供了独特探针，而围绕银河系中心超大质量黑洞Sgr A*运行的S星族轨道为这类测试提供了天然实验室。

Method: 采用Kalb-Ramond引力中的静态球对称黑洞模型，其中相对论（general relativity）的偏离由Kalb-Ramond张量场引入的单一洛伦兹破坏参数ℓ刻画；对S2轨道数据执行完整的14维马尔可夫链蒙特卡洛分析，分别在均匀先验和高斯先验下拟合；此外还单独拟合了S38、S55恒星数据并进行联合分析。

Result: 在均匀先验下得到ℓ = 1.60×10^-5（+1.38×10^-4/−1.76×10^-4），在高斯先验下得到ℓ = −1.02×10^-5（+1.26×10^-4/−1.19×10^-4），置信水平均为1σ；该约束比事件视界望远镜对Sgr A*的成像约束紧约三个数量级。所有仿真中ℓ的最佳拟合值均处于10^-5量级，但S2恒星提供了最严格的参数约束，因其观测数据精度更高，相比之下S38和S55的公开数据较少。

Conclusion: S2恒星轨道数据分析能够为Kalb-Ramond引力中的洛伦兹破坏参数提供显著优于事件视界望远镜成像的约束，表明S星族轨道是检验强引力场中洛伦兹对称性的有力工具。

Abstract: Testing Lorentz symmetry in strong gravitational fields provides a unique probe of extensions to standard model. The orbiting motions of the S-stars around the supermassive black hole Sgr~A* provide a natural laboratory for such tests. In this paper, we analyze the S2 orbital data focusing on a static and spherically symmetric black hole within Kalb--Ramond gravity, where the deviations from general relativity are encoded in a single Lorentz-violating parameter $\ell$ introduced by the Kalb--Ramond tensor field. Using a full 14-dimensional Markov Chain Monte Carlo analysis under uniform and Gaussian priors, we obtain $\ell = {1.60 \times 10^{-5}}^{+1.38 \times 10^{-4}}_{-1.76 \times 10^{-4}} $ and $\ell = {-1.02 \times 10^{-5}}^{+1.26 \times 10^{-4}}_{-1.19 \times 10^{-4}} $ at $1σ$ confidence level, respectively. These constraints are about three orders of magnitude tighter than those from Event Horizon Telescope imaging of Sgr~A*. We also perform MCMC simulation by fitting data of S38 and S55 stars, as well as their joint analysis. Our results show that the best fit values of $\ell$ in all simulations are always of $10^{-5}$ order, but S2 star provides the most stringent constraints on the parameters because S2 star has higher precision observational data comparing to the fewer public data for other two stars.

</details>


### [102] [New results concerning the curvature of the Universe](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16935)
*Maxim Khlopov,Maxim Krasnov,Jan Novák*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文探讨宇宙空间曲率这一宇宙学（cosmology）基础问题，指出实验观测表明宇宙接近空间平坦，但Planck卫星的新结果暗示曲率参数可能为正，同时本文检验了支持闭合宇宙（closed Universe）的全新理论结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 宇宙空间曲率是宇宙学的基本问题之一，是理解宇宙整体几何结构的关键。尽管实验观测显示宇宙接近空间平坦，但Planck卫星的测量结果暗示曲率参数可能为正，这与平坦宇宙的标准图景存在张力，促使研究者重新审视闭合宇宙的可能性。

Method: 本研究通过分析Planck卫星为主的实验观测数据，并考察全新的理论结果，系统性地评估宇宙空间曲率为正的假设，探讨我们是否实际生活在一个闭合的宇宙中。

Result: 观测数据显示宇宙非常接近空间平坦（spatially flat），但Planck卫星测量结果暗示曲率参数可能为正。此外，新的理论结果进一步支持了闭合宇宙的可能性。

Conclusion: 宇宙可能并非空间平坦，而是具有正曲率的闭合宇宙。这些新证据对当前宇宙学标准模型提出重要挑战。

Abstract: One of the fundamental questions we ask in cosmology is what the spatial curvature of the universe is. Experimental observations show that the universe is very close to being spatially flat. However, new results, mainly from measurements by the Planck satellite, indicate that the curvature parameter might be positive. We will, however, examine completely new theoretical results that suggest that we actually live in a closed Universe.

</details>


### [103] [Quantum-corrected thermodynamics of Schwarzschild AdS black holes in conformal Killing gravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16948)
*Saheb Soroushfar,Sayyed Mehrab Ramezani,Hoda Farahani,Saeed Noori Gashti,Behnam Pourhassan*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文在扩展相空间形式（extended phase-space formalism）下研究了共形Killing引力（conformal Killing gravity）中带有非微扰熵修正的Schwarzschild–AdS黑洞，发现量子修正主要在小视界半径处改变相变点和热稳定性，而大黑洞恢复经典行为；同时通过岛方案（island prescription）恢复了霍金辐射纠缠熵的幺正性，并证明了极端极限下普适热力学关系不受量子修正参数η影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 黑洞热力学中的非微扰熵修正在量子引力框架下具有重要意义，但此类修正如何影响共形Killing引力中的黑洞相变、热稳定性、信息恢复以及普适热力学关系尚不清楚，本文旨在系统研究这些效应。

Method: 在扩展相空间形式中推导量子修正后的热容（heat capacity）、亥姆霍兹自由能（Helmholtz free energy）、内能（internal energy）和吉布斯自由能（Gibbs free energy）；采用岛方案分析霍金辐射纠缠熵；并在极端极限下对AdS曲率半径施加微扰以检验普适热力学关系。

Result: 量子修正主要在小视界半径处改变相变点和热稳定性，大黑洞恢复经典行为；van der Waals型临界行为仅出现在共形Killing参数a为正值时；霍金辐射纠缠熵在Page时间后饱和于S_sat = 2(πr_H² + ηe^{−πr_H²})，更强的量子修正提高熵阈值并推迟信息恢复；量子修正参数η在极端极限的普适关系中完全抵消，得到U = −r_ext³/l³。

Conclusion: 共形Killing引力中的Schwarzschild–AdS黑洞通过岛方案可恢复信息幺正性，且普适热力学关系对非微扰熵修正保持稳健，不受量子修正参数η的影响。

Abstract: We study Schwarzschild--AdS black holes in conformal Killing gravity with non-perturbative entropy corrections within the extended phase-space formalism. By deriving the quantum-corrected heat capacity, Helmholtz free energy, internal energy, and Gibbs free energy, we show that quantum corrections modify phase transition points and thermal stability mainly at small horizon radii, while classical behavior is recovered for large black holes. Critical behavior of van der Waals type appears exclusively for positive values of the conformal Killing parameter $a$. Applying the island prescription, we find that the entanglement entropy of Hawking radiation saturates after the Page time at $S_{\text{sat}} = 2(πr_H^2 + ηe^{-πr_H^2})$, thereby restoring unitarity. Stronger quantum corrections increase the entropy threshold and delay information recovery. Furthermore, we examine the universal thermodynamic relation in the extremal limit under a minimal perturbation of the AdS curvature radius. We prove that the quantum correction parameter $η$ cancels out completely, yielding the robust universal combination $U = -r_{\text{ext}}^3 / l^3$. This demonstrates that the universal relation remains stable against non-perturbative quantum corrections to the entropy.

</details>


### [104] [A Non-Singular Cosmic Bounce in $\mathcal{F}(Q)$ Gravity: A Reconstruction and Phase Space Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16952)
*Nusrat Fatima,M. Sharif*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 该研究在 F(Q) 引力框架下分析了一种非奇异宇宙反弹（non-singular cosmic bounce），其中 Q 为非度量标量。通过重构方法和二维自治动力系统分析，论文展示了几何耦合参数 ψ 对违反零能量条件的关键作用，并指出存在临界能量密度决定反弹是否发生。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该研究旨在探索在 F(Q) 引力理论的背景下，非奇异宇宙反弹是否能够作为大爆炸奇点的替代方案而存在，同时与宇宙加速膨胀保持一致。

Method: 采用两种互补方法：一是利用尺度因子假设（scale-factor ansatz）进行重构分析；二是构建二维自治动力系统（autonomous dynamical system）进行定性分析。研究在具有理想流体物质分布的平坦 Friedmann-Robertson-Walker 时空下，选取引力拉格朗日量 F(Q)=Q+ψQ^n 并引入修正的 Chaplygin 型物质源。

Result: 结果表明，几何耦合参数 ψ 通过产生几何排斥力来违反零能量条件。数值扫描 (ρ₀, ψ) 参数空间发现存在临界能量密度：低于该值时宇宙演化为标准奇异宇宙学解。此外，有效状态方程参数缓慢趋向 de Sitter 值（ω_eff→−1），且声速平方受稳定性与因果性条件约束（0≤C_s²≤1）。

Conclusion: F(Q) 引力为无奇异的反弹宇宙提供了一种自洽的几何图景，并与宇宙加速膨胀相协调。

Abstract: The key focus of this research work is the analysis of a non-singular cosmic bounce in the context of $\mathcal{F}(Q)$ gravity, with $Q$ representing the non-metricity scalar. We consider the gravitational Lagrangian $\mathcal{F}(Q)=Q+ψQ^n$ in the presence of a modified Chaplygin-type matter source with a flat Friedmann-Robertson-Walker spacetime and a perfect matter distribution. In order to proceed, we adopt two approaches: a reconstruction approach using scale-factor ansatz and analysis of a two-dimensional autonomous dynamical system. Our results imply that the geometry coupling parameter $ψ$ plays an important role and causes geometrical repulsion for violating the null energy condition. A numerical scan of the $(ρ_0,ψ)$ parameter space suggests that there is a critical energy density for the bounce to take place, below which the universe evolves towards the standard singular cosmological solution. Moreover, the effective equation of state slowly evolves towards the de Sitter value ($ω_{eff}\rightarrow -1$) and the squared sound speed is bounded by the stability and causality condition ($0\leq C_s^2\leq1$). It is found that $\mathcal{F}(Q)$ gravity provides a coherent geometric picture of the non-singular bouncing universe in accordance with the cosmic accelerated expansion.

</details>


### [105] [Polytropic wormholes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16969)
*Remo Garattini,Emmanuel N. Saridakis,Athanasios G. Tzikas*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文在统一框架下研究了由多方物态方程（polytropic equation of state）支撑的可穿越虫洞（traversable wormhole），分别探讨了均匀和非均匀构型，推导了相应解并分析了多方参数对几何与能量条件的影响，建立了多方物质与虫洞几何之间的系统性联系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 广义相对论中的可穿越虫洞需要非标准物质来源，因此识别具有物理动机的物态方程尤为重要。本文旨在通过多方物态方程提供一种物理上自洽的虫洞构造框架。

Method: 在统一框架下分别考虑均匀与非均匀多方物态方程构型；均匀情形下解由常数多方参数控制，非均匀情形下推导出几何完全由径向多方系数 ω(r) 决定的解析表达式；采用幂律轮廓（power-law profile）构造具有不同参数区间的显式解类。

Result: 正 ω(r) 情形下，物理上合理的解要求多方指数限制为奇整数值；取指数 α=2γ-3 时自然出现广义"荒谬良性"可穿越虫洞构型；喷口处的爆竹条件（flare-out condition）导致零能量条件（null energy condition）违背，但非均匀框架可控制其径向分布。

Conclusion: 研究建立了多方物质与虫洞几何之间的系统性联系，为构造具有局域化奇异物质（localized exotic matter）的紧凑虫洞提供了灵活框架。

Abstract: Traversable wormholes in general relativity require non-standard matter sources, making the identification of physically motivated equations of state particularly important. We investigate wormholes supported by a polytropic equation of state, considering homogeneous and inhomogeneous configurations within a unified framework. We derive the corresponding solutions and analyze the effects of the polytropic parameters on the geometry and energy conditions. In the homogeneous case, the polytropic construction yields a consistent wormhole interior whose geometry and matter content are governed by the constant polytropic parameters. For the inhomogeneous case, we obtain a general analytical expression showing that the geometry is completely determined by the radial polytropic coefficient $ω(r)$. For positive $ω(r)$, the requirement for physically meaningful solutions naturally restricts the polytropic exponent to odd integer values. Using a power-law profile, we construct explicit classes of solutions exhibiting distinct parameter regimes and finite radial support. Interestingly enough, for an exponent $α=2γ-3$, a generalized absurdly benign traversable wormhole-like configuration emerges naturally. Although the flare-out condition implies null-energy-condition violation at the throat, the inhomogeneous framework allows its radial distribution to be controlled. Our results establish a systematic connection between polytropic matter and wormhole geometry, providing a flexible framework for constructing compact wormholes with localized exotic matter.

</details>


### [106] [Astrophysical Graviton Squeezing Can Be Hidden in the Far-Field](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17015)
*Cheng-Jun Fang,Zong-Kuan Guo,Zhen-Hong Lyu,Jing Shu,Yu-Heng Sun,Zi-Zheng Zhou*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文利用空间量子光学方法研究宏观引力子压缩态（graviton squeezing）在远场探测器中的传播动力学，发现同光锥测量存在严重的运动学冲突，导致局部量子相干性被高度抑制，使得宏观压缩态对单个探测器呈现经典热态。结果表明全局压缩并不保证局部可观测性，可测量的量子信号远弱于由整体压缩参数所预期的强度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 局域化的天体物理源可以产生宏观引力子压缩态，但其在远场探测器上的可观测量子特征（quantum signature）此前尚未得到解决。作者旨在探究这些压缩态在传播后，局部观测者所能探测到的关联函数是否仍保留量子性质。

Method: 采用空间量子光学（spatial quantum optics）方法研究压缩态的传播动力学，计算局部观测者可访问的关联函数（correlation functions），并分析同光锥（same-cone）测量设置下的量子相干性。

Result: 发现在同光锥测量中存在严重的运动学冲突（kinematic conflict），高度抑制局部量子相干性；这些宏观压缩态在单个探测器看来表现为经典热态（classically thermal）。全局压缩态的可观测量子特征可能显著弱于由整体压缩参数所预期的结果。

Conclusion: 全局压缩（global squeezing）并不保证局部可观测性，压缩态的整体参数不能直接反映远场局部探测器能够观测到的量子信号强度。这对天体物理源产生的压缩引力波的探测可行性提出了重要的理论限制。

Abstract: While localized astrophysical sources can generate macroscopic graviton squeezing, their observable quantum signatures at far-field detectors remain unresolved. In this work, we investigate the propagation dynamics of the squeezed states using spatial quantum optics methods to evaluate correlation functions accessible to a local observer. Crucially, we reveal a severe kinematic conflict in same-cone measurements, which highly suppresses local quantum coherence. Consequently, these macroscopically squeezed states appear classically thermal to a single detector. Our results demonstrate that global squeezing does not guarantee local observability, and the measurable quantum signatures may be significantly weaker than what would be expected from the overall squeezing parameter of the state.

</details>


### [107] [Wormhole Reconstruction in Non-Conservative Unimodular Gravity: Ricci Scalar as an Independently Prescribed Curvature Profile](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17058)
*Marcelo H. Alvarenga,Rodrigo Santos Bufalo,Júlio C. Fabris*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文在非守恒单模引力（Non-Conservative Unimodular Gravity, NUG）框架下提出一种曲率重建（curvature reconstruction）方案，无需借助物质场或度规函数，直接从预设的里奇标量（Ricci scalar）剖面构造可穿越虫洞（traversable wormhole）几何。通过幂律曲率剖面与三种红移函数组合，得到多类静态球对称虫洞解，并分析了其几何性质、渐近行为与零能条件（null energy condition）。结果表明曲率参数调控虫洞所需的奇异物质强度，曲率指数决定全局渐近结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在标准引力理论中，里奇标量与能动张量的迹之间存在代数关系，限制了从预设曲率剖面直接构建时空几何的可能性。而NUG中二者解耦，这为从曲率而非物质或度规函数出发构造时空几何提供了新的自由度与理论动机。

Method: 作者利用NUG中里奇标量与能动张量迹之间缺乏代数关系这一特性，建立曲率重建框架，将可穿越虫洞几何直接由预设曲率剖面生成。具体实现采用幂律里奇标量与三种不同红移函数（redshift function）相结合，构建出多类静态球对称虫洞解。

Result: 研究发现径向零能条件在喉部（throat）必然被违反，但无量纲曲率参数可控制支撑虫洞的奇异物质强度，曲率指数则决定重建时空的全局渐近结构。特别地，重建方案自然涌现出区分渐近平坦解与具有残余渐近形变几何的临界构型。

Conclusion: 从预设里奇标量剖面进行曲率重建，被确立为在非保守单模引力中生成紧致天体时空的可行几何框架，为构造此类引力理论中的虫洞及其他紧致时空提供了新的系统化途径。

Abstract: The absence of an algebraic relation between the Ricci scalar and the trace of the energy-momentum tensor in Non-Conservative Unimodular Gravity ($\mathrm{NUG}$) opens new possibilities for constructing spacetime geometries from prescribed curvature profiles. Exploiting this property, we develop a curvature reconstruction framework in which traversable wormhole geometries are generated directly from a prescribed curvature profile rather than from the matter sector or the metric functions. The reconstruction procedure is implemented for a power-law Ricci scalar combined with three different redshift functions, leading to distinct classes of static and spherically symmetric wormhole solutions. We investigate their geometrical properties, asymptotic behavior and null energy condition. Although the radial null energy condition remains necessarily violated at the throat, we show that the dimensionless curvature parameter controls the intensity of the exotic matter supporting the wormhole, while the curvature exponent determines the global asymptotic structure of the reconstructed spacetime. In particular, the critical configuration separating asymptotically flat solutions from geometries with residual asymptotic deformation naturally emerges from the reconstruction scheme. These results establish curvature reconstruction from prescribed Ricci scalar profiles as a viable geometrical framework for generating compact-object spacetimes within non-conservative unimodular gravity.

</details>


### [108] [Exact black hole solutions with semi-symmetric vectorial torsion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17098)
*Lehel Csillag,Tiberiu Harko*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文在半对称度规引力（semi-symmetric metric gravity）框架下，通过引入带矢量扭转（torsion）的半对称联络扩展广义相对论，推导出两个精确真空解，并通过太阳系经典检验约束新长度尺度，同时验证了所得黑洞解的线性稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 广义相对论在极端尺度下存在局限，需要扩展引力理论以统一描述大尺度宇宙学与强场区域的黑洞物理。半对称度规引力作为一种带有矢量扭转的广义相对论扩展框架，其具体解和可观测后果尚未被充分探索。

Method: 在假设与球对称相容的纯径向扭转向量的条件下，求解修正场方程以获得两个精确真空解；随后利用经典太阳系检验（近日点进动、光线偏折、雷达回波延迟）约束新引入的长度尺度参数；并研究所得黑洞解的热力学性质及矢量扰动扇区的线性稳定性。

Result: 推导出两个精确真空解，度规依赖于两个积分常数，分别定义史瓦西半径（Schwarzschild radius）和新的宇宙学类型长度尺度。太阳系检验可用于约束该长度尺度及与广义相对论的偏差。所得黑洞解的热力学性质被研究，且矢量扰动扇区呈线性稳定。

Conclusion: 半对称度规引力能够产生包含新长度尺度的真空解，该尺度可通过太阳系观测加以约束，所得黑洞解在热力学与线性扰动意义上均具备自洽性。

Abstract: Semi-Symmetric metric gravity extends general relativity by introducing a semi-symmetric connection with vectorial torsion. The modified field equations contain extra contributions from the torsion vector and its derivatives. Assuming a purely radial torsion vector consistent with spherical symmetry, we derive two exact vacuum solutions. The metric depends on two integration constants, defining the Schwarzschild radius and a new cosmological-type length scale. Classical Solar System tests, including perihelion precession, light deflection, and radar echo delay, are used to constrain the new length scale and the associated deviations from general relativity. The thermodynamic properties of the resulting black hole solutions are investigated, and it is shown that the vector perturbation sector is linearly stable.

</details>


### [109] [Rational regular black holes in non-polynomial gravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17158)
*Aimeric Colléaux*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究d≥4维非多项式纯引力理论，这类理论的二维球对称约化在短距离上极小程度地偏离广义相对论。这些理论满足Birkhoff定理，并存在具有任意多个耦合参数的精确黑洞解，其解在熵中含有对数领头修正，且在牛顿势中含有量子化修正。利用阶数p>d/2的不变量可以构造正则黑洞而无需无限阶修正塔；通过特定构造可使黑洞满足Nernst热力学第三定律，并研究了Maxwell场、非最小耦合项以及无限修正塔下的准正则与正则黑洞性质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该研究关注量子引力修正下的黑洞物理学，特别是如何在保持广义相对论在经典尺度上行为的同时，利用非多项式修正实现黑洞的正则化，并研究相关热力学性质（包括Nernst第三定律）以及奇异性（如库仑奇点）的消除。

Method: 研究从d≥4维非多项式纯引力理论入手，通过二维球对称约化方法确保短距离行为对广义相对论的偏离极小。利用正则化的临界阶d=2p理论与偶数维空间中与二维轨道空间欧拉示性数（Euler characteristic）的联系，构造精确黑洞解；通过选择任意阶p>d/2的不变量实现黑洞正则化，并引入Maxwell场与非最小耦合项处理电荷奇异性；对无限修正塔情形构造准正则与正则（A）dS核黑洞。

Result: 这些黑洞解具有对数型熵修正和量子化的牛顿势修正；在偶数维中，解的光滑性对修正数目呈线性依赖且无需精细调参。构造了一类以极端黑洞形变的（A）dS₄或M₄真空为唯一无质量真空的理论，其熵为零从而满足Nernst第三定律。Maxwell场不破坏度规正则性，非最小耦合可正则化电场。带电荷时，在满足M > αℓ + βQ²/ℓ不等式条件下，两类黑洞可阻止或抑制质量膨胀（mass inflation）；准正则黑洞无需非最小耦合即可解决库仑奇点。

Conclusion: 本文展示了非多项式纯引力理论能够在不引入无限修正塔的前提下构造正则黑洞，兼顾热力学一致性（Nernst第三定律）与奇点消除（包括库仑奇点），表明此类理论是研究量子引力效应下黑洞物理的可行框架。

Abstract: We consider the $d\geq 4$ non-polynomial pure gravity theories defined so that their 2D reduction to spherical symmetry minimally modifies that of General Relativity at small distances. They satisfy Birkhoff theorem and admit exact black hole solutions for an arbitrary number of couplings. These solutions have a logarithmic leading correction to the entropy and a quantum-like correction to the Newtonian potential, both arising from the regularised critical order $d=2p$ theory, which is related in even dimensions to the Euler characteristic of the 2D orbit space. These black holes can be made regular, without the need for infinite towers of corrections, using invariants of any order $p>d/2$. In even dimensions, their smoothness is linear in the number of corrections and does not require fine-tuning. We construct a class of theories admitting as unique (massless) vacuum an extremal black hole deformation of (A)dS$_4$ or M$_4$ with vanishing entropy, so that Nernst's third law of thermodynamics is satisfied without discontinuity in the entropy. We show that a Maxwell field does not disrupt the regularity of the metric, and that simple non-minimal couplings in the action enable to regularise the electric field. Considering infinite towers of corrections, we construct quasi-regular black holes with one horizon as well as regular (A)dS-core ones with a near-extremal inner horizon for any mass. When charged, these two types of black holes prevent or tame the mass inflation provided the mass and charge satisfy an inequality of the form $M > α\ell + βQ^2 / \ell$, where $α$ and $β$ are numerical factors depending on the specific model, while $\ell$ is the length scale controlling the high-energy corrections. The quasi-regular black holes are sufficient to resolve the Coulomb singularity without the need for non-minimal couplings.

</details>


### [110] [Removability criterion for radiative corrections to the Starobinsky attractor in weakly nonlocal gravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17215)
*Ekapob Kulchoakrungsun,Phongpichit Channuie,Daris Samart*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究百分比量级的辐射修正如何将 α-吸引子（α-attractor）和 Starobinsky 类模型的预测移至 ACT DR6 所偏好的 (n_s, r) 平面区域。作者提出关键判据：修正是否"可移除"（removable）而非修正的大小，并通过 (V/V')Δ'=常数 的准则区分可保留主导预测的变形。在弱非局域 R+R² 理论中，单圈流将模型演化至 E 模型族，而物质诱导修正及 R^n 项（n≠2）均无法通过该判据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着 ACT DR6 等宇宙学观测数据对原初涨落参数空间 (n_s, r) 的精确约束，百分比量级的辐射修正足以将 α-吸引子与 Starobinsky 类模型的预测移入观测偏好区域，因此需要系统性地理解哪些修正是物理上可接受的。作者旨在建立一套判据，区分可保留模型主导预测能力的修正与不可保留的变形。

Method: 作者在慢滚 N 形式主义（slow-roll N-formalism）中证明，可观测量是 ε(N) 的泛函，ε(N) 在区间上的相等性可将势能重建到振幅重标度与规范场平移的程度。通过引入 (V/V')Δ'=常数 的判据来筛选可保留主导预测的更大类变形。随后将该判据应用于弱非局域 R+R² 完成理论、R^n 项变形以及物质诱导修正等具体案例进行分析。

Result: 研究表明，弱非局域 R+R² 完成理论中，改进尺度 μ=H(φ) 使重整化群时间等于树势对数的二分之一，局部单圈流可通过判据并将模型演化至 E 模型族 V∝(1−e^{−√(2/3)φ/mpl})^p（p=2+K）。在 n_s 中诱导的偏移量级为 O(ln N/N²)，其中可移除部分有闭合形式解。物质诱导修正在领头阶失败，R^n 项仅 n=2 可移除。对标准模型物质，可移除通道由希格斯非最小耦合唯一确定，量级为 O(10^{−11})。

Conclusion: 决定辐射修正能否成功移动预测的核心属性是"可移除性"而非修正幅度。e 折叠数（e-folds）未被判据保护，以 22% 量级并带相反符号贡献。任何提高 n_s 的变形均不满足可移除判据，而标准模型物质的修正通道因 R² 系数的观测约束而被固定为极小量级。

Abstract: Radiative corrections at the percent level can shift the predictions of $α$-attractor and Starobinsky-like models into the region of the $(n_s,r)$ plane preferred by ACT DR6. We show that the decisive property is not the size of a correction but whether it is removable. In the slow-roll $N$-formalism the observables are functionals of $\eps(N)$, and equality of $\eps(N)$ on an interval reconstructs the potential up to an amplitude rescaling and a shift of the canonical field. A larger class, selected by $(V/V')Δ'={\rm const}$, preserves the leading predictions and provides a criterion for any proposed deformation. In weakly nonlocal completions of $R+R^{2}$ the improvement scale $μ=H(φ)$ makes the renormalization-group time one half of the logarithm of the tree potential, so the local one-loop flow passes the test and evolves the model onto the E-model family $V\propto(1-e^{-\sqrt{2/3}\,φ/\mpl})^{p}$ with $p=2+K$. Since the divergent local beta functions of these super-renormalizable completions are one-loop exact, no higher-loop local running modifies this picture. The induced shifts are $\ord(\ln N/N^{2})$ in $n_s$, obtained in closed form for the removable part and numerically for the rest. The number of $e$-folds, which the criterion does not protect, contributes at the \red{$22\%$} level with an opposite sign. Matter-induced corrections fail the same test at leading order, which is why they shift the predictions. Among the deformations proposed since ACT DR6, an $R^{n}$ term in $f(R)$ gives $(V/V')Δ'=\ord(N^{n-1})$, so only $n=2$ is removable, and no deformation that increases $n_s$ satisfies the criterion. For Standard Model matter, the removable channel is fixed by the Higgs nonminimal coupling alone and is $\ord(10^{-11})$, because the measured amplitude sets the coefficient of $R^{2}$ to $5.1\times10^{8}$.

</details>


### [111] [Ostrogradsky's Theorem is Incompatible with Background Independence in Quantum Gravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17364)
*Ken-ji Hamada*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文论证Ostrogradsky定理不适用于引力系统，因为引力系统的哈密顿约束严格成立，使总哈密顿量消失；满足此约束的鬼模是构建时空不可或缺的组成部分。但弱场（引力子）近似作为依赖背景的方案会调用该定理，因此仅允许在普朗克尺度以下（二阶导数项占主导）的区域内使用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Ostrogradsky定理表明高阶导数动力学系统必然产生从下方无界的鬼模，这对高阶引力理论构成严峻挑战；作者试图论证该定理对引力系统并不适用，从而为构造时空提供理论依据。

Method: 通过对Ostrogradsky定理适用条件的分析，论证引力系统的哈密顿约束（Hamiltonian constraint）体现背景无关性（background independence），严格约束总哈密顿量为零，使鬼模受此约束约束而成为构造时空的必要组件；并进一步分析弱场（引力子）近似的适用范围。

Result: Ostrogradsky定理不适用于引力系统；同时指出弱场近似作为依赖背景（background-dependent）的方案会重新调用该定理，故仅能在普朗克尺度（Planck scale）以下、二阶导数项占优的区域内使用。

Conclusion: 在引力背景下，鬼模在哈密顿约束下并非病态自由度，而是构建时空结构的关键成分；背景无关框架与背景依赖的弱场近似之间存在明确的适用边界。

Abstract: Ostrogradsky's theorem shows that higher-derivative dynamical systems inevitably yields a Hamiltonian featuring a ghost mode that is unbounded from below. However, we argue that this theorem is inapplicable to gravitational systems, as the Hamiltonian constraint, which encodes background independence by dictating that the total Hamiltonian vanishes, holds strictly. The ghost mode obeying this constraint constitutes an indispensable component in the construction of space and time.
  Nevertheless, it should be underscored that the use of the weak-field (graviton) approximation, being a background-dependent scheme presupposing the existence of absolute time, invokes this theorem; consequently, it is allowed only in the domain below the Planck scale, where second derivative terms dominate.

</details>


### [112] [Vacuum Polarization in Quantum-Corrected and Effective Black Hole Geometries: a High Performance Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17670)
*Antonino Flachi*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文计算了五种正则且有效的静态球对称量子修正黑洞外部的哈特尔-霍金态（Hartle–Hawking state）下重标定标量真空极化（vacuum polarization）⟨φ²⟩，采用扩展的点分裂模式求和形式并开发了高性能数值代码实现。研究发现五种几何在视界处展现出定性不同的响应，变形印记由背景里奇标量（Ricci scalar）通过德威特-施温格（DeWitt–Schwinger）曲率项的符号决定，并在共形耦合（conformal coupling）下被强烈抑制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子引力修正的黑洞几何提供了研究量子场在弯曲时空中行为的自然平台，而此前对不同量子修正黑洞模型的真空极化行为缺乏系统性比较研究。本文旨在通过统一的数值框架，揭示不同量子引力方案（圈量子引力、重整化群改进、量子变形等）对黑洞外部真空极化的不同影响。

Method: 采用适应化的扩展点分裂模式求和形式（extended point-splitting mode-sum formalism），实现为高性能数值代码。在量子变形参数、场质量 μ=mM 和曲率耦合参数 ξ 构成的网格上，计算五种黑洞几何（Kazakov–Solodukhin量子变形黑洞、Zhang–Lewandowski–Ma–Yang的两种有效圈量子引力几何、Bonanno–Reuter的重整化群改进史瓦西黑洞，以及Bardeen正则黑洞）外部的真空极化轮廓。

Result: 五种几何在视界处表现出定性不同的响应。所有几何中变形印记由背景里奇标量通过线性于 (ξ−1/6)R 的德威特–施温格曲率项的符号控制，并在共形耦合时被强烈抑制。在具有内视界的两种几何的近极端区域中，轻场质量下视界极化出现符号翻转，该现象在匹配温度下的Reissner–Nordström中不存在，且被共形耦合移除，识别出一个本质上由德西特核心驱动的量子几何领域。所有结果在经典极限下均还原为史瓦西值。

Conclusion: 量子修正黑洞的真空极化行为强烈依赖具体量子引力模型，其变形印记可通过(ξ−1/6)R项的符号系统理解。近极端区域发现的视界极化符号翻转标识了一个独特的量子几何效应，为区分不同量子引力方案提供了可检验的物理信号。

Abstract: We compute the renormalized scalar vacuum polarization $\langle φ^2 \rangle$ of a massive, non-minimally coupled quantum field in the Hartle--Hawking state exterior to a family of static, spherically symmetric quantum-corrected, effective and regular black holes: the Kazakov--Solodukhin quantum-deformed black hole, the two effective loop-quantum-gravity geometries of Zhang--Lewandowski--Ma--Yang, the renormalization-group improved Schwarzschild black hole of Bonanno--Reuter, and the Bardeen regular black hole. We adapt the extended point-splitting mode-sum formalism, implemented as a high-performance code, and present the exterior profile of the vacuum polarization over a grid of the quantum-deformation parameter, the field mass $μ=mM$ and the curvature coupling $ξ$. The five geometries display qualitatively distinct horizon responses. In all five geometries the deformation imprint is governed by the sign of the background Ricci scalar through the DeWitt--Schwinger curvature term linear in $(ξ-1/6)\mathcal{R}$, and is strongly suppressed at conformal coupling. We verify this both in magnitude and in the $ξ$-dependence. In the near-extremal regime of the two geometries with inner horizons we find a sign change of the horizon polarization at light field mass -- absent for Reissner--Nordström at matched temperature and removed by conformal coupling -- identifying a genuinely quantum-geometric, de-Sitter-core-driven regime. Every result reduces to the Schwarzschild value in the classical limit, validating the calculation.

</details>


### [113] [Thermomechanics of Horndeski Scalar Hair with Spacelike Gradients](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17730)
*Marcello Miranda,Marco Sebastianutti,Andrea Giusti*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文为可行霍恩德斯基（Horndeski）标量毛（scalar hair）构建了一个热力-力学（thermomechanical）框架，采用(1+1+2)形式化方法并利用有效非理想流体分解，在观测者依赖和观测者无关两种途径下推导了有效介质的本构关系，识别的条件表明该标量毛具有热力-力学解释，并与各向异性/分层介质类比，最后将该框架与Tolman–Ehrenfest热平衡判据联系起来。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对具有类空标量场梯度（spacelike scalar-field gradients）的可行Horndeski标量毛缺乏系统性的热力-力学分析，需要建立统一的框架来表征其有效介质的热力学与力学性质。

Method: 将(1+1+2)形式化方法适配到标量场构型，对模型施加有效非理想流体分解（effective imperfect-fluid decomposition），分别通过观测者依赖和观测者无关两种途径推导有效介质的本构关系（constitutive relations），并将其专门化到静态球对称构型。

Result: 观测者依赖的途径将有效介质类比于具有优先空间方向的各向异性介质；观测者无关的途径则显示其与分层介质（layered media）的相似性。在静态球对称且标量毛仅依赖面积半径（areal radius）的情形下，该框架可与Tolman–Ehrenfest热平衡判据建立联系。

Conclusion: 本文建立了Horndeski标量毛的热力-力学解释条件，揭示其在不同观测视角下分别呈现各向异性介质与分层介质的特征，并确认在特定对称性下该框架与经典热平衡判据相容。

Abstract: We develop a thermomechanical framework for viable Horndeski scalar hair with spacelike scalar-field gradients. Adapting the $(1{+}1{+}2)$ formalism to the scalar configuration, we apply the effective imperfect-fluid decomposition to the model and derive general results governing its thermomechanical properties. Specifically, we infer the constitutive relations for the effective medium, in both observer-dependent and observer-independent approaches, thereby identifying the conditions under which the scalar hair admits thermomechanical interpretations. The observer-dependent approach leads to an analogy with anisotropic media characterized by a preferred spatial direction, whereas the observer-independent view shows similarities with layered media. The general results are then specialized to static, spherically symmetric configurations with scalar hair depending solely on the areal radius. Interestingly, this scenario allows us to connect the thermomechanical framework with the Tolman--Ehrenfest criterion for thermal equilibrium.

</details>


### [114] [Gravitational Waves from Green's Function Decomposition for a Kerr black hole: I. Equatorial ISCO Plunge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17943)
*Junquan Su,Neev Khera,Abhishek Chowdhuri,Marc Casals,Huan Yang*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文在时域（time domain）中给出了Kerr黑洞格林函数（Green's function）的分解方案，将其划分为准正则模（quasinormal mode）贡献、直接部分（direct part）和晚期尾部（late-time tail），并通过时域Teukolsky数值模拟验证了该分解的准确性，进一步将其应用于测试粒子坠入Kerr黑洞的模型问题，获得辐射波和准正则模的激发结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有工作曾在频域（frequency domain）中对史瓦西极限（Schwarzschild limit）下的格林函数进行分解，本文希望将类似分解推广至Kerr黑洞的时域情形，以更好地理解并刻画黑洞摄动中不同物理成分的贡献。

Method: 本文在时域中对Kerr格林函数进行分解，识别出准正则模贡献、直接部分和晚期尾部三个成分；分解时间（split times）由黑洞自旋（black hole spin）以及发射源与接收者（emitter and receiver）的位置决定。通过时域Teukolsky数值模拟验证分解正确性，并将分解后的格林函数与测试粒子的源项做卷积，应用于测试粒子坠入Kerr黑洞的模型问题。

Result: 所提出的时域Kerr格林函数分解与时域Teukolsky数值模拟结果高度一致，验证了分解的有效性。在测试粒子坠入Kerr黑洞的模型问题中，通过格林函数与源项卷积，成功获得了动态激发的直接波和准正则模信号。

Conclusion: 该时域分解方法可以视为旋转黑洞振铃（ringdown）现象关于质量比的第一阶近似描述，为后续研究Kerr黑洞在外部摄动下的辐射机制提供了有效工具。

Abstract: We present a decomposition of the Kerr Green's function in the time domain, motivated by the frequency-domain split previously studied in the Schwarzschild limit. We show that the identification of a quasinormal-mode contribution, a direct part, and a late-time tail is still available, where the split times are determined by the black hole spin and positions of the emitter and receiver. We have checked this Green's function with time-domain Teukolsky numerical simulations and find excellent agreement. We also apply this decomposed Green's function in the time domain to a model problem with a test particle plunging into a Kerr black hole. The dynamically excited direct wave and quasinormal modes are obtained by convoluting the Green's function with the particle's source term, which may be viewed as the first order in mass ratio of a spinning black hole ringdown.

</details>

### [115] [Controlling artificial surface heating in neutron star simulations: Application to hybrid equations of state](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16945)
*Georgios Doulis*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文研究了基于熵的通量限制（entropy-based flux-limiting, EFL）方案在双中子星并合仿真中对数值表面加热的抑制效果，利用有限差分代码BAM对孤立中子星及双中子星旋近系统进行了混合状态方程下的模拟，结果表明EFL方案在所有恒星模型和双星构型中均能显著减少人工表面加热。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 双中子星模拟中恒星表面的处理对数值演化的精度和预言可观测量的物理可靠性至关重要，而表面陡峭梯度处理产生的数值伪影会在旋近期间引起虚假加热，导致中子星内部能量人工增加和非物理膨胀，因此需要有效方案来缓解这一数值问题。

Method: 在有限差分代码BAM中调查熵基通量限制（EFL）方案的效能，对孤立中子星和双中子星旋近并合系统进行模拟，采用一组代表性的混合状态方程（hybrid equations of state）作为热力学输入。

Result: EFL方案在所有考察的恒星模型和双星配置中均一致地显著减少了人工表面加热，证明数值加热的抑制是EFL方法稳健的特征。

Conclusion: 熵基通量限制方案是缓解双中子星模拟中表面数值加热的有效手段，其结果不依赖于具体恒星模型或双星构型，具有广泛的适用性。

Abstract: The treatment of the stellar surface in binary neutron star simulations is crucial for the accuracy of the numerical evolution and the physical reliability of the predicted observables. Numerical artifacts associated with the treatment of steep gradients near the stellar surface can produce spurious heating during the inspiral, leading to an artificial increase of the internal energy and an unphysical expansion of the neutron star. In this work, we investigate the effectiveness of the entropy-based flux-limiting (EFL) scheme in mitigating these numerical effects within the finite-difference code BAM. We perform simulations of both isolated neutron stars and binary neutron star inspirals employing a representative set of hybrid equations of state. We show that the EFL scheme significantly reduces artificial surface heating. This reduction is observed consistently across all stellar models and binary configurations considered, demonstrating that the reduction of numerical heating is a robust feature of the EFL method.

</details>


### [116] [Extreme-Mass-Ratio Inspirals in Gaseous Disks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17003)
*Alexander J. Dittmann,Abhishek Hegade K. R.,Callum W. Fairbairn*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文基于线性理论对极质量比旋进（extreme mass ratio inspiral, EMRI）与吸积盘之间的流体动力学相互作用进行了严格的相对论分析，推导了控制旋涡密度波演化的拉格朗日量，建立了稳定的数值方法计算角动量转移，并系统探讨了黑洞自旋、轨道半长轴、盘标高等参数对相互作用的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: EMRI是探测其绕行超大质量黑洞（supermassive black hole, SMBH）环境的精确探针，但吸积盘对EMRI的流体动力学转矩效应尚未被严格认识。作者希望通过相对论分析量化盘-EMRI相互作用留下的引力波信号印记，为利用激光干涉空间天线（Laser Interferometer Space Antenna, LISA）观测约束吸积盘性质提供理论途径。

Method: 采用线性理论（linear theory）对圆轨道EMRI与薄吸积盘之间的流体动力学相互作用进行相对论分析。首先推导控制盘中旋涡密度波（spiral density waves）演化的拉格朗日量，以此建立角动量在EMRI与盘之间转移的平衡律；随后开发一个稳定的数值方案以处理共转共振（corotation resonances），并在薄盘中求解直至很大方位角数目的模态解。

Result: 研究发现当次级目标轨道位于SMBH约25个史瓦西半径（Schwarzschild radii）之内时，相对论效应会显著改变盘-EMRI相互作用。数值结果与近期解析模型的对比表明，对于有限厚度吸积盘，压力梯度和引力势软化（softening）的影响很重要，无法通过简单调整解析模型中的转矩截断参数（torque cutoff parameters）来捕捉。

Conclusion: 本文建立的相对论框架可用于分析EMRI的形成场景，并为构建盘-EMRI相互作用的相对论精确波形模型提供基础，从而为未来LISA观测中利用引力波约束吸积盘性质铺平道路。

Abstract: Gravitational waves from extreme mass ratio inspirals (EMRIs) are precise probes of the environment of the supermassive black holes (SMBHs) they orbit. If an SMBH is actively accreting, the surrounding gaseous disk can impart hydrodynamic torques on and assist the formation of EMRIs within it. Such disk-EMRI interactions could leave measurable imprints on future observations by the Laser Interferometer Space Antenna (LISA), and potentially provide a route to constrain disk properties using gravitational wave observations. We present herein a detailed relativistic analysis of these hydrodynamic interactions using linear theory. We first derive a Lagrangian governing the evolution of spiral density waves in the disk and use it to formulate a balance law for the transfer of angular momentum between the EMRI and disk. We then develop a stable numerical scheme which can be used to treat corotation resonances and find modal solutions in thin disks up to very large azimuthal numbers. Using this numerical scheme, we explore how SMBH spins, EMRI semi-major axes, disk scale heights, sound speed gradients, and surface density gradients affect the interaction between accretion disks and circular EMRIs. Our results show that relativistic effects substantially alter disk-EMRI interactions once the secondary orbit is within $\mathcal{O}(25)$ Schwarzschild radii from the SMBH. Comparing our numerical results with recent analytical models suggests that the impact of pressure gradients and softening of the gravitational potential is important for disks with finite thickness and cannot be captured by tuning the torque cutoff parameters in the analytical models. The framework provided here will help analyze the formation scenarios of EMRIs and build relativistically accurate waveform models of disk-EMRI interactions.

</details>


### [117] [Modelling mountains on accreting magnetized neutron stars](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17508)
*T. Brusco,B. Haskell,M. Razzano,M. Bejger,J. L. Zdunik*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文提出一种计算吸积中子星四极形变的新模型，首次同时纳入极向磁场、深层地壳加热与地壳弹性的耦合效应，预测的椭率（eccentricity）可达ε~10⁻¹¹，对应可被下一代地面引力波探测器（如爱因斯坦望远镜和宇宙探测器）探及的引力波应变，但低于当前LIGO、Virgo和KAGRA的灵敏度，与现有观测非检测结果一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低质量X射线双星（Low Mass X-ray Binaries）中吸积中子星产生的连续引力波（continuous gravitational waves）是当前及下一代地面探测器的主要搜寻目标之一，可靠的信源预测需要同时考虑磁应力、吸积加热与地壳弹性响应的联合效应，而此前尚无自洽模型整合这些因素。

Method: 该模型通过求解地壳中的线性化形变方程组来计算四极形变，首次纳入极向磁场、深层地壳加热与地壳弹性的耦合效应；其中罗伦兹力密度和热致密度变化驱动的结构扰动被显式求解，地壳考虑完整弹性响应，而海洋和核心则处理为正压流体（barotropic fluid）。

Result: 模型识别出一个阈值吸积率（threshold accretion rate），其取值依赖于地壳微观物理与核心的超流隙（superfluid gaps）；在该阈值之上，磁应力与不对称吸积驱动相反符号的形变，而低于该阈值时两者角色反转。预测的椭率最高达ε~10⁻¹¹，对应特征引力波应变可被下一代探测器探及，但一般低于当前LIGO、Virgo和KAGRA干涉仪的灵敏度。

Conclusion: 上述结果与近期观测活动对低质量X射线双星中吸积中子星连续引力波的非检测结果一致，同时强调了建立可靠模型对于理解引力波发射在这些系统中的影响以及为未来搜索选择相关目标的重要性。

Abstract: Continuous gravitational waves from accreting neutron stars in Low Mass X-ray Binaries are one of the main targets for current and next generation ground based detectors. In order to select the most promising astrophysical sources, however, reliable predictions for the signals are required, and it is therefore necessary to develop models that consistently account for the combined effects of magnetic stresses, accretion-induced heating, and the elastic response of the crust.}{We present a model for computing the quadrupolar deformation, incorporating for the first time the coupled effects of a poloidal magnetic field, deep crustal heating, and crustal elasticity. Perturbations to the star's structure driven by the Lorentz force density and by thermally-induced density variations are computed by solving a system of linearised deformation equations in the crust, for which we consider the full elastic response, while the ocean and core treated as barotropic fluids. We identify a threshold accretion rate whose value depends on crustal microphysics and the superfluid gaps in the core, above which magnetic stresses and asymmetric accretion drive deformations of opposite sign, while below this threshold their roles are reversed. The predicted eccentricities reach magnitudes up to $\varepsilon\sim 10^{-11}$, corresponding to characteristic gravitational-wave strains accessible to next-generation detectors such as the Einstein Telescope or Cosmic Explorer, but generally below the sensitivity of current LIGO, Virgo and KAGRA interferometers. These results are consistent with the non-detection of continuous gravitational waves from accreting neutron stars in Low Mass X-ray Binaries in recent observational campaigns, but highlight the need of reliable models to understand the impact of gravitational wave emission in these systems and select relevant targets for future searches.

</details>

### [118] [A geodesic distance interpretation of Lanczos coefficients](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16982)
*Le-Chen Qu*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了大N随机矩阵理论中无限温度热场双态（thermofield double state）的扩展复杂度（spread complexity），首次建立了Lanczos系数与平面映射（planar maps）生成函数之间的联系，并提出了名为"Lanczos方程"的非线性关系组，揭示了扩展复杂度加速算符与测地线生成算符之间的定量对应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩展复杂度是量子混沌和全息原理中一个重要的动力学诊断量，但其深层数学结构和物理意义尚未完全明确。本文旨在通过随机矩阵理论框架，深入理解Lanczos系数的物理含义，并建立其与组合数学中平面映射计数的联系。

Method: 作者从库仑气体方程（Coulomb-gas equation）出发，推导出一组关于Lanczos系数的非线性关系（称为Lanczos方程）。利用正交多项式理论，将Lanczos系数与平面映射的生成函数建立联系；对偶势情形，证明了差分b_{n+1}²-b_n²生成带两条标记腿的平面映射，其标记腿之间的分离距离恰为测地线距离n。作者在四次矩阵模型和双重缩放的SYK模型（double-scaled Sachdev-Ye-Kitaev model）中验证了这些结果。

Result: 研究得出三个主要结果：其一，Lanczos系数在Krylov指标较大时趋于常数，导致扩展复杂度在晚期呈线性增长；其二，Lanczos系数可作为平面映射的生成函数，且扩展复杂度加速算符等于两倍的测地线生成算符；其三，在四次矩阵模型中，展开与四价平面映射计数对应，双重缩放SYK模型中辅助希尔伯特空间可等同于物理弦希尔伯特空间。

Conclusion: 本文揭示了扩展复杂度与随机矩阵理论、组合数学中平面映射计数之间的深层联系，为理解量子混沌动力学提供了新的数学框架，并为全息原理中的测地线信息与量子动力学算符之间的对应提供了定量基础。

Abstract: We study the spread complexity of the infinite-temperature thermofield double state in large-$N$ random matrix theory and investigate the physical meaning of its Lanczos coefficients. The leading density of states defines a family of orthogonal polynomials whose recursion coefficients coincide with the Lanczos coefficients of the associated Krylov dynamics. Starting from the Coulomb-gas equation, we derive a pair of nonlinear relations for these coefficients, which we call the Lanczos equations. Since the Lanczos coefficients approach constants at large Krylov index, the spread complexity grows linearly at late times. We further show that the Lanczos coefficients can be interpreted as generating functions for planar maps. For an even potential, the difference $b_{n+1}^2-b_n^2$ generates maps with two legs whose marked legs are separated by the exact geodesic distance $n$. Consequently, the spread complexity acceleration operator equals twice the geodesic generating operator. We illustrate these results in the quartic matrix model, where the expansion in the quartic coupling counts tetravalent planar maps, and in the double-scaled Sachdev--Ye--Kitaev model, where the auxiliary Hilbert space can be identified with the physical chord Hilbert space.

</details>


### [119] [Pure gravity OPE density for genus-two handlebody in $\text{AdS}_3$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16985)
*Saptaswa Ghosh,Sounak Pal*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文通过庞加莱构造（Poincaré construction）直接对配分函数（partition function）求逆，解析计算了亏格二（genus-two）环柄体（handlebody）的算符乘积展开密度（OPE density）的钉扎参数（pinching parameter）一阶和二阶修正，并验证了该结果与极端共形场论（extremal conformal field theory）对偶关系的一致性。研究发现，亏格一下存在的OPE密度负性病理（negativity pathology）在亏格二中继续存在，且奇偶自旋态的OPE密度在黑体阈值（black-hole threshold）上方的窄带内仍为负值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该研究旨在利用亏格二环柄体与极端共形场论的二元性，计算超阈值（above-threshold）态的算符乘积展开密度，探究在更高亏格下OPE密度负性病理是否持续存在，从而深化对极端CFT中黑体阈值附近状态结构的理解。

Method: 采用庞加莱构造（Poincaré construction）方法，通过直接对配分函数求逆（direct inversion of the partition function），解析确定钉扎参数下亏格二OPE密度的一阶和二阶修正。作为一致性检验，在同一框架下计算亏格一密度，并与光锥自举分析（lightcone bootstrap）结果对比验证。

Result: 亏格一密度与1916.04184中的光锥自举结果完全一致。亏格二下，尽管出现了非平凡的反演核（inversion kernel），OPE密度的负性病理仍然存在：奇偶自旋态的OPE密度在黑体阈值上方很窄的频带内保持为负。此外，分析了OPE密度从大的正值到大的负值的尖锐转变，该转变跨越密度为零的曲线。

Conclusion: OPE密度的负性病理并非亏格一所特有，而是持续存在于亏格二的情形，表明该病理可能是极端CFT中更高亏格结构的普遍特征。这一发现对理解极端CFT在阈值附近的状态谱具有重要含义。

Abstract: In this work, we compute the operator product expansion density for the genus-two handlebody by exploiting its duality with extremal conformal field theories arXiv:0710.2129. Using a Poincaré construction, we analytically determine the first- and second-order corrections in the pinching parameter to the genus-two OPE density through a direct inversion of the partition function. As a consistency check, we also compute the genus-one density within the same Poincaré framework and find perfect agreement with the result obtained from the lightcone bootstrap analysis arXiv: 1906.04184. It is known that, at genus one, the OPE density of extremal CFTs exhibits a negativity pathology for states lying just above the black-hole threshold. We show that this pathology continues to persist at genus two, despite the emergence of a nontrivial inversion kernel. In particular, the OPE density associated with states of both odd and even spin $j_i$ remains negative within a very narrow band above the black-hole threshold. We further analyze the sharp transition from large positive to large negative values of the OPE density across a curve along which the density vanishes.

</details>


### [120] [Bulk Criticality and Boundary Spectra in AdS from Matrix Product States](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16992)
*Faizan Bhat*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文利用基于矩阵乘积态（matrix product states）的张量网络方法，研究了反德西特（AdS）空间中的相互作用标量量子场论。作者在AdS_2中非微扰地计算了标量φ^4理论的低能边界标度维数，定位了体态Z_2对称性破缺相变，发现其临界指数和中心荷与二维Ising普适类一致，并在临界点识别出Z_2保持的自由/普通Ising共形边界条件，从而同时刻画了体态与边界普适类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在AdS空间中研究相互作用量子场论通常面临微扰方法失效或数值困难的挑战，本文旨在发展一套基于张量网络（tensor network）的非微扰方法，用于系统提取AdS空间中场理论的低能边界谱、探测体态临界行为以及识别共形边界条件，从而更完整地表征体态与边界的普适类。

Method: 采用基于矩阵乘积态（matrix product states）的张量网络方法研究AdS空间中相互作用的标量量子场论。具体发展了三种方法：(1) 从全局AdS能级中提取低能边界谱；(2) 通过对AdS半径进行有限尺寸标度（finite-size scaling）来探测体态临界性；(3) 识别共形边界条件（conformal boundary conditions）。将这些方法应用于AdS_2中的标量φ^4理论。

Result: 计算了AdS_2中φ^4理论最低非平凡的Z_2奇偶边界标度维数（非微扰结果）；定位了体态Z_2对称性破缺相变，提取的临界指数和中心荷与二维Ising普适类（2D Ising universality class）一致；在临界点确定了低能边界谱，并据此识别出Z_2保持的自由/普通Ising共形边界条件。

Conclusion: 本文成功展示了张量网络方法在AdS空间中非微扰研究相互作用场论的可行性，不仅确定了体态临界行为属于二维Ising普适类，还通过低能边界谱识别了边界普适类，同时对体态与边界的普适类做出了完整表征。

Abstract: We study interacting scalar quantum field theories in anti-de Sitter (AdS) space using a tensor-network approach based on matrix product states. We develop methods for extracting low-lying boundary spectra from global-AdS energy levels, probing bulk criticality through finite-size scaling in the AdS radius, and identifying conformal boundary conditions. Applying these methods to scalar $φ^4$ theory in $\mathrm{AdS}_2$, we compute the lowest non-trivial $\mathbb{Z}_2$-odd and even boundary scaling dimensions non-perturbatively. We then locate the bulk $\mathbb{Z}_2$ symmetry-breaking transition and extract critical exponents and central charge consistent with the 2D Ising universality class. At the critical point, we determine the low-lying boundary spectrum and use it to identify the $\mathbb{Z}_2$-preserving free/ordinary Ising conformal boundary condition, thereby characterising both the bulk and boundary universality classes.

</details>


### [121] [Who Writes the Gravitational Second Law of Thermodynamics?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16997)
*V. R. Shajiee,M. M. Sheikh-Jabbari,V. Taghiloo*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文提出将引力熵（gravitational entropy）定义为与局域横向洛伦兹推动（local transverse Lorentz boosts）相关联的显式可积表面电荷，完全绕过了对时空微分同胚（spacetime diffeomorphisms）的传统依赖。作者通过类比牛顿力学中惯性观测者与第一定律的关系，论证引力热力学第二定律应由具有特定路径参数化的自由落体观测者来表述，并证明在该类观测者视角下熵变严格非减，条件是其物质部分满足沿观测者路径的积分强能量条件（integrated strong energy condition）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 任何熵的概念都必须满足热力学第二定律，而传统基于微分同胚的引力熵定义在第二定律的验证上存在困难。

Method: 通过类比牛顿经典力学中惯性观测者由第一定律定义、第二定律由该类观测者书写的关系，作者定义了一类协变的有因果性的自由落体观测者，其测地线的非仿射参数（non-affinity）等于速度矢量场的膨胀（expansion）。在此类观测者视野下，通过研究熵的变化来证明局域第二定律。

Result: 作者证明了在自由落体观测者视角下，熵变严格非减（strictly non-decreasing），前提是物质部分满足沿观测者路径的积分强能量条件。

Conclusion: 引力热力学第二定律的自然表述者是一类具有特定路径参数化的自由落体观测者，而非任意坐标系或观测者；该证明为引力熵概念的自洽性提供了支撑。

Abstract: We define gravitational entropy as the manifestly integrable surface charge associated with local transverse Lorentz boosts, entirely bypassing the conventional reliance on spacetime diffeomorphisms. Any notion of entropy must satisfy the second law. To answer the question posed in the title, an analogy with Newtonian classical mechanics is instructive: Newton's second law of motion is written by inertial observers who are defined by the first law of mechanics. We show that the second law of gravitational thermodynamics is written by free-fall observers with path parameterization in which the non-affinity of the geodesics is equal to the expansion of their velocity vector field. We then provide a proof of the local second law by studying variations in entropy as viewed by this class of covariantly-defined causal free-fall observers. We show that the entropy variation is strictly non-decreasing, provided the matter sector satisfies the integrated strong energy condition along the observer path.

</details>


### [122] [Statistically Steady Holographic Quantum Turbulence: Hyperuniform Vortex Matter and Crossover](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17012)
*Yu-Ping An,Peng-Bo Ding,Zhen-Han Jin,Li Li*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文通过在二维全息超流体湍流中构建稳健的统计稳态，发现了拓扑电荷结构因子呈现一类无序超均匀性（Class I disordered hyperuniformity），并解决了动能谱标度争议——所谓的k^{-5/3}特征实际上是过渡区间而非真正的Kolmogorov惯性级联，表明强耦合量子湍流因缺乏宏观Onsager团簇而不存在逆级联。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长期以来，量子湍流研究面临难以维持稳健统计稳态的问题，这阻碍了对普适涡旋组织和动能标度的明确识别。构建可持续的稳态是解决标度争议、揭示量子湍流普适规律的关键前提。

Method: 采用连续朗道不稳定性驱动（continuous Landau-instability driving）在二维全息超流体湍流中构建稳态，维持约2500个涡旋以消除瞬态伪影；通过拓扑电荷结构因子S_c(k)分析和实空间二阶结构函数S_2(r)来表征系统的组织与标度行为。

Result: 拓扑电荷结构因子在k→0时遵循S_c(k)∝k^{α>1}，揭示一类无序超均匀性，这是该类型最强长程有序，也是首次在强驱动的远非平衡量子流体中观察到以拓扑缺陷为组织原则的系统。动能谱中表观的k^{-5/3}标度实际上是k^{-1}单涡旋区与k^{-3}核心区之间的窄过渡，而非真正的Kolmogorov惯性区；实空间结构函数S_2(r)∝ln r而非r^{2/3}，排除了真实惯性级联的存在。

Conclusion: 强耦合量子湍流因缺乏宏观Onsager团簇而不存在逆级联，其能量输运机制与弱耦合超流体本质上不同，确立了一种新颖的非平衡涡旋相。

Abstract: A long-standing obstacle in quantum turbulence has been the difficulty of sustaining robust statistical steady states, preventing unambiguous identification of universal vortex organization and kinetic scaling. We construct such a steady state in two-dimensional holographic superfluid turbulence by continuous Landau-instability driving, sustaining ${\sim}2500$ vortices free from transient artifacts. The topological charge structure factor $S_c(k)$ reveals Class I disordered hyperuniformity with $S_c(k)\propto k^{α>1}$ as $k\to0$, where $k$ is the wavenumber. This constitutes the strongest long-range order of its kind and its first observation in a strongly driven, far-from-equilibrium quantum fluid with topological defects as the organizing principle, establishing a novel non-equilibrium vortex phase. Exploiting this platform, we resolve the scaling controversy: the apparent $k^{-5/3}$ signature in the kinetic energy spectrum is a narrow crossover between the $k^{-1}$ single-vortex and $k^{-3}$ core regimes, not a genuine Kolmogorov inertial range. The real-space second-order structure function provides decisive evidence via $S_2(r)\propto \ln r$ where $r$ is the spatial separation, with no $r^{2/3}$ Kolmogorov scaling, ruling out a true inertial cascade. These findings reveal that strongly coupled quantum turbulence lacks an inverse cascade due to the absence of macroscopic Onsager clusters, demonstrating energy transport fundamentally distinct from weakly coupled superfluids.

</details>


### [123] [Robin boundary conditions in global AdS$_4$: exact double-trace thermodynamics and a soft-mode instability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17064)
*David A. Lowe,Juanyi Yang*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文在四维全局反德西特空间（anti-de Sitter space, AdS）中，研究了带Robin边界条件的共形耦合标量场。通过边界积分方程的精确对角化，获得了闭合形式的形变两点函数，精确计算了高斯扇区内的自由能和卡西米尔能，揭示了稳定性端点处的临界行为，并指出Robin角可作为精确可控的高斯稳定性端点控制参数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解AdS/CFT对偶中边界条件变形对体场论和边界理论的影响，特别是Robin边界条件所实现的双迹变形（double-trace deformation）如何在精确层面改变理论的性质，并探究其在稳定性端点处的临界行为。

Method: 在四维全局AdS空间中，以角度α参数化Robin边界条件。利用到爱因斯坦静态宇宙（Einstein static universe）一半的精确共形映射，将边界积分方程对角化，从而得到闭合形式的形变两点函数 Ĝ_α = Ĝ_N/(1+λĜ_N)。通过其极点提取正规模谱，利用行列式计算高斯扇区自由能，并引入三个局域边界抵消项来正则化Casimir能。

Result: 卡西米尔能（Casimir energy）在稳定性端点处呈现有限的平方根尖点（square-root cusp）。有限耦合下体的T⁴和T³项与α无关，在相对Neumann条件的差分中抵消，留下π/3·cotα·LT²作为主要α依赖项。所有非解析性来源于一个静态均匀模，在α_crit处变软；磁化率以指数γ=1发散，能隙以指数1/2关闭。超过该角度后模变为快子（tachyonic）模，稳定相需引入稳定化相互作用。平空间极限下物理耦合在固定能量下趋于零，Robin依赖仅存在于软频率扇区，可通过boost权重的亚纯Mellin变换表征。

Conclusion: Robin角提供了一个能够精确追踪的高斯稳定性端点控制参数，其临界行为与经典稳定性阈值一致。该工作进一步提出了在AdS引力中放宽边界条件是否会引起类似问题的开放性疑问。

Abstract: We consider a conformally coupled scalar field in four-dimensional global anti-de Sitter space with Robin boundary conditions, parametrized by an angle $α$. On the boundary cylinder $\mathbb{R}\times S^{2}$ these conditions realize the double-trace deformation $\tfrac12λ\!\int O^{2}$ of the dimension-one operator $O$ in the alternate quantization with $λ=\cotα/L$. Because the conformal map to one half of the Einstein static universe is exact, the boundary integral equation can be diagonalized, and the deformed two-point function follows in closed form, $\widehat{\mathcal G}_α=\widehat{\mathcal G}_{N}/(1+λ\widehat{\mathcal G}_{N})$. Its poles give the normal-mode spectrum, and its determinant gives the free energy exactly within this Gaussian sector. After three local boundary counterterms, the Casimir energy reaches the stability endpoint with a finite square-root cusp. At any finite coupling the bulk $T^{4}$ and $T^{3}$ terms are independent of $α$ and cancel in the difference from Neumann, leaving $\tfracπ{3}\cotα\,LT^{2}$ as the leading $α$-dependent term. All nonanalyticity comes from one static homogeneous mode, which becomes soft at $α_{\rm crit}$, in agreement with the known classical stability threshold. The susceptibility diverges with exponent $γ=1$ and the gap closes with exponent $1/2$. Beyond this angle the mode is tachyonic, and a stable phase would require a stabilizing interaction. In the flat-space limit the physical coupling scales to zero at fixed energy, so the Robin dependence survives only in the soft-frequency sector, which we characterize by a meromorphic Mellin transform in the boost weight. The Robin angle thus gives a control parameter for a Gaussian stability endpoint that can be followed exactly, and raises the analogous question for relaxed boundary conditions in AdS gravity.

</details>


### [124] [Variable-mass sine-Gordon with point defects: integrability, soliton transmission, and quasi-conservation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17211)
*A. R. Aguirre,H. Blas,H. F. Callisaya,M. C. de Oliveira*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了带有点缺陷的可变质量正弦-戈登模型（variable-mass sine-Gordon model, vmSG），通过Lax和Bäcklund-规范构造了I型和II型缺陷矩阵，推导出缝合条件并生成无穷层次守恒荷。将缝合条件用参数α和β形变后导出缺陷反常，数值模拟验证了可积传输/转换区间以及非可积缺陷产生的准守恒行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在非均匀介质中，缺陷会破坏可积性，如何将精确可积性（exact integrability）与缺陷引起的耗散、辐射效应统一在一个框架内，是理解和控制孤子传输的关键问题。作者希望建立描述缺陷对可变质量可积模型影响的完整理论框架。

Method: 采用Lax与Bäcklund-规范（Bäcklund-gauge）方法构造I型和II型缺陷矩阵并推导缝合条件；利用该框架生成体与缺陷贡献的无穷层次守恒荷；通过参数α和β对缝合条件进行形变以引入缺陷反常；采用数值模拟检验可积与非可积情况下的孤子传输、拓扑转换及吸收/发射行为。

Result: 在均匀极限下最低阶守恒荷约化为标准正弦-戈登能量与动量，对非均匀背景则定义了可积性产生的能量和动量型量。可积传输要求αβ=1；当αβ≠1时，宇称中心的扭结-扭结和扭结-反扭结传输的最低阶积分反常为零但高阶不为零。非可积缺陷产生弱辐射尾巴和最低阶准守恒荷，高阶准守恒性未被证实。

Conclusion: 精确缺陷可积性会形变为依赖荷的准守恒（quasi-conservation）行为，这一框架为缺陷控制的孤子在非均匀介质中的传输提供了理论基础，可应用于非均匀约瑟夫森结（Josephson junctions）、磁性与非线性光学系统及分子和DNA模型。

Abstract: We study integrable variable-mass sine-Gordon model (vmSG) with point defects. Using the Lax and Bäcklund-gauge formulations, we construct type-I and type-II defect matrices, derive the corresponding sewing conditions, and generate the bulk and defect contributions to an infinite hierarchy of conserved charges. The lowest members reduce to the standard sine-Gordon energy and momentum in the homogeneous limit, while for inhomogeneous backgrounds they define integrability-generated energy- and momentum-type quantities. Defect compatibility imposes matching conditions on the variable-mass functions across the defect. An analytical transmission factor $z$ characterizes soliton transmission, topological conversion, and absorption/emission processes. We then deform the type-I sewing conditions by parameters $α$ and $β$ and derive the associated defect anomalies. Consistent one-soliton transmission is recovered for $αβ=1$, whereas for $αβ\neq 1$ parity-centered kink-kink and kink-antikink transmissions display vanishing lowest order integrated anomalies but no generic vanishing at higher order. Numerical simulations reproduce the integrable transmission/conversion regimes and show that non-integrable defects generate weak radiative tails and lowest order quasi-conserved charges, while quasi-conservation of the higher order charges remains unestablished. These results elucidate how exact defect integrability deforms into charge-dependent quasi-conservation and establish a framework for defect-controlled soliton transport in inhomogeneous media, with potential applications to nonuniform Josephson junctions, magnetic and nonlinear-optical systems, and effective molecular and DNA models.

</details>


### [125] [Elliptic spin Ruijsenaars-Schneider integrable models from 5d $\mathcal{N}=1$ gauge theories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17837)
*Gleb Arutyunov,Lukas Hardi*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文证明5维$\mathcal{N}=1$项链型箭形规范理论（necklace quiver gauge theories）在$\mathbb{R}^3 \times T^2$上的Coulomb分支（Coulomb branch）与具有动力学非均匀性的椭圆自旋Ruijsenaars-Schneider模型的相空间同构，从而解决了该模型的泊松结构与量子化这一长期问题，并进一步表明所得量子系统正是支配4维$\mathcal{N}=1$ class $\mathcal{S}_k$理论超对称指标的可积系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 椭圆自旋Ruijsenaars-Schneider模型缺乏明确的泊松结构（Poisson structure）和量子化方案，这一问题长期悬而未决，阻碍了对该类可积模型的深入理解及其与规范场论对应关系的建立。

Method: 通过将5维$\mathcal{N}=1$项链型箭形规范理论在$\mathbb{R}^3 \times T^2$上的Coulomb分支与椭圆自旋Ruijsenaars-Schneider模型的相空间建立同构关系，利用规范理论的几何结构赋予该可积模型泊松结构与量子化。

Result: 成功将Coulomb分支与带动力学非均匀性的椭圆自旋Ruijsenaars-Schneider模型的相空间等同起来，由此确定了该模型的泊松结构与量子化；同时证明所得量子系统精确描述4维$\mathcal{N}=1$ class $\mathcal{S}_k$理论的超对称指标（supersymmetric indices）对应的可积系统。

Conclusion: 该同构为椭圆自旋Ruijsenaars-Schneider模型的泊松结构和量子化提供了明确答案，并建立了从高维规范理论到4维可积系统之间的完整对应链，深化了Coulomb分支几何与可积系统之间联系的理解。

Abstract: We show that the Coulomb branches of 5d $\mathcal{N}=1$ necklace quiver gauge theories on $\mathbb{R}^3 \times T^2$ are identified with the phase spaces of elliptic spin Ruijsenaars-Schneider models with dynamical inhomogeneities. This identification resolves the long-standing problem of determining a Poisson structure and a quantization of these models. We further show that the resulting quantum system is the integrable system governing supersymmetric indices of 4d $\mathcal{N}=1$ theories of class $\mathcal{S}_k$.

</details>


### [126] [A compact analytic formula for the one-loop triangle cosmological correlator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17877)
*Johannes Henn,Jiajie Mei,Qinglin Yang*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文推导出了德西特（de Sitter）空间中共形耦合标量场单圈三点关联函数的紧凑解析公式，该公式由六个前导奇性（leading-singularity）因子乘以六个能量变量的纯权二函数构成，包含四十二个双对数函数而非此前的一百二十个，无需辅助正则化器，并通过符号（symbol）与Stieltjes函数性质验证了其结构与可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在德西特空间中计算宇宙学单圈关联函数（one-loop cosmological correlators）通常涉及复杂的解析结构和高阶多对数函数，此前已知的闭式表示包含约一百二十个双对数函数，形式冗长且可能需要辅助正则化器，不利于进一步分析与推广至更高阶关联函数。

Method: 推导了德西特空间中共形耦合标量场的单圈三角形关联函数（triangle correlator）的紧凑解析公式；利用Galois共轭对使每个贡献在物理区域中分别保持实值；通过数值计算验证结果，并从带披辐角积分表示（dressed integral representation）直接导出其符号，或独立地从Landau奇异性与一般一致性条件自举（bootstrap）获得该符号；同时检验该关联函数在适当归一化下对每个平方能量分别满足Stieltjes函数性质，并对全部六个平方能量联合完全单调（completely monotone）。

Result: 所得公式仅含四十二个双对数函数（dilogarithms），较此前表示约减少三分之二；双对数以Galois共轭对形式出现，确保物理区域内各项分别实值；数值验证成功；符号可从两种独立途径获得；关联函数被证明对每个平方能量分别是Stieltjes函数，且在六个平方能量上联合完全单调。

Conclusion: 这些解析结构与单调性性质为构建更高阶点的单圈宇宙学关联函数提供了一条可行路径。

Abstract: We derive a compact analytic formula for the one-loop triangle correlator of conformally coupled scalars in de Sitter space. The result is organised as six leading-singularity prefactors multiplying pure weight-two functions of the six energy variables. It contains forty-two dilogarithms, compared with approximately one hundred and twenty in the previously known closed-form representation, and requires no auxiliary regulator. The dilogarithms occur in Galois-conjugate pairs, making each contribution separately real throughout the physical region. We validate the result numerically. Moreover, we show that its symbol can be derived directly from the dressed integral representation or, independently, bootstrapped from Landau singularities and general consistency conditions. Finally, we show that the correlator (in a suitable normalization) is a Stieltjes function of each squared energy separately and is jointly completely monotone in all six squared energies. These structures suggest a route towards higher-point one-loop cosmological correlators.

</details>


### [127] [Holographic subregion complexity in unbalanced Stückelberg holographic superconductors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17898)
*Yu Shi,Chikun Ding,Yuebing Zhou,Qiyuan Pan,Jiliang Jing*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文在亚区域复杂度-体积猜想（subregion complexity-volume conjecture）框架下，以数值方法比较了不平衡Stückelberg全息超导体中条带（strip）几何的全息亚区域复杂度（HSC）与全息纠缠熵（HEE）。研究发现两者都能反映相变信号，但稳健性差异显著：HEE的定性特征不依赖于条带宽度，而HSC强烈依赖宽度，甚至出现与HEE相反的温度趋势和分支次序反转，在交叉区域无法单独可靠判断相变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全息对应关系中，熵和复杂度都是描述量子态的关键可观测量，但它们在相变中的诊断能力可能不同。作者希望检验在全息超导体相变中，HSC与HEE作为相变探针的相对可靠性与尺度依赖性，从而深化对两者物理意义差异的理解。

Method: 在不平衡Stückelberg全息超导体模型中选取条带子系统，在亚区域复杂度-体积猜想假设下，采用数值方法分别计算全息亚区域复杂度（HSC）和全息纠缠熵（HEE）。通过变化Stückelberg参数γ来扫描相变，并针对不同条带宽度ℓ系统比较两个观测量的温度依赖行为。

Result: 改变Stückelberg参数γ可诱导二级和一级两种相变，HSC与HEE均能检测到这些转变，但稳健性显著不同。HEE的定性特征对所有条带宽度保持不变，且超导相中HEE的有限部分始终小于正常相。HSC则强烈依赖于宽度ℓ：在小ℓ下其温度趋势与HEE相反，在大ℓ下才一致；超导与正常相的HSC分支相对次序发生反转，形成两者几乎重合的交叉区域。

Conclusion: HEE是更稳健的相变诊断量，而HSC是强尺度依赖的探针，其解释依赖于子系统的大小。在交叉区域，仅凭HSC无法可靠判断相变的发生或级数，必须借助自由能（grand potential）来选取物理分支。

Abstract: Within the subregion complexity-volume conjecture, we numerically compare holographic subregion complexity (HSC) and holographic entanglement entropy (HEE) for a strip in unbalanced Stückelberg holographic superconductors. Varying the Stückelberg parameter $γ$ yields both second- and first-order transitions. Both observables signal these transitions, but with markedly different robustness. The qualitative HEE signatures persist across strip widths, and the finite part of HEE remains smaller in the superconducting phase than in the normal phase. The HSC is instead strongly width dependent: its temperature trend is opposite to that of HEE at small $\ell$ and agrees with it at large $\ell$. Consequently, the superconducting and normal HSC branches reverse their relative ordering, creating a crossover region where they nearly coincide. There, HSC alone cannot reliably determine the occurrence or order of the transition, and the physical branch must be selected from the grand potential. Thus, HEE provides a more robust diagnostic, whereas HSC is a scale-dependent probe whose interpretation depends explicitly on the subsystem size.

</details>


### [128] [Gravity and Matter from Soldered Chiral Superconnections](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17900)
*P. D. Alvarez,J. T. Hartwig,A. Sharma*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文提出了四维手性引力（chiral gravity）的重构方案：利用手性规范联络（chiral gauge connection）和克利福德值余标架（Clifford-valued coframe）构建规范协变微分形式，通过手性作用的合适组合再现带宇宙学项的Einstein–Cartan–Holst引力。手性超联络不变量无法使费米子传播，需引入旋量BF型项提供旋量传播并约化为含挠率耦合的标准一阶Weyl动力学作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者试图以手性规范联络和克利福德值余标架为基本变量，重新表述四维手性引力，从而为规范协变微分形式的构造提供系统化框架，并澄清费米子在原超联络不变量框架下的动力学来源问题。

Method: 利用由 $e^a\gamma_a$ 生成的外代数与规范曲率 $F$ 构造规范协变微分形式；对手性作用进行适当组合以复现含宇宙学项的 Einstein–Cartan–Holst 引力；引入旋量 BF 型项（spinorial BF-type term）以提供旋量传播，并通过物质ansatz将其约化到标准一阶 Weyl 动力学作用（含挠率耦合）。

Result: 原超联络不变量仅产生代数型费米方程，使费米子一形式无法传播，而是产生两个手性之间的类质量混合；旋量BF型项在物质ansatz下约化得到包括挠率耦合在内的标准一阶Weyl动力学项。

Conclusion: 该文还综述了物质ansatz的对称性与变分性质，包括其Lorentz等变性、与剩余超对称性（residual supersymmetry）及旋量（twistor）的关系，以及约化场方程与无约束场方程之间的联系。

Abstract: We present a reformulation of four-dimensional chiral gravity in terms of a chiral gauge connection and a Clifford-valued coframe. The exterior algebra generated by $e^a γ_a$, together with the gauge curvature $F$, provides a natural and systematic construction of gauge-covariant differential forms. We show that suitable combinations of the chiral actions reproduce Einstein--Cartan--Holst gravity with a cosmological term. The original superconnection invariants yield only algebraic fermionic equations, so the fermionic one-forms do not propagate but instead generate a mass-like mixing between the two chiralities. Spinor propagation is supplied by a spinorial BF-type term, which under the matter ansatz reduces to the standard first-order Weyl kinetic action, including the torsion coupling. We also review the symmetry and variational properties of the matter ansatz, including its Lorentz equivariance, its relation to residual supersymmetry and twistor spinors, and the connection between the reduced and unrestricted field equations.

</details>


### [129] [Gravitational Compton scattering at the fifth post-Minkowskian order](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17946)
*Giacomo Brunello,Mario Meo,Sid Smith*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文在世界线量子场论（Worldline Quantum Field Theory）框架下，计算了引力的经典康普顿振幅（classical gravitational Compton amplitude）直至第五后闵可夫斯基阶（fifth post-Minkowskian order, O(G^5)），通过匹配黑洞微扰理论，确认了史瓦西黑洞静态潮汐威尔逊系数（static tidal Wilson coefficients）为零，从而验证了其静态形变数（static Love numbers）的消失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在更高后闵可夫斯基阶（post-Minkowskian order）下，点粒子描述必须补充静态潮汐算符，因此需要系统地计算康普顿振幅并确定相应的潮汐系数。同时，验证史瓦西黑洞静态形变数（Love numbers）为零是该研究的关键动机。

Method: 在世界线量子场论框架中，计算通过第五后闵可夫斯基阶（O(G^5)）的经典引力康普顿振幅。在该阶数下引入前导静态潮汐算符（leading static tidal operators），去除低阶迭代贡献后提取对应的N矩阵元（N-matrix element），并将其与黑洞微扰理论（black-hole perturbation theory）进行匹配。

Result: 匹配结果显示静态潮汐威尔逊系数（static tidal Wilson coefficients）被固定为零，为史瓦西黑洞静态形变数（Love numbers）的消失提供了确认。

Conclusion: 研究表明，在世界线量子场论框架下通过第五后闵可夫斯基阶的振幅匹配，可以一致地确认史瓦西黑洞的静态形变数为零，这为黑洞潮汐性质的理解提供了新的理论验证。

Abstract: Working in the Worldline Quantum Field Theory framework, we obtain the classical gravitational Compton amplitude through the fifth post-Minkowskian order, $\mathcal{O}(G^5)$. At this order, the point-particle description must be supplemented by the leading static tidal operators. After removing lower-order iterations, we extract the corresponding $N$-matrix element and match it to black-hole perturbation theory. The matching fixes the static tidal Wilson coefficients to zero, providing confirmation of the vanishing static Love numbers of a Schwarzschild black hole.

</details>


### [130] [Analytic Continuation of Conformal Integrals in Momentum Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18073)
*Jonathan Gräfe,Prashanth Raman,Denis Werth*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文利用括号法（method of brackets），为动量空间中构象Ward恒等式所得的多重-K积分（multiple-K integrals）构造了在整个物理运动学区域收敛的级数表示。对三重-K积分（triple-K integrals），给出了一个简洁的两分支级数，在整个物理区域和任意标度维度下均收敛；并将此构造推广至一般的多重-K积分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多重-K积分是构象关联函数的基本构建模块，属于广义超几何函数类，但其现有级数表示均无法在整个由动量守恒所限定的物理运动学区域内收敛，需要构造恰好适配该物理区域的级数表示。

Method: 采用括号法将定积分求值转化为求解线性代数方程组，从而推导出多重-K积分的各种Lauricella型级数表示。对三重-K积分引入新的运动学变量，构建了在全部物理区域和任意标度维度下都收敛的迭代级数表示。

Result: 对于三重-K积分，找到了一个紧凑的两分支级数，其在整个物理区域和任意标度维度下均收敛，解决了传统的Appell F4级数仅在三角形不等式区域之外收敛的问题。该构造进一步推广到一般的多重-K积分，通过引入新的运动学变量构建了在全部物理运动学构型下都收敛的迭代级数表示。

Conclusion: 这项工作为多重-K积分提供了在整个物理运动学区域内收敛的级数表示，克服了传统级数表示的收敛性缺陷，为构象关联函数的计算提供了更完善的数学工具。

Abstract: Conformal symmetry strongly constrains correlation functions. In momentum space, the conformal Ward identities are solved for three-point functions by integrals of a product of three Bessel functions ("triple-$K$ integrals"); more generally, integrals of this type ("multiple-$K$ integrals"), serve as the building blocks of a wide class of higher-point conformal correlators. These integrals belong to the class of generalised hypergeometric functions, and while their series representations are known in principle, none converges throughout the entire physical region of kinematic space selected by momentum conservation. In this work, we construct series representations adapted to exactly this physical domain. Using the method of brackets, which turns the evaluation of definite integrals into solving a linear system of algebraic equations, we derive various Lauricella-type series representations for multiple-$K$ integrals. For triple-$K$ integrals, conventionally expressed in terms of the Appell $F_4$ function whose series converges only outside the triangle-inequality region, we find instead a compact, two-branch series that converges throughout the entire physical region and for arbitrary scaling dimensions. We then extend this construction to general multiple-$K$ integrals: by introducing a new set of kinematic variables, we build an iterative series representation that converges for all physical kinematic configurations.

</details>


### [131] [Holographic RG flows and wormholes from sinusoidal scalars](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06465)
*Pompey Leung*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究在两个渐近反德西特（anti-de Sitter, AdS）边界上施加相同正弦标量源所诱导的半经典几何。研究发现超过某个阈值后会出现大、小虫洞（wormhole）解，且大虫洞始终是两边界引力路径积分中的主导鞍点，不存在虫洞次主导的参数区域。这导致系综解释下相对平均值存在指数级大的涨落，与以往非均匀物质源虫洞例子不同。将体几何解释为全息重整化群（renormalization group, RG）流动时，正弦调制产生多值β函数，表明非平庸的RG流行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该研究旨在探索由相同边界正弦源诱导的虫洞解在引力路径积分中的主导性质，并对比以往非均匀物质源虫洞在参数空间中的次主导区域，从而理解引力路径积分系综解释下系综涨落行为的差异。

Method: 作者将相同正弦标量源同时施加在两个渐近AdS边界上，通过改变源的强度来扫描解空间，并确定虫洞解出现后的主导鞍点；同时将体几何重新解释为全息RG流，通过β函数分析流的结构。

Result: 发现在源强度超过某阈值后，大、小虫洞解均出现；在该阈值以上，大虫洞始终是两边界引力路径积分中的主导鞍点。在系综解释下，虫洞一旦存在，系综就具有相对于平均值的指数级大涨落，这与先前非均匀物质源虫洞例子中系综集中分布形成鲜明对比。此外，正弦标量源调制导致全息β函数多值，其中连通几何对应流向红外（infrared）有能隙相的流动，而非连通几何对应在红外返回起始不动点的流动。

Conclusion: 该工作表明，由相同边界正弦源产生的虫洞与之前研究的不均匀物质源虫洞在参数空间中的主导性质有本质不同，这深刻影响引力路径积分的系综解释。RG流视角进一步揭示几何结构（连通与否）与红外相变之间的对应关系，展示了正弦调制源导致的多值β函数这一非平庸现象。

Abstract: We study semiclassical geometries induced by turning on identical sinusoidal scalar sources on two asymptotic anti-de Sitter (AdS) boundaries. Varying the source strength, we find that large and small wormhole solutions appear beyond a certain threshold. Above this threshold, the large wormhole is always the dominant saddle in the two-boundary gravitational path integral; there is no regime where wormholes are subdominant. Under the ensemble interpretation of the gravitational path integral, this implies that once wormhole solutions exist, the putative ensemble has exponentially large fluctuations relative to the mean. This is unlike previously studied examples of wormholes sourced by inhomogeneous matter which have a region in parameter space where wormholes are subdominant, and therefore the ensemble is sharply concentrated around the mean. Interpreting the bulk geometries as holographic renormalization group (RG) flows, we also find that the sinusoidal modulation of scalar boundary sources results in effective holographic $β$-functions which are multivalued, indicating exotic RG flows. In particular, disconnected geometries correspond to flows that return to the starting fixed point in the infrared, while wormholes describe flows to a gapped phase in the infrared.

</details>


### [132] [Composite worldline instantons and the nonperturbative particle decay in constant external electric and magnetic fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12083)
*Alexander Gorsky,Ivan Poluboyarinov*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文验证了复合世界线瞬子（composite worldline instanton）方法在描述带电粒子于恒定电场和磁场中非微扰衰变时的有效性，并指出该方法的瞬子结果在不同极限下与自能虚部法和波函数重叠法所得结果一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对带电粒子在常电磁场中的非微扰衰变问题，作者希望验证复合世界线瞬子方法的有效性。该验证具有重要意义，因为此方法有望用于估算质子在外加电磁场中的衰变率。

Method: 作者采用复合世界线瞬子方法计算衰变振幅，并将其结果与两种独立方法——外场中自能（self-energy）的虚部以及波函数重叠（overlap of wave functions）——在不同极限下进行对比。

Result: 研究表明，在指数级主导近似（leading exponential approximation）下，复合世界线瞬子方法在各类极限中得到的结果与外场自能虚部法及波函数重叠法的结果相符合。

Conclusion: 该方法适用于估算质子在外电场和外磁场中的衰变率（指数级主导近似下）。此外，作者简要提及了末态粒子纠缠效应对衰变过程的影响。

Abstract: In this paper, we discuss the validity of the composite worldline instanton approach for the nonperturbative decay of charged particles in constant electric and magnetic fields. It is shown that the instanton results in the leading exponential approximation in the different limits agree with the ones obtained by the imaginary part of the self-energy in the external field and by overlap of the wave functions. We comment on the possible application of our results for the estimation of the decay rate of the proton in the external electric and magnetic field in the leading exponential approximation. The effects of entanglement of the particles in the final state are briefly mentioned.

</details>
