### [133] [Demonstration of a scalable all-solid-state refrigerator exploiting diffusion geometries and limiting interfacial conductances at temperatures below 1](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17125)
*Northrop Grumman Microelectronics Design,Applications Team,:,Robert M. Young,Zachary Stegen,John X. Przybysz,Edward R. Engbrecht,Aaron A. Hathaway,Justin C. Hackley,Kirby B. Myers,Christian C. Thorpe,Aurelius L. Graninger,Robert Miller,Diego A. Morales,Roberto D. Carcamo,Glen Walters,Jeric P. Sarad,Nicholas F. Pleim,Seth Whitsitt,Joshua T. Shipman,Anil Erol,Melissa G. Loving,Evan Donohue,Corey A. Kegerreis,Benjamin Dalfort,Moe S. Khalil,Christopher Pinion,Randi Jaramillo,John Milinichik,Sandro J. Di Giacomo,Thomas Zodda,Nilesh Tralshawala,Gregory R. Boyd,Jonathan M. Cochran,Katherine A. Maddock,Michael P. De Feo,Aaron A. Pesetski,Marc E. Sherwin*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 本文报道了一种利用正常金属/绝缘体/超导体（Normal-metal/Insulator/Superconductor, NIS）结的固态制冷器件，通过优化准粒子扩散和界面工程，首次实现了用NIS结将整块硅片冷却至1 K以下（从120 mK浴温降至70 mK有效声子温度）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以往NIS结固态制冷器虽能有效冷却电子，但对声子（phonon）的冷却能力有限，这是一项关键瓶颈。作者认识到可通过（1）利用电场无关超导体中浓度梯度驱动的准粒子扩散流，以及（2）工程化超导体-捕获阱界面以抑制反向热泄漏，从而提升整体制冷性能。

Method: 器件采用钨和钛钨合金作为冷端正常金属，氧化铝作为绝缘层，铝作为超导体，金作为热侧准粒子捕获阱（quasi-particle trap）。冷端NIS部分通过凸块键合（bump bonding）连接至热侧金捕获阱。制冷器由1121对结组成，每对为SINIS单元，全部串联电连接。

Result: 在浴温120 mK条件下，将3.9 mm × 3.9 mm × 0.65 mm硅芯片的有效声子温度降至70 mK；在271 mK排斥温度下可降至174 mK（冷却量为-97 mK）。这是首次用NIS结实现整块硅芯片亚1 K冷却的演示。

Conclusion: 该工作证明了通过优化准粒子传输机制和界面设计，NIS结制冷器能够实现整块芯片的亚1 K声子冷却，拓展了NIS结在固态制冷领域的应用能力。

Abstract: Solid-state refrigerators using Normal-metal/Insulator/Superconductor (NIS) junctions have previously demonstrated excellent electron cooling but limited ability to cool phonons. The energy gap of the superconductor is used as an energy filter to allow higher than average energy electrons to preferentially tunnel from the normal-metal through the insulator into the superconductor where they travel as quasi-particles. Typically, the heat is moved and work is done to deposit hot quasi-particles into a normal-metal quasi-particle trap for rejection to the next refrigeration stage. Realizing that (1) the quasi-particles flow diffusively, driven by a concentration gradient in the electric field-free superconductor, and (2) that the undesirable backwards leaking of heat from the hot-side trap can be reduced by engineering the geometry and materials at the superconductor-to-trap interface, enhanced cooling can be achieved. Fabrication of the refrigerator was accomplished using a tungsten and titanium-tungsten alloy as the cold-side normal-metal, aluminum oxide as the insulator, aluminum as the superconductor, and gold as the trap, with the cold-side NIS portion being attached to the hot-side gold trap by bump bonding. The refrigerator consisted of 1121 junction pairs, each pair being an SINIS unit, all electrically connected in series. Using this we have measured the effective phonon temperature of a 3.9 mm x 3.9 mm x 0.65 mm silicon chip driven down to 70 mK from a bath temperature of 120 mK, and down to 174 mK from a 271 mK rejection temperature (a cooling of -97 mK). This is the first demonstration of the sub 1 K cooling of an entire silicon chip using NIS junctions.

</details>


### [134] [Unconventional Pressure Evolution of Spin-Density-Wave State in La$_{3}$Ni$_{2}$O$_{7}$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17505)
*Xiaoxiang Zhou,Shiyu Xie,Liangxin Qiao,Hengyuan Zhang,Jun Shu,Rui Liu,Mengwu Huo,Deyuan Hu,Hengjie Liu,Chuansheng Hu,Yilin Wang,Ge He,Zeming Qi,Meng Wang,Dong-Lai Feng,Zengyi Du*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 本研究通过系统电子拉曼（electronic Raman）散射实验，测量了双层镍酸盐 La₃Ni₂O₇ 单晶在高达16.51 GPa静水压下的自旋密度波（SDW）态演变，发现SDW能隙和转变温度随压力增加，但耦合比恒定，SDW峰展宽表明长程有序逐渐减弱。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 压力诱导的双层镍酸盐 La₃Ni₂O₇ 中发现高温超导电性，引发了对该体系中自旋密度波态如何向超导畴演化的疑问，需要通过光谱手段系统研究其压力依赖行为。

Method: 对 La₃Ni₂O₇ 单晶在最高16.51 GPa的静水压力条件下进行系统性电子拉曼散射实验，测量SDW能隙能量、转变温度及拉曼峰宽度随压力的变化。

Result: SDW能隙能量与转变温度T_SDW均随压力整体增大，而无量纲耦合比2Δ_SDW/(k_B*T_SDW)恒定为约7.5，表明SDW态具有稳健的强耦合特征；同时拉曼SDW峰随压力展宽，指示长程SDW序逐渐减弱。

Conclusion: 这些结果揭示了SDW能量尺度增强但SDW态日益失去相干性的反常压力演化，为理解双层镍酸盐中与超导相关的磁关联提供了光谱学约束。

Abstract: The discovery of pressure-induced high temperature superconductivity in the bilayer nickelate La$_{3}$Ni$_{2}$O$_{7}$ has raised the question of how its spin-density-wave (SDW) state evolves toward the superconducting regime. Here, we report a systematic electronic Raman study of La$_{3}$Ni$_{2}$O$_{7}$ single crystals under hydrostatic pressures up to 16.51 GPa. Both the SDW gap energy and the transition temperature $T_{\mathrm{SDW}}$ show an overall increase with pressure, while the dimensionless coupling ratio 2$Δ_{\text{SDW}}/(k_{\text{B}}T_{\text{SDW}})$ remains constant around $\sim7.5$, indicating a robust strong-coupling character of SDW state. At the same time, the Raman SDW peak broadens as pressure is applied, indicating a gradual weakening of long-range SDW order. These results reveal an unusual pressure evolution in which the SDW energy scale is enhanced while the SDW state becomes progressively less coherent, providing spectroscopic constraints on the magnetic correlations relevant to superconductivity in bilayer nickelates.

</details>


### [135] [Bridging ambient- and high-pressure superconductivity in La$_2$LnNi$_2$O$_7$ films](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17745)
*Motoki Osada,Chieko Terakura,Shusaku Imajo,Jean-Baptiste Morée,Akiko Kikkawa,Masamichi Nakajima,Hsiao-Yi Chen,Yusuke Nomura,Koichi Kindo,Ryotaro Arita,Yoshinori Tokura,Atsushi Tsukazaki*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了压缩应变La₂LnNi₂O₇双层镍氧化物薄膜在常压和高压下的超导电性。常压下通过59 T磁场抑制超导后，正常态电阻率趋于T²行为；高压下临界温度从常压的41-42 K提升至16 GPa下的67-73 K，而通过In离子取代引入的晶格压缩则降低Tc。两种情况下Tc均与正常态输运在T²与T线性行为之间的演化相关联。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 双层镍氧化物在高压下发现接近80 K的高临界温度超导电性引发了广泛研究，尽管随后在压缩应变薄膜中发现了常压下超过40 K的超导电性，但常压与高压区域之间的关联仍是一个未解问题。

Method: 作者对压缩应变的La₂LnNi₂O₇薄膜（Ln = 镧系元素）在常压和高压条件下进行了系统的超导电性研究。常压下利用59 T磁场抑制超导以揭示正常态电阻率行为，高压实验则采用立方砧压机（cubic-anvil cell）将压力提升至16 GPa，同时通过Ln取代改变晶格压缩程度。

Result: 常压下正常态电阻率趋于T²行为；高压下Tc从常压的41-42 K增强至67-73 K。相反，通过In取代引起的晶格压缩降低了Tc。在这两种情况下，Tc均与正常态输运从T²行为向T线性行为演化的过程相关。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了晶格结构与双层镍氧化物超导电性之间的相互作用，为理解常压与高压超导区域之间的关系提供了新的见解。

Abstract: The discovery of high critical-temperature $T_{\mathrm{c}}$ superconductivity near 80 K in bilayer nickelates under high pressure has sparked extensive studies. While superconductivity exceeding 40 K was subsequently discovered at ambient pressure in compressively strained films, the relationship between ambient- and high-pressure regimes remains an open question. Here we present a systematic investigation of superconductivity in compressively strained La$_2$LnNi$_2$O$_7$ films (Ln = lanthanides) at ambient and high pressures. The normal-state resistivity at ambient pressure, revealed by suppressing superconductivity with magnetic fields of 59 T, tends toward $T^2$ behaviour. Under high pressure in a cubic-anvil cell, $T_{\mathrm{c}}$ was enhanced from 41-42 K at ambient pressure to 67-73 K at 16 GPa. On the other hand, lattice compression induced by Ln substitution, which may mimic effects of pressure, lowers $T_{\mathrm{c}}$. In both cases, $T_{\mathrm{c}}$ correlates with the evolution of normal-state transport between $T^2$ and $T$-linear behaviour, offering insight into the interplay between lattice structure and superconductivity in bilayer nickelates.

</details>


### [136] [Entropy mapping under uniaxial pressure utilizing the elastocaloric effect](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17777)
*Zhenhai Hu,You-Sheng Li,Aleksei V. Frolov,Fabian Jerzembeck,Manuel Brando,Naoki Kikugawa,Dmitry A. Sokolov,Hilary M. L. Noad,Andrew P. Mackenzie,Michael Nicklas,Andreas W. Rost*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 本文开发了一套交流弹性热效应（a.c. elastocaloric effect）测量的定量分析框架，能够重构绝对熵和比热，并应用于超导体Sr₂RuO₄，获得了跨越整个相图的绝对熵图谱，证实了超导熵猝灭在临界应变处最强，并指出此前对Δc/(γT)增强的估计被高估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单轴压力（uniaxial pressure）是调控量子材料的关键参数，但传统的热力学探测手段（如比热测量）难以在应变装置的受限几何结构中实现，因此需要一种能在这类装置中定量测量热力学量的新方法。

Method: 开发了交流弹性热效应测量的定量分析框架，通过结合低频强耦合区（strong coupling regime）测量与高频准绝热区（quasi-adiabatic regime）的高信噪比测量，实现绝对熵乃至比热的绝对精度重构。

Result: 将该方法应用于关联超导体Sr₂RuO₄，获得了跨越相图的绝对熵图谱，包括深入超导态的相变区域；由此进一步推导出绝对比热。结果显示：超导体中熵的猝灭在临界应变处最强，与超导能隙在范霍夫奇点（Van Hove singularity, VHs）处最大的结论一致；同时发现Δc/(γT)在范霍夫应变处虽有所增加，但增幅远弱于此前间接量热实验所推断的结果。

Conclusion: 该定量分析框架为复杂相图中绝对熵与比热的重构提供了可行路径，弥补了传统热力学探测在应变装置中的空白，并对超导比热跳变的定量理解提供了更精确的修正。

Abstract: Uniaxial pressure is a powerful tuning parameter for quantum materials, but conventional thermodynamic probes such as specific heat are difficult to realize in the constrained geometries of strain apparatus. We develop a quantitative analysis framework for a.c. elastocaloric effect measurements that enable the reconstruction of the absolute entropy and hence specific heat across complex phase diagrams. The absolute accuracy is achieved by combining measurements in the strong coupling regime at low frequencies with high signal-to-noise measurements in the quasi-adiabatic regime at high frequencies. Applying the approach to the correlated superconductor Sr$_2$RuO$_4$, we obtain an absolute entropy map across the phase diagram including across phase transitions deep into the superconducting state. We demonstrate that from such data one can derive the absolute specific heat which is currently not possible through other approaches. This data reinforces the finding that the quenching of entropy within the superconductor Sr$_2$RuO$_4$ is strongest at the critical strain consistent with the superconducting gap being maximized at the Van Hove singularity (VHs). Furthermore, we demonstrate that, although $Δc /(γT) $ does increase at the VH strain, this increase is much weaker than previously inferred from more indirect caloric experiments.

</details>

### [137] [Spectral design of confining optical structures by supersymmetry techniques](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17346)
*Samantha Cervantes-Neri,Zulema Gress-Mendoza,Daniel O-Campa,Erik Díaz-Bautista,Sara Cruz y Cruz*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 本研究将超对称变换（supersymmetric transformations）应用于具有椭圆折射率分布的光学介质中，通过一阶和二阶变换构造了可承载一个或两个附属导模的多参数渐变折射率模式，并可推广至任意阶以调控光束的光谱和传播特性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在光学领域，利用超对称量子力学思想构建新型折射率分布，以调控光波的导模数目、光谱和空间分布，为光束操控提供更灵活的手段。

Method: 对横向两个坐标均为二次分布（elliptic profile）的折射率介质应用超对称变换，实施一阶（first-order）和二阶（second-order）变换，构造多参数渐变折射率分布，并可连续进行至任意阶。

Result: 所构造的折射率分布可承载一个或两个附属导模（subsidiary guided modes），这些导模具有广泛可调的光谱特性以及横向强度和相位分布。

Conclusion: 超对称变换可无缝延伸至任意阶，从而为控制光束的频谱和传播性质提供了途径。

Abstract: In this work, we apply supersymmetric transformations to an optical medium with quadratic index distributions on both transversal coordinates defining an elliptic profile. In particular, we implement first- and second-order transformations to construct new multi-parametric gradient index patterns that may host one or two subsidiary guided modes with a wide range of spectral properties and transversal intensity and phase distributions. These transformations may be continued up to an arbitrary order enabling the possibility to control the spectral and propagation properties of light beams.

</details>

### [138] [Hydrodynamization in 1D Bose gases at nonzero temperature](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16980)
*Jeff Leiberton,Marcos Rigol*

Main category: cond-mat.quant-gas

TL;DR: 本文研究了非零温度对一维硬核玻色子气体中流体动力学化（hydrodynamization）的影响，发现初始温度升高会缩短流体动力学化振荡的阻尼时间和预热化（prethermalization）时间，而流体动力学化相干时间保持不变，其原因归结为初始快度（rapidity）分布的展宽。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 流体动力学化在相对论重离子碰撞和超冷一维玻色气体中被推测为量子系统在高能淬火后的普适动力学现象，但此前的冷原子理论研究仅局限于从基态出发的淬火，尚无工作考察非零温度对该过程的影响。

Method: 使用均匀一维硬核玻色子（hard-core bosons）气体模型，在Bragg脉冲淬火后系统考察初始温度对流体动力学化相干时间、振荡阻尼时间和预热化时间的影响。

Result: 研究发现流体动力学化相干时间不随温度变化，而升高温度会缩短流体动力学化振荡的阻尼时间和预热化时间；作者将这一行为归因于初始快度分布的展宽，并引入了一个基于快度分布范围定义的有限温度去相位时间（dephasing time）。

Conclusion: 非零温度显著影响流体动力学化与预热化的特征时间尺度，初始快度分布展宽是其中的主要机制，新引入的去相位时间有助于刻画这一温度依赖行为。

Abstract: Hydrodynamization refers to the remarkably rapid process in relativistic heavy-ion collisions by which hydrodynamic descriptions become applicable. Following the observation of analogous behavior in ultracold one-dimensional (1D) Bose gases, hydrodynamization has been conjectured to be a universal dynamical phenomenon in quantum systems following high-energy quenches. Theoretical studies in this cold-atom setting have so far been restricted to quenches from ground states. Here we study how nonzero temperatures affect hydrodynamization. Specifically, using a homogeneous 1D gas of hard-core bosons, we explore how the initial temperature affects the timescales associated with hydrodynamization and prethermalization following a Bragg-pulse quench. We find that while the hydrodynamization coherence time remains unchanged, increasing temperature shortens both the damping time of the hydrodynamization oscillations and the prethermalization time. We argue that this is mainly the result of the broadening of the initial rapidity distribution, and introduce a nonzero-temperature dephasing time defined in terms of the extent of the rapidity distribution.

</details>


### [139] [Hidden Ergodic Relaxation in the Quench Dynamics of a Bichromatic Mott Lattice](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17076)
*Áttis V. M. Marino,Rhombik Roy,M. A. Caracanhas,N. D. Chavda,Antônio M. S. Macêdo,V. S. Bagnato,Robin P Sagar,B. Chakrabarti*

Main category: cond-mat.quant-gas

TL;DR: 本文研究有限双色莫特晶格（bichromatic Mott lattice）中强相互作用玻色子在外加次级晶格振幅的突然淬火（quench）后的非平衡动力学。结果显示系数熵与二阶Rényi熵显著增长至高斯正交系综（GOE）预测值，而实验可观测量几乎不变，揭示了被持久的局域莫特有序所掩盖的隐藏遍历弛豫机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解强相互作用多体系统在淬火后的非平衡弛豫动力学，尤其是量子遍历性（quantum ergodicity）如何在与局域有序共存的条件下涌现，是量子模拟与热化研究中的核心问题。

Method: 在有限双色莫特晶格中通过突然改变次级晶格振幅诱导强淬火，采用多组态表示（multiconfigurational representation）计算系数熵（coefficient entropies）、二阶Rényi熵以及动量分布、碎片化（fragmentation）和Glauber关联函数，并与随机矩阵理论的GOE预测及嵌入式随机矩阵模型进行对比。

Result: 系数熵和二阶Rényi熵随演化显著增长并趋近GOE预测值；可观测的动量分布、碎片化及Glauber关联函数几乎不变；在强淬火下各熵量坍塌到一条与扰动强度基本无关的共同弛豫轨迹。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了一种在持续局域莫特有序之下隐藏的遍历弛豫过程（hidden ergodic relaxation），并提出有效嵌入式随机矩阵模型可定性描述从受限到广泛希尔伯特空间扩展的交叉行为，为观测到的弛豫动力学提供了解释框架。

Abstract: We investigate the nonequilibrium dynamics of strongly interacting bosons in a finite bichromatic Mott lattice following a sudden quench of the secondary lattice amplitude. The coefficient entropies and second Rényi entropy exhibit pronounced growth toward their Gaussian Orthogonal Ensemble (GOE) predictions from random-matrix theory, consistent with GOE-like statistical spreading in the employed multiconfigurational representation. In striking contrast, experimentally accessible observables, including the momentum distribution, fragmentation, and Glauber correlation functions, remain nearly unchanged throughout the evolution. For the sampled strong quenches, the coefficient entropies, second Rényi entropy, and the $N$-body coefficient spreading collapse onto a common relaxation trajectory that becomes largely independent of the perturbation strength. Our results reveal an emergent hidden ergodic relaxation beneath the persistent local Mott-like order. An effective embedded random-matrix model captures the qualitative crossover from restricted to extensive Hilbert-space spreading, providing an interpretive framework for the observed relaxation dynamics.

</details>


### [140] [Universal quantum corrections of two-body correlation in a weakly interspecies interacting binary Bose mixture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17473)
*Rui-Yan Chen,Zhaoxin Liang,Gao Xianlong*

Main category: cond-mat.quant-gas

TL;DR: 本文利用Cornwall-Jackiw-Tomboulis双粒子不可约有效作用量形式，研究了零温度二元玻色混合物中的普适量子修正。在弱种间耦合条件下，通过鞍点处理、两圈展开和无能隙Hartree-Fock修正，将双组分问题简化为两个可解析求解的单组分理论，导出了基态能量密度、量子耗尽和化学势的低密度展开式，并发现其与单组分结果存在简洁映射。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 二元玻色混合物中的普适量子修正是理解超冷原子混合体系的关键问题，但多组分系统的量子修正通常比单组分情况复杂得多，难以直接求解。作者试图寻找一种方法，将双组分问题的量子修正映射到已知的单组分结果，从而简化计算并揭示其普适结构。

Method: 采用Cornwall-Jackiw-Tomboulis双粒子不可约（two-particle-irreducible, 2PI）有效作用量形式；在弱种间耦合下，结合Hubbard-Stratonovich变换的鞍点处理与两圈展开，并施加无能隙的Hartree-Fock修正以保持Goldstone定理；将耦合的双组分问题约化为两个可解析求解的单组分理论。

Result: 导出了基态能量密度作为气体参数的低密度展开式，以及量子耗尽（quantum depletion）和化学势。结果呈现对单组分情形的简洁映射：混合物中每种组分的普适量子修正等价于在有效散射长度$a_{\sigma\sigma}-a_{12}$处评估单组分序列。单圈阶可复现Petrov状态方程，两圈阶给出超越Lee-Huang-Yang的修正。质量不平衡通过精确重标度因子$(1+m_1/m_2)/2$进入能量密度。

Conclusion: 该研究建立了一个解析框架，将零温度二元玻色混合物的普适量子修正归结为单组分结果的简单映射，并在弱耦合极限下统一描述了单圈和两圈的超越平均场修正，同时阐明了质量不平衡对体系的影响。

Abstract: We investigate universal quantum corrections to two-body correlations in a zero-temperature binary Bose mixture using the Cornwall-Jackiw-Tomboulis two-particle-irreducible effective action formalism. In the weak interspecies-coupling regime, a saddle-point treatment based on Hubbard-Stratonovich transformations can be combined with a two-loop expansion and a gapless Hartree-Fock correction, thereby preserving the Goldstone theorem and reducing the coupled two-component problem to two analytically solvable single-component theories. Within this framework, we derive the ground-state energy density as a low-density expansion in the gas parameter, together with the quantum depletion and chemical potentials. The results exhibit a simple mapping to the single-component case: the universal quantum corrections of the mixture are obtained by evaluating the known single-component series at an effective scattering length $a_{σσ}-a_{12}$ for each species, where $a_{σσ}$ and $a_{12}$ are the intra- and interspecies $s$-wave scattering lengths. This reproduces Petrov's equation of state at one-loop order in the weak-coupling limit and yields beyond-Lee-Huang-Yang corrections at two-loop order. We also analyze the role of mass imbalance, which enters the energy density through the exact rescaling factor $(1+m_{1}/m_{2})/2$.

</details>


### [141] [KPZ scaling in one-dimensional arrays of photon condensates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17602)
*Michiel Yzewyn,Michiel Wouters*

Main category: cond-mat.quant-gas

TL;DR: 本文通过基于经典场模型（classical field model）的模拟，揭示了染料填充微腔中光子玻色-爱因斯坦凝聚体（Bose-Einstein condensate of photons）由于其驱动耗散（driven-dissipative）本质，其一阶相干性（first-order coherence）表现出Kardar-Parisi-Zhang（KPZ）标度行为，并在大密度涨落区域依然存在。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 染料填充微腔中的光子玻色-爱因斯坦凝聚体虽然具备理想玻色气体在热平衡下的许多性质，但由于光子有限寿命，该系统实为驱动耗散体系，损耗由泵浦激光连续驱动补偿。作者旨在探究这种非平衡本质对其相干性质标度行为的影响。

Method: 采用基于经典场模型的数值模拟方法，研究驱动耗散型光子凝聚体的一阶相干性的标度行为；并通过改变激发态占据和光谱滤波（spectral filtering）来考察标度行为的修正与恢复。

Result: 模拟发现该系统一阶相干性表现出Kardar-Parisi-Zhang（KPZ）标度现象，这与相关的激子-偏振子（exciton-polariton）凝聚体中已知的现象一致；且该KPZ标度在密度涨落大的区域仍会出现。此外，激发态的占据会修饰该标度行为，而通过光谱滤波可以恢复普适标度行为。

Conclusion: 驱动耗散本质使光子凝聚体的一阶相干性呈现KPZ普适标度，即使在大密度涨落情形下也不例外；光谱滤波可作为恢复普适标度行为的有效手段。

Abstract: Bose-Einstein condensates of photons in dye filled microcavities are known to feature many properties of the ideal bose gas at thermal equilibrium. Nevertheless, the finite photon life time renders them driven dissipative systems where losses are balanced by continuous driving by a pump laser. We show here with simulations based on a classical field model that their driven-dissipative nature causes their first order coherence to feature Kardar-Parisi-Zhang (KPZ) scaling, a phenomenon well known in the related condensates of exciton-polaritons. Remarkably, we find that the KPZ scaling even occurs in a regime with large density fluctuations. We discuss the modification of the scaling by the occupation of excited states and how to recover the universal scaling behavior by spectral filtering.

</details>


### [142] [Dispersive Shock Waves in a 1D Quantum Liquid](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17668)
*Philipp Schüttelkopf,Mohammadamin Tajik,Federica Cataldini,Si-Cong Ji,Igor Mazets,Sebastian Erne,Nataliia Bazhan,Mojtaba Alyannezhadi,Mostafa Alyannezhadi,Jörg Schmiedmayer,Frederik Møller*

Main category: cond-mat.quant-gas

TL;DR: 本文在一维量子液体（quantum liquid）中实现移动边界条件，利用可编程光势阱（programmable optical potentials）以最高三倍声速压缩超冷玻色气体（ultra-cold Bose gas），观测到激波前沿与尾部速度及线性增长的激波宽度，验证了色散激波（dispersive shock waves）的渐近预测，并在有限温度下与数值模拟定量吻合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解量子流体中非线性波破碎（nonlinear wave breaking）及其被色散效应（dispersion）规整化的基本物理机制。

Method: 在原子芯片（atomchip）上利用可编程光势阱引入移动边界条件，以最高三倍声速可调速度压缩弱相互作用的超冷玻色气体，并通过准原位（quasi-in-situ）密度分布测量提取激波边缘动力学；结果与使用Whitham方法（Whitham's method）的渐近预测以及有限温度非多项式薛定谔方程（finite temperature non-polynomial Schrödinger equation）模拟进行定量对比。

Result: 实验同时分辨了激波前沿与尾部两种速度，观察到激波宽度随时间线性增大，这与色散激波的特征行为一致，并与Whitham方法渐近预测符合；同时，考虑成像过程与活塞势有限高度后，有限温度模拟与实验结果定量吻合。

Conclusion: 该工作对一维量子流体中的色散激波动力学进行了受控、定量的实验检验，并表明即使微观动力学偏离严格的完全可积（integrable）一维区域，粗粒度色散激波现象学仍然保持鲁棒。

Abstract: We implement a moving boundary condition in a 1D quantum liquid to study nonlinear wave breaking and its regularization by dispersion. Programmable optical potentials allow us to compress a weakly interacting ultra-cold Bose gas trapped on an atomchip at tunable speeds of up to three times the speed of sound and we subsequently measure the quasi-in-situ density distribution to extract the shock wave edge dynamics. We resolve both leading and trailing edge velocities and observe a shock wave width that increases linearly in time, which are distinguishing features of dispersive shock waves, consistent with asymptotic predictions using Whitham's method. Quantitative agreement is found with finite temperature non-polynomial Schrödinger equation simulations, taking into account the imaging process and the finite height of the piston potential. Our results constitute a controlled, quantitative test of dispersive shock dynamics in a 1D quantum fluid and demonstrate that the coarse-grained dispersive-shock phenomenology remains robust even as the microscopic dynamics depart from the strictly integrable 1D regime.

</details>
